## Ward W88: Shadows follow the liquid

**North star:** none. W88 adds visual fidelity: the author's `box-shadow` follows the liquid. The 8 acceptance steps and the slice matrix are unchanged, and the acceptance scene has no `box-shadow`.

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: superpowers:subagent-driven-development or superpowers:executing-plans. WDD / GS-TDD as every ward. **This plan is written in two parts, as W86's.**
> - **Part 1 (this file now) is W88.0:** the epic file, the ward file, the baseline, a time-boxed spike, and the direction gate. The spike answers what Canvas2D's per-element shadow mask costs (Q1), what the WebGPU blur and its radius buckets cost and look like (Q2), and how visible the handoff from the renderer's shadow to the browser's is (Q3).
> - **Part 2 (W88.1 red … gold)** is written after the gate, against the approved decisions, and presented to Dennis before red.

**Goal:** a button with `box-shadow: 0 0 24px cyan` keeps its glow when liquiddom observes it. A splash stretches the glow with the liquid, a drag carries it, and two merged liquids share one shadow. At rest the browser's own shadow is pixel-exact.

**Spec:** [`2026-10-24-liquiddom-shadows-design.md`](../../specs/2026-10-24-liquiddom-shadows-design.md) (approved by Dennis 2026-10-25; binding for this ward).

## Survey (2026-10-25, at `38165db`)

**Stylesheet** (`packages/core/ts/src/stylesheet.ts`, 432 lines)
- `:44` is `.liquid-element { background: transparent !important; border-color: transparent !important; box-shadow: none !important; }`. The `box-shadow: none` leaves this rule (spec §2).
- The toggle classes are `TEXT_CLASS`, `HALO_CLASS` (`:27`) and `AWAY_CLASS` (`:33`). They are refcounted through `classHolds(...)` (`:404-405`).

**Toggles**
- `away-toggle.ts` (68 lines) is the pattern for the new toggle: `wantsAway`, `syncAwayClasses`, `clearAwayClasses`, `releaseAwayClass`, a per-instance `held` set, and a sweep for elements no longer observed.
- `halo-toggle.ts` (67 lines) follows the same pattern.
- `liquid-away` is Canvas2D-only. `liquid-shadow-away` applies to both renderers.

**Runtime** (`runtime.ts`, 1144 lines)
- Toggle order after `render()`: `syncText` → `syncAway` → `syncHalo` (`:847-849`).
- `onTransitionEnd` (`:456-461`) queues colours only for `background-color` and `color`, so `box-shadow` must join that list.
- `watchElement` / `unwatchElement` are at `:462-470`.
- The `colourPending` set is re-read in `applyDomChanges()`.

**Snapshot** (`element-registry.ts`, 374 lines)
- `observe()` calls `snapshotColorsWithout(el, ELEMENT_CLASS)` before decorating (`:194`). `refresh(el)` re-reads it (`:256`).
- `InternalRecord` holds `background`, `text`, `rawRadius`.
- `color.ts` (173 lines) has `parseCssColor`, `snapshotColors` (`:128`) and `snapshotColorsWithout` (`:147`), plus the W86 colour probe.

**Paint** (`renderers/frame.ts:13-34`)
- `ElementPaint` has `id`, `background`, `text`, `radiusPx`, `particleCount`, `areaPerParticle`, `spacingPx`, `atlasRect`, `owner`.
- Spec §3 adds `shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[]`.

**Canvas2D** (`renderers/fluid-canvas2d.ts`, 189 lines; `density-grid.ts`, 209 lines)
- `splatMoving` splats **every** moving element into one shared density grid (`:123-153`).
- `drawDensity` does one `putImageData` into an offscreen canvas, then one `drawImage` (`:155-164`).
- `drawResting` draws a `roundRect` per element at `restAlpha` (`:166-189`).
- A per-element mask is therefore new work per shadowed element. Spike Q1 measures that cost.

**WebGPU** (`renderers/webgpu/webgpu-renderer.ts`, 1245 lines; `shaders.ts`, 422 lines)
- Passes, in order: `splat` (`:585`), the text pass while text moves (`:595`), then `screen` (`:602`).
- The screen pass draws the composite, the rest SDF and the text composite.
- T0 is half resolution by default (`t0Scale` 0.5).

**Docs that say shadows are not drawn**
- `site/src/pages/docs/getting-started.md:44` and `site/src/pages/docs/limitations.md:14` (named in spec §7).
- `packages/core/README.md:24` and `:120`. These are not named in the spec; the same correction applies to them.

**Pages with a `box-shadow`**
- `demo/scenes/landing.html:181` (`0 1px 2px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06)`, no baseline).
- The site's playground panel (`site/src/pages/playground/index.astro:92`, not an observed element).
- The acceptance scene has none.

**The gate test** (`packages/core/__tests__/wdd-docs.test.ts`)
- `discoverFluidWards` (`:163-170`) keeps only `epic: "fluid-engine"` wards. A W88 with `epic: "liquid-look"` would escape the North-star, Decisions and PENDING guards.
- Widening that discovery to Epic 16 changes an approved test. It is flagged to Dennis in the gate (D88-9).

**Counts at `38165db`**
- From W87 complete, with only docs changed since: cargo `245 passed; 0 failed; 1 ignored`; vitest `870 passed (870)`, 88 files.
- Playwright `--list`: canvas2d 101 / webgpu 51 / webgpu-hw 99 / perf 6 / dist 2 / site 7 / record 4 / record-webgpu 4.
- Step 3 re-measures these.

**Open PRs and branches:** none open (checked 2026-10-25). The side branches are old soft-body and spike work.

## Part 2

Written after the W88.0 gate (Step 7), against `6c8d0f5` (every `Decision:` line approved 2026-10-25; D88-6 amended to exact radii). Steps 1–6 of W88.0 are done (`b921313`, `171ca5d`, `6c8d0f5`). Revised in fix round 1 after an independent review: a pre-red gate (W88.1 Step 0) now comes before any red test, the author-transition flicker is fixed instead of documented, and a gold perf step is added.

**Architecture:** one new pure module reads the author's shadow (`shadow.ts`: the parser, the outer-layer cap, `snapshotShadow`, and `liquidAway`, the one "the liquid is away" rule that the toggle and both renderers share). The registry snapshots the shadow wherever it snapshots colours, and the paints carry the outer layers (`ElementPaint.shadows`). The renderers draw a shadow for every painted, active element with outer layers whose liquid is away (`renderers/shadow-plan.ts` `ShadowCasters`, shared), before all liquid, knocked out by the casters' own closed coverage, and report what they drew (`Renderer.shadowsDrawn(id)`). After `render()` the runtime's fourth toggle (`shadow-toggle.ts`, the W74/W75/W78 pattern) holds `liquid-shadow-away` exactly on the elements whose shadow the renderer drew this frame (plus inset-only elements in motion), so the browser's shadow and the renderer's swap in the same frame (D88-7) and never both show or both hide. Canvas2D draws per caster a cell-resolution alpha mask with canvas shadows (`renderers/shadow-canvas2d.ts`); WebGPU splats one density per source into channels of a half-resolution density array and resolves, blurs and accumulates per exact radius group (`renderers/webgpu/shadow-passes.ts`, five new WGSL shaders). Rust, the FFI and the public API are untouched.

**Tech stack:** TypeScript (strict), Canvas2D, WebGPU/WGSL, Vitest 4 (jsdom, the fake canvas and fake GPU, both extended), Playwright 1.63.0 (`canvas2d` blocking; the WebGPU twins in `webgpu-hw` on Metal, local only), Astro 7 (the site's glow).

**Decisions (approved 2026-10-25; this part implements them as recorded in `ward-088.md`):**

| Item | What this plan implements |
|---|---|
| D88-1 Hybrid model (saga dec_3e11178b) | `box-shadow: none` leaves `.liquid-element`; `.liquid-shadow-away { box-shadow: none !important }` in the layer and the screen block; `addShadowAwayClass`/`removeShadowAwayClass` (refcounted `classHolds`); `shadow-toggle.ts`; `liquid-shadow-away` joins `OWN_CLASSES`; cleared on unobserve, destroy, a stop, a lost device, a failed rebuild and reduced motion; written with an author box-shadow transition held off (resolution 12) |
| D88-2 Layers (dec_acefbc25) | `outerShadows()` keeps the first 4 outer layers; an inset-only element holds the class while its liquid is away (its inset hides in motion) |
| D88-3 Snapshot (dec_a4fea5a7) | `parseBoxShadow`, `snapshotShadow` (lifts `liquid-shadow-away` with W66's lift, now `withClassLifted` in `color.ts`); at observe, in `refresh()` (W75's own class/style trigger and `refresh(el)`), and a shadow-only re-read on `transitionend` for `box-shadow` (`registry.refreshShadow`, no atlas re-render); `ElementRecord.shadows`, `ElementPaint.shadows` |
| D88-4 Draw order (dec_c32cd7eb) | both renderers draw every shadow before all liquid; parked particles and inactive slots cast none (`ShadowCasters`) |
| D88-5 Canvas2D (dec_a36ea6f4) | per caster a mask at the density cell, one `drawImage` per layer with `shadowColor/Blur/Offset`, the source off the canvas and the offset compensating; spread by 8 shifted copies (dilation, `destination-in` erosion for a negative spread); no ceiling |
| D88-6 WebGPU (dec_8c14c096, amended) | one group per exact radius, the radius rounded to half px first (`groupBlur`, so float noise never splits a group: blur 12.3 and 12.4 draw at 12.5), half resolution, quarter for blur ≥ 32 px, scissored; past 6 distinct radii the log-nearest bucket {4, 8, 16, 32, 64} |
| D88-7 Handoff (dec_90ce2674) | the toggle runs after `render()` and follows `shadowsDrawn`, so the class goes in the frame restAlpha reaches 1; no cross-fade |
| D88-8 Scene, site, docs (dec_30400751) | `demo/scenes/shadows.html` + `shadows.ts` (`window.__shadows`), linked from `demo/index.html`; the hero glow in `site/src/styles/global.css`; `getting-started.md:44`, `limitations.md:14`, `packages/core/README.md:24` and `:120` |
| D88-9 Gate covers Epic 16 (dec_133bcc13) | `wdd-docs.test.ts`: `discoverGatedWards(dir, epics)`, `GATED_WARDS` (fluid-engine + liquid-look) for every gate guard, `FLUID_WARDS` for the Epic 15 table |
| D88-10 Knockout (dec_5af57bbe; to be AMENDED at the pre-red gate, W88.1 Step 0) | each caster's closed own coverage knocks the shadows out (Canvas2D: one `destination-out` image; WebGPU: an r8unorm knockout pass, the composite × (1 − k)); design resolution 3 |
| D88-11 Pass structure (dec_0dba6795) | one density draw per source (element and distinct spread) without an offset, four sources per `rgba16float` layer; the offset in the resolve; one resolve pass per radius group (instanced quads); the widened, mass-scaled kernel for spread; every pass scissored |
| D88-12 Spread fringe (dec_30e72f80) | a Canvas2D spread layer with blur 0 draws with `shadowBlur` = one density cell |
| D88-13 Composite OVER (dec_793867f6) | `composite` gets `OVER_BLEND`; the shadow composite is the screen pass's first draw |
| D88-14 DPR (dec_668a8f0c) | Canvas2D multiplies `shadowBlur` and both offsets (with the off-canvas compensation) by the DPR |

**Design resolutions this plan makes.** Resolutions 1, 3, 4, 6, 7, 12, 13 and 14 deviate from or extend an approved decision; W88.1 Step 0 presents them to Dennis before any red test is written. Resolution 3 is recorded as an amendment of D88-10.
1. **The class follows what the renderer drew.** `Renderer` gains `shadowsDrawn?(id)` (true when the last `render()` drew slot `id`'s outer layers). The toggle holds the class on an element with outer layers only then, and on an inset-only element whenever its liquid is away and a renderer draws. So a frame that draws nothing (a lost device, a rebuild in flight, a WebGPU element past the source cap, a scratch canvas without a 2d context) keeps the browser's shadow: no gap. It reuses W74's `textReady` pattern.
2. **One rule for "away".** `liquidAway(reducedMotion, restAlpha, interaction)` in `shadow.ts` (restAlpha < 1 or slot 5 = DRAGGED, never under reduced motion, NaN = rest). The toggle reads slot 5 from the element view of the same frame the renderer read, never `dragTracker.active`, so both always agree.
3. **The knockout is the closed own coverage (an amendment of D88-10, gate Question 1).** The approved text says a hole or tear shows the page. The plain coverage is 0 in a hole, so masking by it alone removes nothing there. The knockout takes the coverage and also covers a cell lying between two covered cells at ± 1.5 kernel radii (and half that) along four axes (`KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL`, in `shadow-plan.ts`, shared by both renderers). So a hole narrower than about 3 kernel radii (≈ 15–25 px) shows the page and a straight outer edge keeps its shadow. A wider hole shows the shadow along its own rim, as two separate pieces of liquid would: that is the amendment. The union over all casters knocks out (both renderers), so no shadow lies under any shadowed element's translucent liquid.
4. **No double shadow when an instance stops (Review Focus 1).** The class goes at once, but no frame follows, so the canvas would keep the last frame with the renderer's shadows. The runtime repaints once with `RenderFrame.suppressShadows` (the liquid as it is, no shadow) before it clears the class; when even that repaint throws, it resets the canvas bitmap (`canvas.width = canvas.width`). It only does this when the instance holds a class. A lost device needs no repaint (its canvas is remounted), and neither does reduced motion: its class goes at once and the next frame draws no shadow (row 29), so a running instance shows both for at most that one frame.
5. **A shadow-only re-read.** `transitionend` for `box-shadow` queues `shadowPending`, which re-reads only the shadow (`registry.refreshShadow`): no colour snapshot, no atlas re-render. It waits for running CSS transitions like `colourPending`, and a colour re-read (`refresh`) reads the shadow too and clears it.
6. **WebGPU density channels.** Sources (element × distinct spread, spread 0 always first, because the knockout needs it) go four per layer of one `rgba16float` 2d-array texture, at most 4 layers (16 sources). **The source cap (a limitation for the gate):** an element whose sources do not fit draws no renderer shadow that frame. By resolution 1 it then holds no class either, so its browser shadow stays visible, static at the DOM box, while its liquid moves away from it; it follows the liquid again in the first frame its sources fit. 16 sources are 16 moving shadowed elements with one spread each, or fewer with several distinct spreads; elements are taken in slot order. Canvas2D has no cap. Targets are created on the first frame with shadows and kept (like T1); a resize drops them. Pass records sit at 256-byte dynamic offsets in one uniform buffer, and layer records in one storage buffer. `planShadows` allocates a few small objects per frame, only while shadows draw.
7. **The shadow pipelines are built at init**, in the liquid's validation scope, so a shadow shader bug rejects `create()` like any pipeline bug. Building them lazily would avoid changing three more approved pins (resolution 9), but it would compile pipelines mid-splash and report a WGSL bug as a runtime error.
8. **Spread per renderer.** Canvas2D dilates by 8 shifted copies, or erodes with `destination-in` for a negative spread (D88-5). WebGPU widens the kernel to R + |s| and scales the mass by k with k · tail(s / R') = 0.5 (`spreadKernel`, D88-11), so the 0.5 contour moves s px out of (or into) a straight edge.
9. **Approved tests that change** (presented at the pre-red gate, W88.1 Step 0, and shown again at the red STOP):
    - (i) `stylesheet.test.ts:105`: the `.liquid-element` rule has no `box-shadow` (D88-1).
    - (ii) `wdd-docs.test.ts`: the discovery is widened (D88-9).
    - (iii) `webgpu-renderer.test.ts:161`: the composite blends OVER (D88-13), which the gate named. The same test also pins 10 shader modules, 11 pipelines and 12 uniform / 12 storage / 24 buffer layout entries (D88-11), and its name changes from `…five_pipelines…` to `…eleven_pipelines…_D88_11`.
    - (iv) `element-observer.test.ts:54`: `OWN_CLASSES` gains `liquid-shadow-away` (D88-1).
    - (v) `auto-renderer.test.ts:194`: 11 pipelines (D88-11).
    - (vi) `webgpu-lifecycle.test.ts:226`: the init log gains the shadow modules and pipelines (D88-11).

    (iv)–(vi) and the second half of (iii) were not named at the gate; the plan validation found them, and W88.1 Step 0 presents all six. Without (iv) every shadow toggle would re-snapshot colours and re-render the atlas.
10. **Toggle order.** The order is `syncText → syncAway → syncHalo → syncShadow`: last, because it reads the renderer's `shadowsDrawn` of this frame, and none of the four reads another's class.
11. **The scene lands in red.** It uses only the public API, `runtimeOf` and `FluidRuntime.shadowLayers` (stubbed in W88.1), so the e2e specs load and fail on assertions. The specs need it from the first run.
12. **An author `transition: box-shadow` is held off at the toggle (gate Question 2).** Every add or removal of `liquid-shadow-away` would start the author's own box-shadow transition (CSS transitions win over `!important`). Measured on the validation copy (Fade, `transition: box-shadow 0.3s`, a splash at 0.5):
    - **without the fix**, the browser's shadow faded out over ≈ 3 frames while the renderer's was already drawn (ring energy 1.55 → 2.15 × the rest value: a double shadow), and faded in over ≈ 3 frames after the handoff (0.00 → 0.16 → 0.64 → 0.98: a gap);
    - there was **no loop**: one `transitionend` after each class change, one shadow re-read with its lift, nothing re-triggered.

    So W88 writes the class with the transition held off. `classHolds` gains a `write` hook; for an element whose computed transitions animate `box-shadow` (`runsBoxShadowTransition`: `all` or `box-shadow` with a duration > 0), the shadow class is written under inline `transition: none !important` held over a style flush (`withTransitionsOff`, W66's lift, which `withClassLifted` now uses too). The style attribute is restored to its exact string. Other elements get a plain class write. The side effect: any other transition running on that element at that moment jumps to its end. E9 measures it in the browser (the browser's shadow is `none` in the first moving frame and the full author value at the handoff, with no `transitionend`), and the plain write fails E9 (a mutation run).
13. **A resting element's browser shadow paints over a neighbour's moving liquid (a limitation for the gate).** The canvas sits at `z-index: −1` (W82), so a resting element's own box-shadow (drawn by the browser, in the page) lies above any liquid that splashes or is dragged past it. A liquid passing a resting glowing element goes under its glow. It is accepted: drawing every shadow in the canvas would give up the pixel-exact, free shadow at rest (D88-1). W88.5 Step 8 records a still of it.
14. **Unobserve mid-motion (Review Focus 1).** `unobserve(el)` releases the class at once, but the canvas keeps that element's renderer shadow until the next frame (≤ 1 frame, or until resume on a paused instance). Accepted like W74's text: the next frame repaints.
15. **CSS layer order is kept only approximately.** Within a radius group the layers composite in CSS order (the first on top); across groups, and between the half- and quarter-resolution accumulators, the order is the groups' creation order. Overlapping layers of different radii can stack in a different order than CSS does. That is invisible for the usual one- or two-layer shadows.

## Global Constraints (W88.1 onward)

- **Unchanged contracts:** no Rust change, and the FFI, strides and 7-argument constructor stay as they are. Every public export of the three packages stays (`FluidRuntime.shadowLayers` and `RenderFrame.suppressShadows` are internal). Every acceptance step and Linux baseline stays: the acceptance scene has no `box-shadow`.
- **Rule of Two:** renderers never touch the DOM. Their scratch canvases are detached `document.createElement("canvas")`, as the density buffer already is (A6). The runtime owns every class write.
- **Stylesheet:** the new rule sits in `@layer liquiddom` and `@media screen and (forced-colors: none)`, so print and forced colours show the author's shadow.
- **Values:**
  - `SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS = "liquid-shadow-away"`, `MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS = 4`, `KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL = 1.5` (in `shadow-plan.ts`).
  - `SHADOW_SCALE_HALF = 0.5`, `SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER = 0.25`, `SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX = 32`, `SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS = 6`, `SHADOW_BUCKETS_PX = [4, 8, 16, 32, 64]`.
  - `SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES = 16` (4 channels × 4 layers), `SHADOW_MAX_TAPS = 64`, 256-byte pass slots.
  - Canvas2D `shadowBlur` = CSS blur × DPR (D88-14).
- **Must NOT (ward):**
  - change the engine, the FFI or any public API;
  - touch the DOM from a renderer;
  - move a Linux baseline;
  - change an approved test beyond resolution 9 without Dennis' approval;
  - copy spike code (D2).
- **Process:**
  - commit by path, never `git add -A`; `git add` and `git commit -F <message file>` as separate commands, with the message files in `$S`;
  - trailers `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` and `Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01JWKWkrzUtppUzFBpDS954E`;
  - no pushes;
  - ward status only via `wdd ward status 88 …` at the STOP points;
  - one changeset (`minor` for `liquiddom`, which the fixed group follows);
  - never write repo files while an e2e run or Vite is running.
- **Questions to Dennis are laid out in full in the chat**, never as a pointer into a file.

## Review Focus

1. **No double shadow when the class goes outside a frame.**
    - A stopped instance clears the class while its canvas still shows the last frame. → rows 30–31 (the repaint with `suppressShadows`; the canvas reset when the repaint throws).
    - Reduced motion: the class goes at once and the next frame draws no shadow. → row 29.
    - `unobserve(el)` mid-motion releases the class at once; the canvas keeps that element's shadow for one frame (resolution 14, accepted). → row 28 (the class), W88.5 Step 8 (vision).
2. **A `:hover` box-shadow change without a transition** shows the old shadow until `refresh(el)`, and never a stale one or `none` after it, even mid-motion with the class held. → row 26, row 8.
3. **Two instances observing one element** keep one hold each; the shadow is drawn by the instance(s) whose liquid is away, and the class is released only by the last. → rows 20 and 33.
4. **A shadow larger than the container or the canvas** is clipped by the canvas, with no error and no scissor outside its target. → rows 44 and 57.
5. **A box-shadow with `currentColor` or a CSS variable**: the computed value arrives resolved. → row 9, E5.
6. **Spike open point: the non-monotonic restAlpha before rest** (0.14 → 0.97 → 0.83 → 1). Every frame of that window has the class exactly while restAlpha < 1, with no gap and no double frame. → E3 and E3w (both record from the first frame with restAlpha ≥ 0.8; at red they catch f120 at restAlpha 0.833).
7. **Spike open point: the handoff with exact radii (WebGPU).** → E3w; the plan validation measured ring ratios 0.981–1.028 on Metal.
8. **The class never hides the browser's shadow without the renderer's taking its place** (resolution 1). → rows 16, 32, 61.
9. **The toggle's own class never re-reads colours, shadows or atlas text** (resolution 9 (iv)). → rows 14 and 24.
10. **An author `transition: box-shadow` never fades the browser's shadow at a toggle** (resolution 12), and the hold-off leaves the style attribute exactly as it was. → rows 13a, 13b, E9.

## Shared interfaces (binding)

- `packages/core/ts/src/shadow.ts`:
  - `interface ShadowLayer { readonly color: RGBA; readonly dx: number; readonly dy: number; readonly blur: number; readonly spread: number; readonly inset: boolean }` (CSS px);
  - `MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS = 4`;
  - `parseBoxShadow(computed: string | null | undefined): readonly ShadowLayer[]` (every layer, CSS order);
  - `outerShadows(layers)` (memoised, ≤ 4 outer);
  - `hasOuterLayer(layers)`;
  - `snapshotShadow(el: HTMLElement): readonly ShadowLayer[]`;
  - `liquidAway(reducedMotion: boolean, restAlpha: number, interaction: number): boolean`.
- `color.ts`: `withTransitionsOff<T>(el, change: () => T): T` (W66's lift: inline `transition: none !important` over a style flush, the style attribute restored exactly) and `withClassLifted<T>(el, className, read: () => T): T` (built on it; `snapshotColorsWithout` uses it).
- `stylesheet.ts`: `SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS = "liquid-shadow-away"`, `addShadowAwayClass(el)`, `removeShadowAwayClass(el)`, `runsBoxShadowTransition(el): boolean`; `classHolds(cls, write?)`.
- `element-registry.ts`: `ElementRecord.shadows` (all layers) and `refreshShadow(el): boolean`.
- `renderers/frame.ts`: `ElementPaint.shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[]` (outer, ≤ 4), `RenderFrame.suppressShadows?: boolean` and `Renderer.shadowsDrawn?(id: number): boolean`.
- `shadow-toggle.ts`:
  - `interface ShadowEntry { id; el; shadows }`;
  - `interface ShadowSyncArgs { entries; restAlpha(id); interaction(id); renderer: Renderer | null; reducedMotion; held }`;
  - `wantsShadowAway(rendererOn: boolean, away: boolean, layers: readonly ShadowLayer[], drawn: boolean): boolean`;
  - `syncShadowClasses(a)`, `clearShadowClasses(held)`, `releaseShadowClass(held, el)`.
- `runtime.ts`: `FluidRuntime.shadowLayers(el)` (@internal, scene hooks only).
- Canvas2D:
  - `renderers/shadow-plan.ts`: `ShadowCasters` (`collect(frame)`, `ids`, `bounds`, `start`, `count`, `order`), `KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL` (shared with WebGPU);
  - `renderers/shadow-canvas2d.ts`: `ShadowCanvas2D` (`draw(ctx, frame, cellPx, gridW, gridH)`, `drawn(id)`, `destroy()`), `MASK_MAX_CELLS`, `DILATE_DIRS`;
  - `FluidCanvas2DRenderer.shadowsDrawn(id)`.
- WebGPU:
  - `renderers/shadow-plan.ts`: `planShadows(casters, frame, texelCss): ShadowPlan` (`sources`, `records`, `groups`, `drawn`, `densityLayers`), `spreadKernel`, `nearestBucket`, `groupBlur` and the constants above;
  - `renderers/webgpu/shadow-passes.ts`: `ShadowPasses` (`prepare`, `encode`, `drawComposite`, `drawn`, `resetDrawn`, `resize`, `destroy`), `ShadowPipelines`, `SP`, `SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS = 64`, `SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS = 32`, `scissorOf`;
  - `renderers/webgpu/shaders.ts`: `SHADOW_DENSITY_WGSL`, `SHADOW_RESOLVE_WGSL`, `SHADOW_BLUR_WGSL`, `SHADOW_KNOCKOUT_WGSL`, `SHADOW_COMPOSITE_WGSL`, `SHADOW_MAX_TAPS`;
  - `WebGPURenderer.shadowsDrawn(id)`.
- `window.__shadows` (`demo/test-hooks.ts`):
  - `ShadowsId = "drop" | "glow" | "spread" | "current" | "inset" | "glass" | "plain" | "fade"` (Fade carries `transition: box-shadow 0.3s`);
  - `ShadowsLayer`;
  - `ShadowsHook { ready; ids; advance(frames); pixels(); restAlpha(); held(); splash(id, strength?); layers(id); rect(id); activeRenderer }`.

- [ ] **Step 0: Pre-red gate (STOP before any red test).** This plan amends D88-10 and extends other approved decisions, and decisions come before red. Present to Dennis **in full in the chat**, each with its consequence:
  1. **Question 1, D88-10 as an AMENDMENT** (resolution 3). Approved: "a hole or tear in the liquid shows the page and not the shadow". Planned: the closed own coverage, where a hole narrower than about 3 kernel radii (≈ 15–25 px on the scene) shows the page, and a wider hole (a strength-1 splash punches 40 × 25 px openings) shows the shadow along its own inner rim, as two pieces of liquid would. The validation stills showed a cyan glow inside the Glow pill's big hole and grey inside Drop's.
     - **Alternative:** fill every enclosed hole. That is a flood fill of each mask in Canvas2D; WebGPU has no cheap flood fill, so the knockout would be built on the CPU from the casters' particles and uploaded as a texture, ≈ 0.16 ms per shadowed element.
     - **Recommendation:** the closing. The reported flecks were small holes; a wide hole is where the liquid really parted.
  2. **Question 2, an author `transition: box-shadow`** (resolution 12). Report the measurement: without a fix, a double shadow for the transition's duration when the liquid leaves rest (ring energy up to 2.15 ×) and a gap after the handoff (0.00 → 0.98 over ≈ 3 frames); no loop. The plan fixes it in W88.2: the shadow class is written with the author's box-shadow transition held off (inline `transition: none` over a style flush, only for elements whose transitions animate box-shadow). Side effect: another transition running on that element at that moment jumps to its end. That will be common, not rare: Tailwind's default `transition` utility animates `box-shadow` together with `transform`, colours and opacity, so a hover scale or colour change still in progress when the liquid leaves or reaches rest snaps to its end. Alternative: document it only.
  3. **The WebGPU source cap** (resolution 6): past 16 density sources per frame an element draws no renderer shadow and keeps its browser shadow static at the DOM box while its liquid moves. Canvas2D has no cap.
  4. **A resting element's browser shadow paints over a neighbour's moving liquid** (resolution 13), because the canvas sits under the page. Accepted as a limitation.
  5. **The other extensions of approved decisions:**
     - resolution 1: the class follows `Renderer.shadowsDrawn`;
     - resolution 4: the stop repaint with `suppressShadows`, or a canvas reset;
     - resolution 7: the shadow pipelines are built at init;
     - resolution 14: one frame of double shadow after `unobserve` mid-motion.
  6. **All six approved-test changes** (resolution 9). The four not named at the gate:
     - the pipeline, module and layout counts (and the test name) in `webgpu-renderer.test.ts:161`;
     - `element-observer.test.ts:54`;
     - `auto-renderer.test.ts:194`;
     - `webgpu-lifecycle.test.ts:226`.

  On his answers:
  - `saga_record_decision` for each, and an AMENDED D88-10. In `ward-088.md` the line becomes `Decision: AMENDED YYYY-MM-DD — knockout by the closed own coverage: holes narrower than ≈ 3 kernel radii show the page, wider holes show the shadow on their rim (saga dec_…)`, or the answer he gives.
  - **Rewrite the existing `| D88-10 |` row of NORTH-STAR's "Plan decisions" log in place. Do not add a second row.** Mirror the amended rows of D68-2 and D70-3: `| D88-10 | Knockout: AMENDED YYYY-MM-DD — <the answer, e.g. each element's shadow masked by its closed own coverage; holes wider than ≈ 3 kernel radii show the shadow on their rim> (was own liquid coverage, dec_5af57bbe) | W88 | dec_<new> |`. This keeps `wdd-docs.test.ts` "given_gated_fluid_ward_when_checked_then_each_decision_is_in_the_north_star_plan_log_with_its_saga_id" green. That test takes the **first** row starting with `|D88-10|` and expects it to contain the saga id of the ward's `Decision:` line. After the D88-9 widening it checks W88 once the ward is past `planned`, so a second row, or the old row, would fail the red run.

```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-1 … D88-3): the author's `box-shadow`, read for the renderers. W88.1 stub: the shared
 * interface only; W88.2 fills it.
 */
import type { RGBA } from "./color";

/** One `box-shadow` layer in CSS px (D88-3). */
export interface ShadowLayer {
  readonly color: RGBA;
  readonly dx: number;
  readonly dy: number;
  /** ≥ 0. */
  readonly blur: number;
  /** May be negative. */
  readonly spread: number;
  readonly inset: boolean;
}

/** D88-2: the renderer draws at most this many outer layers per element (the first ones in CSS order). */
export const MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS = 4;

const NONE: readonly ShadowLayer[] = Object.freeze([]);

export function parseBoxShadow(_computed: string | null | undefined): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  return NONE;
}

export function outerShadows(_layers: readonly ShadowLayer[]): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  return NONE;
}

export function hasOuterLayer(_layers: readonly ShadowLayer[]): boolean {
  return false;
}

export function snapshotShadow(_el: HTMLElement): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  return NONE;
}

export function liquidAway(_reducedMotion: boolean, _restAlpha: number, _interaction: number): boolean {
  return false;
}
```

```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-1, D88-2, D88-7): the `liquid-shadow-away` toggle. W88.1 stub: the shared interface
 * only; W88.2 fills it.
 */
import type { Renderer } from "./renderers/frame";
import type { ShadowLayer } from "./shadow";

export interface ShadowEntry {
  readonly id: number;
  readonly el: HTMLElement;
  readonly shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[];
}

export interface ShadowSyncArgs {
  readonly entries: Iterable<ShadowEntry>;
  restAlpha(id: number): number;
  interaction(id: number): number;
  readonly renderer: Renderer | null;
  readonly reducedMotion: boolean;
  readonly held: Set<HTMLElement>;
}

export function wantsShadowAway(_rendererOn: boolean, _away: boolean, _layers: readonly ShadowLayer[], _drawn: boolean): boolean {
  return false;
}

export function syncShadowClasses(_a: ShadowSyncArgs): void {}

export function clearShadowClasses(_held: Set<HTMLElement>): void {}

export function releaseShadowClass(_held: Set<HTMLElement>, _el: HTMLElement): void {}
```

```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-6, D88-11): the shadow casters and the WebGPU shadow plan. W88.1 stub: the shared
 * interface only; W88.3 fills it.
 */
import type { RenderFrame } from "./frame";

export const SHADOW_SCALE_HALF = 0.5;
export const SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER = 0.25;
export const SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX = 32;
export const SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS = 6;
export const SHADOW_BUCKETS_PX: readonly number[] = Object.freeze([4, 8, 16, 32, 64]);
export const SHADOW_CHANNELS = 4;
export const SHADOW_MAX_DENSITY_LAYERS = 4;
export const SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES = SHADOW_CHANNELS * SHADOW_MAX_DENSITY_LAYERS;
export const KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL = 1.5;

export interface Rect {
  x0: number;
  y0: number;
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
}

export class ShadowCasters {
  readonly ids: number[] = [];
  bounds = new Float64Array(0);
  start = new Int32Array(0);
  count = new Int32Array(0);
  order = new Int32Array(0);

  collect(_frame: RenderFrame): number {
    return 0;
  }
}

export function nearestBucket(blur: number): number {
  return blur;
}

export function groupBlur(blur: number): number {
  return blur;
}

export function spreadKernel(kernelR: number, _spread: number): { readonly radius: number; readonly weightScale: number } {
  return { radius: kernelR, weightScale: 1 };
}

export interface ShadowSource {
  readonly id: number;
  readonly spread: number;
  readonly layer: number;
  readonly channel: number;
  readonly radius: number;
  readonly weight: number;
  readonly rect: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowRecord {
  readonly source: number;
  readonly dx: number;
  readonly dy: number;
  readonly color: readonly [number, number, number, number];
  readonly rect: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowGroup {
  readonly blur: number;
  readonly scale: number;
  readonly first: number;
  readonly count: number;
  readonly reach: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowPlan {
  readonly sources: readonly ShadowSource[];
  readonly records: readonly ShadowRecord[];
  readonly groups: readonly ShadowGroup[];
  readonly drawn: readonly number[];
  readonly densityLayers: number;
}

export const EMPTY_PLAN: ShadowPlan = Object.freeze({ sources: [], records: [], groups: [], drawn: [], densityLayers: 0 });

export function planShadows(_casters: ShadowCasters, _frame: RenderFrame, _texelCss: number): ShadowPlan {
  return EMPTY_PLAN;
}
```

**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/shadow.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * @vitest-environment jsdom
 * W88 (D88-1 … D88-3): the box-shadow parser, the outer-layer cap, the snapshot that lifts
 * `liquid-shadow-away`, and the shared "liquid is away" rule. jsdom returns a box-shadow as
 * specified (not normalised), so the parser takes the parts in any order; jsdom ignores @layer,
 * so the snapshot test emulates the layered !important rule with an unlayered one.
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { setColorProbe } from "../src/color";
import { Interaction } from "../src/fluid-layout";
import { hasOuterLayer, liquidAway, MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS, outerShadows, parseBoxShadow, snapshotShadow, type ShadowLayer } from "../src/shadow";
import { SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS } from "../src/stylesheet";

const layer = (o: Partial<ShadowLayer>): ShadowLayer => ({ color: [0, 0, 0, 1], dx: 0, dy: 0, blur: 0, spread: 0, inset: false, ...o });

beforeEach(() => {
  document.head.replaceChildren();
  document.body.replaceChildren();
  setColorProbe(() => null); // W86: jsdom has no 2d canvas; a named colour stays unread
});
afterEach(() => {
  setColorProbe(null);
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("W88: parseBoxShadow (D88-3)", () => {
  it("given_a_computed_value_with_two_layers_when_parsed_then_colour_offsets_blur_spread_and_inset_in_css_order", () => {
    expect(parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px, rgb(0, 255, 255) 0px 0px 12px 2px inset")).toEqual([
      layer({ color: [0, 0, 0, 0.4], dy: 8, blur: 20 }),
      layer({ color: [0, 255, 255, 1], blur: 12, spread: 2, inset: true }),
    ]);
  });

  it("given_none_empty_or_garbage_when_parsed_then_no_layers", () => {
    for (const raw of ["none", "", "  ", "foo", "rgb(0, 0, 0) 1px", "rgb(0, 0, 0) 1px 2px 3px 4px 5px", "rgb(0, 0, 0) 1px 2px -3px", "rgb(0, 0, 0) 1em 2em", "1px 2px 3px", "rgb(0, 0, 0) 2 3"]) {
      expect(parseBoxShadow(raw), JSON.stringify(raw)).toEqual([]);
    }
    expect(parseBoxShadow(null)).toEqual([]);
    expect(parseBoxShadow(undefined)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("given_the_specified_form_with_lengths_first_unitless_zero_and_short_alpha_when_parsed_then_the_same_layer_as_the_computed_form", () => {
    const computed = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px");
    expect(parseBoxShadow("0 8px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4)")).toEqual(computed);
    expect(parseBoxShadow("inset 0 0 2px #ff0000")).toEqual([layer({ color: [255, 0, 0, 1], blur: 2, inset: true })]);
    expect(parseBoxShadow("2px 3px rgb(1, 2, 3)")).toEqual([layer({ color: [1, 2, 3, 1], dx: 2, dy: 3 })]);
  });

  it("given_an_oklch_layer_when_parsed_then_its_colour_comes_from_the_W86_colour_probe", () => {
    setColorProbe((raw) => (raw === "oklch(0.82 0.15 205 / 0.55)" ? [0, 223, 242, 0.55] : null));
    expect(parseBoxShadow("oklch(0.82 0.15 205 / 0.55) 0px 0px 28px 2px")).toEqual([layer({ color: [0, 223, 242, 0.55], blur: 28, spread: 2 })]);
  });

  it("given_a_transparent_or_unreadable_layer_among_others_when_parsed_then_only_that_layer_is_dropped", () => {
    expect(parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 0px 4px 8px 0px, rgb(9, 9, 9) 1px 1px 0px 0px, chartreuse 0px 0px 2px 0px")).toEqual([
      layer({ color: [9, 9, 9, 1], dx: 1, dy: 1 }),
    ]);
  });

  it("given_zero_blur_and_negative_spread_when_parsed_then_both_are_kept", () => {
    expect(parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 0px 4px 0px -6px")).toEqual([layer({ color: [0, 0, 0, 0.3], dy: 4, spread: -6 })]);
  });

  it("given_six_layers_with_two_inset_when_outerShadows_then_the_first_four_outer_layers_and_the_same_array_twice_D88_2", () => {
    const all = parseBoxShadow(
      "rgb(1, 0, 0) 0px 0px 1px 0px, rgb(2, 0, 0) 0px 0px 2px 0px inset, rgb(3, 0, 0) 0px 0px 3px 0px, rgb(4, 0, 0) 0px 0px 4px 0px, rgb(5, 0, 0) 0px 0px 5px 0px inset, rgb(6, 0, 0) 0px 0px 6px 0px, rgb(7, 0, 0) 0px 0px 7px 0px",
    );
    expect(MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS).toBe(4);
    const outer = outerShadows(all);
    expect(outer.map((l) => l.blur)).toEqual([1, 3, 4, 6]);
    expect(outerShadows(all), "memoised per array").toBe(outer);
    expect(hasOuterLayer(all)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasOuterLayer(parseBoxShadow("rgb(1, 1, 1) 0px 0px 2px 0px inset"))).toBe(false);
    expect(outerShadows(parseBoxShadow("rgb(1, 1, 1) 0px 0px 2px 0px inset"))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("W88: snapshotShadow (D88-3)", () => {
  it("given_an_element_holding_liquid_shadow_away_when_snapshotted_then_the_author_shadow_is_read_and_class_and_style_are_restored_exactly", () => {
    const style = document.createElement("style");
    style.textContent = `.card { box-shadow: 0 8px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4); } .${SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS} { box-shadow: none !important; }`;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
    const el = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("div"));
    el.className = `card ${SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS}`;
    el.setAttribute("style", "--probe:  1 ;");
    expect(getComputedStyle(el).boxShadow, "the toggle class hides the author's shadow").toBe("none");
    expect(snapshotShadow(el)).toEqual([layer({ color: [0, 0, 0, 0.4], dy: 8, blur: 20 })]);
    expect(el.getAttribute("class")).toBe(`card ${SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS}`);
    expect(el.getAttribute("style")).toBe("--probe:  1 ;");
    const plain = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("div"));
    expect(snapshotShadow(plain), "no box-shadow").toEqual([]);
    expect(plain.hasAttribute("style"), "no lift without the class").toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_currentColor_resolved_by_the_browser_when_snapshotted_then_the_layer_carries_the_text_colour_review_focus_5", () => {
    const el = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("button"));
    vi.spyOn(window, "getComputedStyle").mockReturnValue({ boxShadow: "rgb(200, 40, 120) 0px 0px 16px 0px", color: "rgb(200, 40, 120)" } as CSSStyleDeclaration);
    expect(snapshotShadow(el)).toEqual([layer({ color: [200, 40, 120, 1], blur: 16 })]);
  });
});

describe("W88: liquidAway (D88-1)", () => {
  it("given_restAlpha_and_interaction_when_asked_then_away_below_1_or_dragged_never_under_reduced_motion_and_nan_is_rest", () => {
    expect(liquidAway(false, 0.4, Interaction.IDLE)).toBe(true);
    expect(liquidAway(false, 1, Interaction.IDLE)).toBe(false);
    expect(liquidAway(false, 1, Interaction.DRAGGED), "dragged").toBe(true);
    expect(liquidAway(false, 1, Interaction.HOVER)).toBe(false);
    expect(liquidAway(false, Number.NaN, Interaction.IDLE), "NaN counts as rest").toBe(false);
    expect(liquidAway(true, 0.2, Interaction.DRAGGED), "reduced motion").toBe(false);
  });
});
```

**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/shadow-toggle.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * @vitest-environment jsdom
 * W88 (D88-1, D88-2; Review Focus 3): the `liquid-shadow-away` toggle (shadow-toggle.ts), the
 * W74/W75/W78 toggle pattern: on while the element's liquid is away and the renderer drew its
 * outer layers this frame (an inset-only element whenever its liquid is away), never under
 * reduced motion or without a renderer; refcounted across instances; written only on change.
 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { Interaction } from "../src/fluid-layout";
import type { Renderer } from "../src/renderers/frame";
import { parseBoxShadow, type ShadowLayer } from "../src/shadow";
import { clearShadowClasses, releaseShadowClass, syncShadowClasses, wantsShadowAway, type ShadowEntry } from "../src/shadow-toggle";
import { SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS } from "../src/stylesheet";

const DROP: readonly ShadowLayer[] = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px");
const INSET: readonly ShadowLayer[] = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 2px 6px 0px inset");
const NONE: readonly ShadowLayer[] = [];

let a: HTMLElement;
let b: HTMLElement;
beforeEach(() => {
  document.body.replaceChildren();
  a = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("button"));
  b = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("div"));
});

const on = (el: HTMLElement): boolean => el.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS);

function classWrites(el: HTMLElement, run: () => void): number {
  const mo = new MutationObserver(() => {});
  mo.observe(el, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });
  run();
  const n = mo.takeRecords().length;
  mo.disconnect();
  return n;
}

/** A renderer stand-in: `drawn` holds the slots whose shadows its last render drew. */
function renderer(drawn: Set<number>): Renderer {
  return {
    init: async () => {},
    render: () => {},
    resize: () => {},
    destroy: () => {},
    shadowsDrawn: (id: number) => drawn.has(id),
  };
}

interface Opts {
  entries: ShadowEntry[];
  alpha: Map<number, number>;
  interaction?: Map<number, number>;
  r: Renderer | null;
  reducedMotion?: boolean;
  held: Set<HTMLElement>;
}

function sync(o: Opts): void {
  syncShadowClasses({
    entries: o.entries,
    restAlpha: (id) => o.alpha.get(id) ?? 1,
    interaction: (id) => o.interaction?.get(id) ?? Interaction.IDLE,
    renderer: o.r,
    reducedMotion: o.reducedMotion ?? false,
    held: o.held,
  });
}

describe("W88: syncShadowClasses (D88-1, D88-2)", () => {
  it("given_a_moving_element_whose_outer_shadow_the_renderer_drew_when_synced_then_the_class_once_and_off_in_the_frame_restAlpha_reaches_1", () => {
    const held = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const alpha = new Map([[0, 0.4], [1, 0.4]]);
    const drawn = new Set([0]);
    const o: Opts = { entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }, { id: 1, el: b, shadows: NONE }], alpha, r: renderer(drawn), held };
    const writes = classWrites(a, () => {
      for (let f = 0; f < 5; f++) sync(o);
    });
    expect(writes, "one class write for five moving frames").toBe(1);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(true);
    expect(classWrites(b, () => sync(o)), "an element without a box-shadow costs nothing").toBe(0);
    expect(on(b)).toBe(false);
    alpha.set(0, 1); // D88-7: the instant handoff, in the frame restAlpha reaches 1
    drawn.clear();
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(false);
    expect(held.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("given_outer_layers_the_renderer_did_not_draw_or_no_renderer_when_synced_then_no_class_and_an_existing_hold_is_withdrawn", () => {
    const held = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const drawn = new Set([0]);
    const o: Opts = { entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }], alpha: new Map([[0, 0.2]]), r: renderer(drawn), held };
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(true);
    drawn.clear(); // e.g. a WebGPU frame that drew nothing (a lost device) or an element past the source cap
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a), "the browser's shadow never hides without the renderer's").toBe(false);
    drawn.add(0);
    sync({ ...o, r: null }); // a device-lost rebuild in flight
    expect(on(a)).toBe(false);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(true, true, DROP, false)).toBe(false);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(true, true, DROP, true)).toBe(true);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(false, true, DROP, true)).toBe(false);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(true, false, DROP, true), "at rest").toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_an_inset_only_element_whose_liquid_is_away_when_synced_then_the_class_hides_the_inset_D88_2", () => {
    const held = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const alpha = new Map([[0, 0.5]]);
    const o: Opts = { entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: INSET }], alpha, r: renderer(new Set()), held };
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a), "inset shows at rest and hides in motion").toBe(true);
    alpha.set(0, 1);
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(false);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(true, true, INSET, false)).toBe(true);
    expect(wantsShadowAway(true, true, NONE, true)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_a_dragged_element_at_restAlpha_1_when_synced_then_the_class_D88_1", () => {
    const held = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    sync({ entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }], alpha: new Map([[0, 1]]), interaction: new Map([[0, Interaction.DRAGGED]]), r: renderer(new Set([0])), held });
    expect(on(a)).toBe(true);
    clearShadowClasses(held);
  });

  it("given_reduced_motion_or_a_nan_restAlpha_when_synced_then_no_class_and_an_existing_hold_is_withdrawn", () => {
    const held = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const alpha = new Map([[0, 0.3]]);
    const o: Opts = { entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }], alpha, r: renderer(new Set([0])), held };
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(true);
    sync({ ...o, reducedMotion: true });
    expect(on(a), "reduced motion: the browser's shadow throughout").toBe(false);
    alpha.set(0, Number.NaN);
    sync(o);
    expect(on(a), "NaN counts as rest").toBe(false);
    expect(held.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("given_two_instances_holding_one_element_when_one_releases_then_the_class_stays_until_both_release_review_focus_3", () => {
    const one = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const two = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const entries: ShadowEntry[] = [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }];
    for (const held of [one, two]) sync({ entries, alpha: new Map([[0, 0.5]]), r: renderer(new Set([0])), held });
    releaseShadowClass(one, a);
    expect(on(a), "the other instance's liquid is still away").toBe(true);
    releaseShadowClass(two, a);
    expect(on(a)).toBe(false);
    releaseShadowClass(two, a); // no hold left: a no-op
    // One instance at rest, the other away: one hold, the class stays for the moving one.
    sync({ entries, alpha: new Map([[0, 1]]), r: renderer(new Set()), held: one });
    sync({ entries, alpha: new Map([[0, 0.5]]), r: renderer(new Set([0])), held: two });
    expect([one.size, two.size, on(a)]).toEqual([0, 1, true]);
    clearShadowClasses(two);
  });

  it("given_clearShadowClasses_or_a_missed_release_when_called_then_this_instance_lets_go", () => {
    const mine = new Set<HTMLElement>();
    const r = renderer(new Set([0, 1]));
    sync({ entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }, { id: 1, el: b, shadows: DROP }], alpha: new Map([[0, 0.5], [1, 0.5]]), r, held: mine });
    expect([on(a), on(b)]).toEqual([true, true]);
    sync({ entries: [{ id: 0, el: a, shadows: DROP }], alpha: new Map([[0, 0.5]]), r, held: mine }); // b no longer observed
    expect(on(b)).toBe(false);
    clearShadowClasses(mine); // a stop, reduced motion, a lost device, destroy
    expect(on(a)).toBe(false);
    expect(mine.size).toBe(0);
  });
});
```
**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/shadow-runtime.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * @vitest-environment jsdom
 * W88 in the runtime (D88-1, D88-3, D88-7; Review Focus 1, 2, 3): the snapshot reaches the paints,
 * the toggle runs after render() and follows what the renderer drew, the W75 triggers and
 * `box-shadow` transitionend re-read the shadow, and every fail-safe path takes the class off without
 * a double shadow. jsdom ignores @layer and returns box-shadow as specified, so the author styles
 * add the unlayered emulation of `.liquid-shadow-away`.
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createFluidRuntime, type FluidRuntime } from "../src/runtime";
import { createManualClock, type ManualClock } from "../src/clock";
import { ElementRegistry } from "../src/element-registry";
import { FluidCanvas2DRenderer } from "../src/renderers/fluid-canvas2d";
import type { RenderFrame } from "../src/renderers/frame";
import { WebGPURenderer } from "../src/renderers/webgpu/webgpu-renderer";
import { ELEMENT_CLASS, SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS } from "../src/stylesheet";
import { fakeAtlasCanvas } from "./_fake-atlas";
import { installFakeCanvas2D, type FakeCanvasHandle } from "./_fake-canvas";
import { mockRect, resetDom, setupFacadeTestEnv } from "./_facade-helpers";
import { installFakeGpuLifecycle, type LifecycleGpu } from "./_fake-gpu";
import { movingBackend, type MovingBackend } from "./_moving-backend";

let fake2d: FakeCanvasHandle;
let gpu: LifecycleGpu | null = null;
const live: FluidRuntime[] = [];
const flush = (): Promise<void> => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

beforeEach(() => {
  resetDom();
  fake2d = installFakeCanvas2D();
  setupFacadeTestEnv();
});
afterEach(() => {
  for (const rt of live.splice(0)) rt.destroy();
  gpu?.restore();
  gpu = null;
  fake2d.restore();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

const DROP = { color: [0, 0, 0, 0.4], dx: 0, dy: 8, blur: 20, spread: 0, inset: false };
const INSET = { color: [255, 255, 255, 1], dx: 0, dy: 2, blur: 4, spread: 0, inset: true };
const GLOW = { color: [0, 255, 255, 1], dx: 0, dy: 0, blur: 12, spread: 0, inset: false };

/** Author styles plus the unlayered emulation of `.liquid-element` and `.liquid-shadow-away`. */
function authorStyles(): HTMLStyleElement {
  const style = document.createElement("style");
  style.textContent = [
    ".btn { background-color: rgb(47, 111, 222); color: rgb(255, 255, 255); box-shadow: 0 8px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4), inset 0 2px 4px rgb(255, 255, 255); }",
    ".btn.alt { box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgb(0, 255, 255); }",
    `.${ELEMENT_CLASS} { background-color: transparent !important; }`,
    `.${SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS} { box-shadow: none !important; }`,
  ].join("\n");
  document.head.appendChild(style);
  return style;
}

function button(x = 10): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.createElement("button");
  el.className = "btn";
  mockRect(el, x, 10, 140, 48);
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  return el;
}

async function start(renderer: "canvas2d" | "webgpu" = "canvas2d", moving: MovingBackend = movingBackend()): Promise<{ rt: FluidRuntime; clock: ManualClock; moving: MovingBackend }> {
  if (renderer === "webgpu") gpu = installFakeGpuLifecycle();
  const clock = createManualClock();
  const rt = await createFluidRuntime({
    particles: 256, maxElements: 4, seed: 1, testBackend: moving.backend, clock, renderer, textAtlasCanvas: fakeAtlasCanvas().create,
  });
  live.push(rt);
  return { rt, clock, moving };
}

/** Records every frame render() was called with, and the class state of `el` at that moment. */
function captureRenders(proto: { render(frame: RenderFrame): void }, el?: () => HTMLElement | undefined): Array<{ frame: RenderFrame; held: boolean }> {
  const out: Array<{ frame: RenderFrame; held: boolean }> = [];
  const original = proto.render;
  vi.spyOn(proto, "render").mockImplementation(function (this: unknown, frame: RenderFrame) {
    out.push({ frame, held: el?.()?.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS) ?? false });
    return original.call(this, frame);
  });
  return out;
}

function transitionEnd(prop: string): Event {
  const e = new Event("transitionend", { bubbles: true });
  Object.defineProperty(e, "propertyName", { value: prop });
  return e;
}

const held = (el: HTMLElement): boolean => el.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS);

describe("W88: the snapshot in the runtime (D88-2, D88-3)", () => {
  it("given_an_observed_element_with_an_outer_and_an_inset_layer_when_a_frame_runs_then_the_paint_carries_the_outer_layer_and_shadowLayers_both", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock } = await start();
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows).toEqual([DROP]);
    expect(rt.shadowLayers(el)).toEqual([DROP, INSET]);
    expect(rt.shadowLayers(document.createElement("div")), "unobserved").toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("W88: the toggle in the runtime (D88-1, D88-7)", () => {
  it("given_canvas2d_and_a_moving_shadowed_element_when_frames_run_then_the_class_follows_each_render_and_goes_in_the_frame_restAlpha_reaches_1", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype, () => el);
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.5);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.held, "at the first moving render the browser's shadow still shows").toBe(false);
    expect(held(el), "after that render the renderer's shadow replaces it").toBe(true);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.held).toBe(true);
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, null); // restAlpha 1
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "D88-7: the instant handoff, in the same frame").toBe(false);
    expect(getComputedStyle(el).boxShadow).toContain("20px");
  });

  it("given_the_class_toggling_every_frame_for_60_frames_when_they_run_then_no_colour_or_shadow_re_read_and_no_atlas_invalidation", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    const refresh = vi.spyOn(ElementRegistry.prototype, "refresh");
    const refreshShadow = vi.spyOn(ElementRegistry.prototype, "refreshShadow");
    const before = rt.textInvalidations;
    let toggles = 0;
    for (let f = 0; f < 60; f++) {
      moving.setRestAlpha(id, f % 2 === 0 ? 0.5 : null);
      const was = held(el);
      clock.advance(1);
      await flush();
      if (held(el) !== was) toggles += 1;
    }
    expect(toggles).toBeGreaterThan(50);
    expect(refresh).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(refreshShadow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(rt.textInvalidations).toBe(before);
  });
});

describe("W88: re-reading the shadow (D88-3; Review Focus 2)", () => {
  it("given_a_box_shadow_transitionend_on_the_element_when_the_next_frame_runs_then_only_the_shadow_is_re_read_and_the_paint_carries_it", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const child = el.appendChild(document.createElement("span"));
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
    const refresh = vi.spyOn(ElementRegistry.prototype, "refresh");
    const refreshShadow = vi.spyOn(ElementRegistry.prototype, "refreshShadow");
    const before = rt.textInvalidations;
    const later = document.createElement("style");
    later.textContent = ".btn.btn { box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgb(0, 255, 255); }"; // a hover style, emulated
    document.head.appendChild(later);
    child.dispatchEvent(transitionEnd("box-shadow")); // a descendant's: ignored
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(refreshShadow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    el.dispatchEvent(transitionEnd("box-shadow"));
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(refreshShadow).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(refresh, "colours are untouched").not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(rt.textInvalidations, "no atlas re-render").toBe(before);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows).toEqual([GLOW]);
  });

  it("given_an_own_class_change_when_the_next_frame_runs_then_the_shadow_is_re_read_with_the_colours", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
    el.classList.add("alt");
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows).toEqual([GLOW]);
  });

  it("given_an_own_style_change_when_the_next_frame_runs_then_the_shadow_is_re_read", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
    el.style.boxShadow = "0 0 12px rgb(0, 255, 255)";
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows).toEqual([GLOW]);
    el.removeAttribute("style");
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows, "back to the stylesheet's").toEqual([DROP]);
  });

  it("given_a_shadow_change_without_a_transition_or_mutation_when_frames_run_then_the_old_shadow_until_refresh_and_a_refresh_mid_motion_reads_the_author_shadow_not_none_review_focus_2", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.4);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "moving: the class hides the browser's shadow").toBe(true);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
    const hover = document.createElement("style");
    hover.textContent = ".btn.btn { box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgb(0, 255, 255); }";
    document.head.appendChild(hover);
    for (let f = 0; f < 3; f++) clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows, "the old shadow, as the spec says").toEqual([DROP]);
    rt.refresh(el);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(renders.at(-1)?.frame.paints[id]?.shadows, "never stale and never `none` after refresh").toEqual([GLOW]);
    expect(held(el), "the class is back on").toBe(true);
    expect(el.getAttribute("style"), "the lift leaves no inline style").toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("W88: the class on the fail-safe paths (D88-1; Review Focus 1, 3)", () => {
  it("given_the_class_held_when_unobserve_then_destroy_run_then_it_is_gone_each_time", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const a = button(10);
    const b = button(200);
    const ia = rt.observe(a);
    const ib = rt.observe(b);
    await flush();
    moving.setRestAlpha(ia, 0.5);
    moving.setRestAlpha(ib, 0.5);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect([held(a), held(b)]).toEqual([true, true]);
    rt.unobserve(a);
    expect(held(a)).toBe(false);
    rt.destroy();
    expect(held(b)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_the_class_held_when_reduced_motion_switches_on_then_it_goes_at_once_and_the_next_frame_draws_no_shadow", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    // The reduced-motion media query stub of reduced-motion-input.test.ts (jsdom has no matchMedia).
    type Listener = (e: { matches: boolean }) => void;
    const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
    const mql = {
      matches: false,
      media: "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
      addEventListener: (_t: string, cb: Listener) => listeners.add(cb),
      removeEventListener: (_t: string, cb: Listener) => listeners.delete(cb),
    };
    const original = window.matchMedia;
    Object.defineProperty(window, "matchMedia", {
      configurable: true,
      writable: true,
      value: (q: string) => (q.includes("prefers-reduced-motion") ? mql : { ...mql, media: q, addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {} }),
    });
    try {
      const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
      const el = button();
      const id = rt.observe(el);
      await flush();
      moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.5);
      clock.advance(1);
      expect(held(el)).toBe(true);
      const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype);
      mql.matches = true;
      for (const cb of [...listeners]) cb({ matches: true });
      expect(held(el), "no frame needed").toBe(false);
      expect(renders, "no repaint: the next frame draws no shadow").toEqual([]);
      expect(rt.reducedMotion).toBe(true);
      clock.advance(1);
      expect(renders.map((r) => r.frame.reducedMotion)).toEqual([true]);
      expect(held(el), "that frame drew no shadow (reduced motion): still the browser's").toBe(false);
    } finally {
      Object.defineProperty(window, "matchMedia", { configurable: true, writable: true, value: original });
    }
  });

  it("given_the_class_held_when_a_frame_throws_then_the_instance_stops_the_canvas_is_repainted_without_shadows_and_the_class_goes_review_focus_1", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.5);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el)).toBe(true);
    const renders = captureRenders(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype, () => el);
    vi.spyOn(moving.backend.cores[0]!, "tick").mockImplementation(() => {
      throw new Error("boom");
    });
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(held(el)).toBe(false);
    expect(renders.map((r) => r.frame.suppressShadows), "the stop's repaint: the liquid without its shadow").toEqual([true]);
    expect(renders[0]?.held, "drawn while the class was still on").toBe(true);
    expect(rt.shadowLayers(el)).toEqual([DROP, INSET]);
  });

  it("given_the_stop_repaint_itself_throws_when_the_instance_stops_then_the_canvas_bitmap_is_reset_and_still_one_console_error", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start();
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.5);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el)).toBe(true);
    vi.spyOn(FluidCanvas2DRenderer.prototype, "render").mockImplementation(() => {
      throw new Error("render broke");
    });
    const width = vi.spyOn(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, "width", "set");
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(width.mock.contexts.includes(rt.canvas), "the canvas is cleared: no shadow can double").toBe(true);
    expect(held(el)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_webgpu_and_the_class_held_when_the_device_is_lost_then_the_class_goes_at_once_and_returns_under_the_canvas2d_rebuild", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { rt, clock, moving } = await start("webgpu");
    expect(rt.activeRenderer).toBe("webgpu");
    const el = button();
    const id = rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    moving.setRestAlpha(id, 0.5);
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "WebGPU drew the shadow").toBe(true);
    gpu!.lose("unknown", "gpu reset");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(rt.activeRenderer).toBe("canvas2d"));
    expect(held(el), "a lost device: the browser's shadow at once").toBe(false);
    await flush();
    clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "the Canvas2D rebuild draws it again").toBe(true);
  });

  it("given_two_instances_observing_one_element_when_one_moves_then_one_hold_and_the_class_stays_until_the_last_moving_instance_releases_review_focus_3", async () => {
    authorStyles();
    const a = await start();
    const b = await start();
    const el = button();
    const ia = a.rt.observe(el);
    const ib = b.rt.observe(el);
    await flush();
    a.moving.setRestAlpha(ia, 0.5);
    a.clock.advance(1);
    b.clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "a's liquid is away; b's rests").toBe(true);
    b.moving.setRestAlpha(ib, 0.5);
    b.clock.advance(1);
    a.moving.setRestAlpha(ia, null);
    a.clock.advance(1);
    expect(held(el), "b still holds it").toBe(true);
    b.rt.destroy();
    expect(held(el)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```
**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/shadow-canvas2d.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * @vitest-environment jsdom
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-5, D88-10, D88-12, D88-14; Review Focus 4): Canvas2D shadows through
 * FluidCanvas2DRenderer and the shared fake canvas (A6), which records the shadow state and the
 * composite operation of every call. Contexts in creation order: the main canvas, the density
 * buffer (init), then the shadow mask, the spread scratch and the knockout image (first shadow).
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { Dyn, DYNAMIC_FIELDS, ELEMENT_STRIDE, FLAG_PARKED, Interaction, St, STATE_STRIDE, Stat, STATIC_FIELDS } from "../src/fluid-layout";
import { FluidCanvas2DRenderer } from "../src/renderers/fluid-canvas2d";
import type { ElementPaint, RenderFrame } from "../src/renderers/frame";
import { kernelRadiusPx } from "../src/renderers/kernel-params";
import { KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL } from "../src/renderers/shadow-plan";
import { parseBoxShadow, type ShadowLayer } from "../src/shadow";
import { installFakeCanvas2D, type FakeCall, type FakeCanvasHandle, type FakeContext2D } from "./_fake-canvas";

const CAP = 700; // 70 × 10 particles on a 2 px lattice inside (100, 100, 140, 48)
const BLUE = [47, 111, 222, 1] as const;
const R = kernelRadiusPx(2); // 4.6 px; the density cell stays 2 px
const CELL = 2;
const MIN_X = 101;
const MIN_Y = 101;
const DROP = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px");

let fake: FakeCanvasHandle;
beforeEach(() => {
  fake = installFakeCanvas2D();
});
afterEach(() => {
  fake.restore();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

function paint(id: number, shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[] = DROP): ElementPaint {
  return { id, background: BLUE, text: [255, 255, 255, 1], radiusPx: 24, particleCount: CAP, areaPerParticle: 4, spacingPx: 2, atlasRect: null, shadows };
}

interface FrameOpts {
  restAlpha?: number;
  paints?: Array<ElementPaint | undefined>;
  homes?: (i: number) => number;
  /** Particles removed (their home set to −1) — e.g. a hole. */
  drop?: (x: number, y: number) => boolean;
  flags?: number;
  interaction?: number;
  w?: number;
  reducedMotion?: boolean;
  suppressShadows?: boolean;
  viewport?: { widthCss: number; heightCss: number; dpr: number };
}

function frame(o: FrameOpts = {}): RenderFrame {
  const dynamicView = new Float32Array(CAP * DYNAMIC_FIELDS);
  const staticView = new Float32Array(CAP * STATIC_FIELDS);
  const stateView = new Float32Array(4 * STATE_STRIDE);
  const elementView = new Float32Array(4 * ELEMENT_STRIDE);
  elementView.set([100, 100, o.w ?? 140, 48, 24, o.interaction ?? Interaction.IDLE, 0, 0, NaN, NaN], 0);
  elementView.set([300, 100, 60, 40, 8, Interaction.IDLE, 0, 0, NaN, NaN], ELEMENT_STRIDE);
  for (let i = 0; i < CAP; i++) {
    const x = MIN_X + 2 * (i % 70);
    const y = MIN_Y + 2 * Math.floor(i / 70);
    dynamicView[Dyn.X * CAP + i] = x;
    dynamicView[Dyn.Y * CAP + i] = y;
    dynamicView[Dyn.F00 * CAP + i] = 1;
    dynamicView[Dyn.F11 * CAP + i] = 1;
    dynamicView[Dyn.FLAGS * CAP + i] = o.flags ?? 0;
    staticView[Stat.HOME * CAP + i] = o.drop?.(x, y) ? -1 : o.homes ? o.homes(i) : 0;
  }
  stateView[St.REST_ALPHA] = o.restAlpha ?? 0;
  stateView[STATE_STRIDE + St.REST_ALPHA] = o.restAlpha ?? 0;
  return {
    dynamicView,
    staticView,
    generation: 1,
    stateView,
    elementView,
    particleCapacity: CAP,
    activeParticles: CAP,
    paints: o.paints ?? [paint(0)],
    viewport: o.viewport ?? { widthCss: 400, heightCss: 300, dpr: 1 },
    reducedMotion: o.reducedMotion ?? false,
    ...(o.suppressShadows === undefined ? {} : { suppressShadows: o.suppressShadows }),
  };
}

async function setup(widthCss = 400, heightCss = 300, dpr = 1): Promise<{ renderer: FluidCanvas2DRenderer; ctx: FakeContext2D; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement }> {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  const renderer = new FluidCanvas2DRenderer();
  await renderer.init(canvas);
  renderer.resize(widthCss * dpr, heightCss * dpr, dpr);
  return { renderer, ctx: fake.ctxFor(canvas)!, canvas };
}

const shadowDraws = (ctx: FakeContext2D): FakeCall[] => ctx.ops("drawImage").filter((c) => c.shadowColor !== "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)");
const knockoutDraws = (ctx: FakeContext2D): FakeCall[] => ctx.ops("drawImage").filter((c) => c.composite === "destination-out");
/** The density image: the one drawImage with five arguments (the off canvas, 0, 0, w, h). */
const densityIndex = (ctx: FakeContext2D): number => ctx.calls.findIndex((c) => c.op === "drawImage" && c.args.length === 5 && c.composite === "source-over");
/** The mask's CSS origin: the caster's particle bounds grown by the kernel radius, floored to the cell. */
const MASK_X = Math.floor((MIN_X - R) / CELL) * CELL;
const MASK_Y = Math.floor((MIN_Y - R) / CELL) * CELL;

describe("W88: Canvas2D shadows (D88-4, D88-5, D88-14)", () => {
  it("given_a_moving_element_with_one_layer_when_rendered_then_its_shadow_is_drawn_before_the_density_image_with_the_css_colour_blur_and_offset", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
    renderer.render(frame());
    const draws = shadowDraws(ctx);
    expect(draws).toHaveLength(1);
    const d = draws[0]!;
    expect(d.shadowColor).toBe("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)");
    expect(d.shadowBlur).toBe(20);
    expect(d.shadowOffsetY).toBe(8);
    expect(d.composite).toBe("source-over");
    expect(ctx.calls.indexOf(d), "D88-4: before all liquid").toBeLessThan(densityIndex(ctx));
    expect(densityIndex(ctx)).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    expect(ctx.shadowColor, "the shadow state is reset after the shadows").toBe("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)");
    expect([ctx.shadowBlur, ctx.shadowOffsetX, ctx.shadowOffsetY]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
    expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("given_the_mask_drawn_off_the_canvas_when_rendered_then_the_source_lies_left_of_x_0_and_the_offset_brings_the_shadow_back_to_the_mask_D88_5", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
    renderer.render(frame());
    const d = shadowDraws(ctx)[0]!;
    const [, , , , , dx, dy, dw, dh] = d.args as number[];
    expect(dx! + dw!, "the source's right edge is off the canvas").toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(dx! + d.shadowOffsetX, "the shadow lands on the mask's own place").toBeCloseTo(MASK_X, 6);
    expect(dy).toBeCloseTo(MASK_Y, 6);
    expect(dw! % CELL).toBe(0);
    expect(dh! % CELL).toBe(0);
  });

  it("given_dpr_2_when_rendered_then_shadowBlur_and_both_offsets_including_the_off_canvas_compensation_are_scaled_by_the_dpr_D88_14", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup(400, 300, 2);
    renderer.render(frame({ viewport: { widthCss: 400, heightCss: 300, dpr: 2 } }));
    const d = shadowDraws(ctx)[0]!;
    const dx = (d.args as number[])[5]!;
    expect(d.shadowBlur).toBe(40);
    expect(d.shadowOffsetY).toBe(16);
    expect(dx * 2 + d.shadowOffsetX, "device px: the destination is transformed, the offset is not").toBeCloseTo(MASK_X * 2, 6);
    expect(ctx.ops("setTransform")[0]!.args).toEqual([2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0]);
  });

  it("given_three_layers_when_rendered_then_one_shadow_draw_per_layer_with_the_last_css_layer_first", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
    const layers = parseBoxShadow("rgb(255, 0, 0) 0px 0px 4px 0px, rgb(0, 255, 0) 0px 2px 8px 0px, rgb(0, 0, 255) 0px 4px 16px 0px");
    renderer.render(frame({ paints: [paint(0, layers)] }));
    expect(shadowDraws(ctx).map((c) => [c.shadowColor, c.shadowBlur])).toEqual([
      ["rgba(0, 0, 255, 1)", 16],
      ["rgba(0, 255, 0, 1)", 8],
      ["rgba(255, 0, 0, 1)", 4],
    ]);
  });

  it("given_a_spread_layer_with_blur_0_when_rendered_then_an_8_direction_dilation_and_a_shadowBlur_floor_of_one_cell_D88_5_D88_12", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
    renderer.render(frame({ paints: [paint(0, parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 0px 4px 0px 6px"))] }));
    const spread = fake.contexts[3]!;
    const copies = spread.ops("drawImage");
    expect(copies, "the centre and 8 shifted copies").toHaveLength(9);
    expect(copies.every((c) => c.composite === "source-over")).toBe(true);
    const offsets = copies.slice(1).map((c) => [(c.args[5] as number) - (copies[0]!.args[5] as number), (c.args[6] as number) - (copies[0]!.args[6] as number)]);
    for (const [ox, oy] of offsets) expect(Math.hypot(ox!, oy!), "each copy is shifted by the spread in cells").toBeCloseTo(6 / CELL, 6);
    const d = shadowDraws(ctx)[0]!;
    expect(d.args[0], "the dilated mask casts the shadow").toBe(spread.canvas);
    expect(d.shadowBlur, "D88-12: one density cell").toBe(CELL);
  });

  it("given_a_negative_spread_when_rendered_then_the_8_shifted_copies_erode_the_mask_with_destination_in", async () => {
    const { renderer } = await setup();
    renderer.render(frame({ paints: [paint(0, parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 0px 6px 10px -4px"))] }));
    const copies = fake.contexts[3]!.ops("drawImage");
    expect(copies[0]!.composite).toBe("source-over");
    expect(copies.slice(1).map((c) => c.composite)).toEqual(Array(8).fill("destination-in"));
  });
});

describe("W88: the Canvas2D knockout (D88-10)", () => {
  it("given_shadows_when_rendered_then_one_destination_out_draw_of_the_knockout_image_follows_the_shadows_and_precedes_the_density_image", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
    renderer.render(frame());
    const knock = knockoutDraws(ctx);
    expect(knock).toHaveLength(1);
    const k = ctx.calls.indexOf(knock[0]!);
    expect(k).toBeGreaterThan(ctx.calls.indexOf(shadowDraws(ctx)[0]!));
    expect(k).toBeLessThan(densityIndex(ctx));
    expect(knock[0]!.args.slice(1)).toEqual([0, 0, 400, 300]);
    expect(knock[0]!.shadowColor, "the knockout casts no shadow").toBe("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)");
    expect(ctx.globalCompositeOperation).toBe("source-over");
    expect(fake.contexts[4]!.ops("putImageData")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("given_a_hole_in_the_liquid_when_rendered_then_the_mask_shows_the_hole_and_the_knockout_closes_it_while_the_page_beside_the_liquid_stays_clear", async () => {
    expect(KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL).toBe(1.5);
    const { renderer } = await setup();
    const hole = (x: number, y: number): boolean => Math.hypot(x - 171, y - 111) < 4.5;
    renderer.render(frame({ drop: hole }));
    const mask = fake.contexts[2]!.lastImage!;
    const knock = fake.contexts[4]!.lastImage!;
    const maskAlpha = (cx: number, cy: number): number => mask.data[((Math.floor(cy / CELL) - MASK_Y / CELL) * mask.width + (Math.floor(cx / CELL) - MASK_X / CELL)) * 4 + 3]!;
    const knockAlpha = (cx: number, cy: number): number => knock.data[(Math.floor(cy / CELL) * knock.width + Math.floor(cx / CELL)) * 4 + 3]!;
    expect(maskAlpha(171, 111), "the hole is in the liquid's own coverage").toBeLessThan(64);
    expect(maskAlpha(140, 111), "the liquid beside it").toBe(255);
    expect(knockAlpha(171, 111), "the closed coverage covers the hole").toBeGreaterThan(200);
    expect(knockAlpha(140, 111)).toBe(255);
    expect(knockAlpha(80, 111), "20 px beside the liquid the shadow stays").toBe(0);
    expect(knockAlpha(171, 130), "below the liquid (where the drop shadow falls) too").toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("W88: what casts no Canvas2D shadow (D88-4) and shadowsDrawn", () => {
  it("given_rest_parked_inactive_reduced_motion_suppressed_or_no_layers_when_rendered_then_no_shadow_draw_no_knockout_and_shadowsDrawn_false", async () => {
    const cases: Array<[string, FrameOpts]> = [
      ["at rest", { restAlpha: 1 }],
      ["parked", { flags: FLAG_PARKED }],
      ["inactive (w == 0)", { w: 0 }],
      ["reduced motion", { reducedMotion: true }],
      ["the stop repaint", { suppressShadows: true }],
      ["no layers", { paints: [paint(0, [])] }],
    ];
    for (const [name, o] of cases) {
      const { renderer, ctx } = await setup();
      renderer.render(frame(o));
      expect(shadowDraws(ctx), name).toEqual([]);
      expect(knockoutDraws(ctx), name).toEqual([]);
      expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0), name).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it("given_two_elements_and_a_dragged_one_at_rest_when_rendered_then_shadowsDrawn_is_true_exactly_for_the_casters_of_the_last_render", async () => {
    const { renderer } = await setup();
    const homes = (i: number): number => (i < 350 ? 0 : 1);
    renderer.render(frame({ homes, paints: [paint(0), paint(1, [])] }));
    expect([renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0), renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(1), renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(7)]).toEqual([true, false, false]);
    renderer.render(frame({ homes, restAlpha: 1, paints: [paint(0), paint(1, [])] }));
    expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0), "at rest the browser draws it").toBe(false);
    renderer.render(frame({ homes, restAlpha: 1, interaction: Interaction.DRAGGED, paints: [paint(0), paint(1, [])] }));
    expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0), "D88-1: dragged counts as away").toBe(true);
    renderer.destroy();
    expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_a_small_container_canvas_and_a_large_blur_partly_outside_it_when_rendered_then_no_throw_and_the_shadow_is_drawn_for_the_canvas_to_clip_review_focus_4", async () => {
    const { renderer, ctx } = await setup(120, 80, 2);
    const big = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) 30px 40px 60px 12px");
    expect(() => renderer.render(frame({ paints: [paint(0, big)], viewport: { widthCss: 120, heightCss: 80, dpr: 2 } }))).not.toThrow();
    expect(shadowDraws(ctx)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(knockoutDraws(ctx)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(renderer.shadowsDrawn?.(0)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```
