**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/shadow-plan.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-6, D88-11): the shadow casters both renderers share, and the WebGPU plan: one
 * density source per element and distinct spread, exact radius groups (half resolution, quarter for
 * blur ≥ 32 px), the bucket overflow past six radii, the spread kernel, the source cap.
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { Dyn, DYNAMIC_FIELDS, ELEMENT_STRIDE, FLAG_PARKED, Interaction, St, STATE_STRIDE, Stat, STATIC_FIELDS } from "../src/fluid-layout";
import type { ElementPaint, RenderFrame } from "../src/renderers/frame";
import {
  nearestBucket,
  planShadows,
  SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS,
  SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES,
  SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX,
  SHADOW_SCALE_HALF,
  SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER,
  ShadowCasters,
  spreadKernel,
} from "../src/renderers/shadow-plan";
import { parseBoxShadow, type ShadowLayer } from "../src/shadow";

const PER = 20; // particles per element: a 10 × 2 block on a 4 px lattice
const paint = (id: number, shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[]): ElementPaint => ({
  id, background: [47, 111, 222, 1], text: [255, 255, 255, 1], radiusPx: 8, particleCount: PER, areaPerParticle: 16, spacingPx: 4, atlasRect: null, shadows,
});

/** `paints.length` elements side by side, every one moving (restAlpha 0) unless `rest` names it. */
function frame(paints: Array<ElementPaint | undefined>, o: { rest?: number[]; parked?: number[]; w0?: number[]; dragged?: number[] } = {}): RenderFrame {
  const n = paints.length;
  const cap = PER * n;
  const dynamicView = new Float32Array(cap * DYNAMIC_FIELDS);
  const staticView = new Float32Array(cap * STATIC_FIELDS);
  const stateView = new Float32Array(n * STATE_STRIDE);
  const elementView = new Float32Array(n * ELEMENT_STRIDE);
  for (let id = 0; id < n; id++) {
    elementView.set([100 + 60 * id, 100, 40, 8, 4, o.dragged?.includes(id) ? Interaction.DRAGGED : Interaction.IDLE, 0, 0, NaN, NaN], id * ELEMENT_STRIDE);
    if (o.w0?.includes(id)) elementView[id * ELEMENT_STRIDE + 2] = 0;
    stateView[id * STATE_STRIDE + St.REST_ALPHA] = o.rest?.includes(id) ? 1 : 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < PER; k++) {
      const i = id * PER + k;
      dynamicView[Dyn.X * cap + i] = 102 + 60 * id + 4 * (k % 10);
      dynamicView[Dyn.Y * cap + i] = 102 + 4 * Math.floor(k / 10);
      dynamicView[Dyn.FLAGS * cap + i] = o.parked?.includes(id) ? FLAG_PARKED : 0;
      staticView[Stat.HOME * cap + i] = id;
    }
  }
  return {
    dynamicView, staticView, generation: 1, stateView, elementView, particleCapacity: cap, activeParticles: cap,
    paints, viewport: { widthCss: 800, heightCss: 600, dpr: 2 }, reducedMotion: false,
  };
}

const L = (css: string): readonly ShadowLayer[] => parseBoxShadow(css);
const TEXEL = 1; // CSS px of one half-resolution texel at dpr 2

describe("W88: ShadowCasters (D88-4)", () => {
  it("given_moving_resting_parked_inactive_dragged_and_shadowless_elements_when_collected_then_only_moving_or_dragged_active_elements_with_layers_with_their_particles_and_bounds", () => {
    const drop = L("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px");
    const f = frame([paint(0, drop), paint(1, drop), paint(2, drop), paint(3, drop), paint(4, []), paint(5, drop), undefined], {
      rest: [1, 5], parked: [2], w0: [3], dragged: [5],
    });
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    expect(c.collect(f)).toBe(2);
    expect(c.ids, "0 moves, 5 is dragged at rest; 1 rests, 2 is parked, 3 inactive, 4 has no layers, 6 no paint").toEqual([0, 5]);
    expect(Array.from(c.bounds.subarray(0, 4))).toEqual([102, 102, 138, 106]);
    expect(c.count[0]).toBe(PER);
    const mine = Array.from(c.order.subarray(c.start[0]!, c.start[0]! + c.count[0]!));
    expect(mine).toEqual(Array.from({ length: PER }, (_, k) => k));
    expect(c.collect({ ...f, reducedMotion: true }), "reduced motion").toBe(0);
    expect(c.collect({ ...f, suppressShadows: true }), "the stop repaint").toBe(0);
    expect(c.ids).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("W88: the WebGPU shadow plan (D88-6, D88-11)", () => {
  it("given_layers_with_three_distinct_blurs_on_two_elements_when_planned_then_three_exact_groups_one_record_per_layer_last_css_layer_first_and_offsets_in_the_records", () => {
    const f = frame([
      paint(0, L("rgb(0, 255, 255) 0px 0px 12px 0px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px")),
      paint(1, L("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 2px 8px 20px 0px, rgb(255, 0, 0) 0px 0px 5px 0px")),
    ]);
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    c.collect(f);
    const plan = planShadows(c, f, TEXEL);
    expect(plan.drawn).toEqual([0, 1]);
    expect(plan.groups.map((g) => [g.blur, g.scale, g.count])).toEqual([[20, SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, 2], [12, SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, 1], [5, SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, 1]]);
    const g20 = plan.groups[0]!;
    const recs = plan.records.slice(g20.first, g20.first + g20.count);
    expect(recs.map((r) => [plan.sources[r.source]!.id, r.dx, r.dy])).toEqual([[0, 0, 8], [1, 2, 8]]);
    expect(plan.records[0]!.color).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0.4]);
    expect(plan.sources.map((s) => [s.id, s.spread, s.layer, s.channel])).toEqual([[0, 0, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0, 1]]);
    expect(plan.densityLayers).toBe(1);
    const src = plan.sources[0]!;
    expect(src.rect).toEqual({ x0: 102 - src.radius, y0: 102 - src.radius, x1: 138 + src.radius, y1: 106 + src.radius });
    expect(plan.records[0]!.rect).toEqual({ x0: src.rect.x0, y0: src.rect.y0 + 8, x1: src.rect.x1, y1: src.rect.y1 + 8 });
    expect(g20.reach.y1, "3σ = 1.5 × blur beyond the lowest record").toBeCloseTo(src.rect.y1 + 8 + 30, 6);
  });

  it("given_a_blur_at_or_above_32_px_when_planned_then_its_group_uses_the_quarter_resolution_targets", () => {
    expect(SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX).toBe(32);
    const f = frame([paint(0, L("rgb(0, 255, 255) 0px 0px 32px 8px, rgb(0, 255, 255) 0px 0px 31.5px 0px"))]);
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    c.collect(f);
    expect(planShadows(c, f, TEXEL).groups.map((g) => [g.blur, g.scale])).toEqual([[31.5, SHADOW_SCALE_HALF], [32, SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER]]);
  });

  it("given_eight_distinct_blurs_when_planned_then_the_first_six_stay_exact_and_the_rest_join_their_log_nearest_bucket_D88_6", () => {
    expect(SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS).toBe(6);
    expect([1, 5, 6, 11, 12, 20, 40, 100].map(nearestBucket)).toEqual([4, 4, 8, 8, 16, 16, 32, 64]);
    const blurs = [3, 5, 7, 9, 11, 13, 15, 30];
    const css = blurs.map((b) => `rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 0px ${b}px 0px`).join(", ");
    const f = frame([paint(0, L(css).slice(0, 4)), paint(1, L(css).slice(4))]);
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    c.collect(f);
    const keys = planShadows(c, f, TEXEL).groups.map((g) => g.blur);
    // Records go last layer first per element: 0 → 9, 7, 5, 3; 1 → 30, 15, then 13 and 11 overflow.
    expect(keys).toEqual([9, 7, 5, 3, 30, 15, 16, 8]);
  });

  it("given_layers_with_different_spreads_on_one_element_when_planned_then_one_density_source_per_distinct_spread_with_spread_0_first", () => {
    const f = frame([paint(0, L("rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 4px 0px 6px, rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 0px 8px 6px, rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 0px 2px -2px"))]);
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    c.collect(f);
    const plan = planShadows(c, f, TEXEL);
    expect(plan.sources.map((s) => s.spread)).toEqual([0, 6, -2]);
    expect(plan.records.map((r) => plan.sources[r.source]!.spread)).toEqual([-2, 6, 6]);
    expect(plan.sources[1]!.radius).toBeCloseTo(plan.sources[0]!.radius + 6, 9);
  });

  it("given_more_density_sources_than_four_layers_hold_when_planned_then_whole_elements_that_do_not_fit_are_not_drawn", () => {
    expect(SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES).toBe(16);
    const three = L("rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 0px 4px 2px, rgb(0, 0, 0) 0px 0px 4px 4px"); // spreads 0 (implied), 2, 4
    const f = frame(Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, id) => paint(id, three)));
    const c = new ShadowCasters();
    c.collect(f);
    const plan = planShadows(c, f, TEXEL);
    expect(plan.drawn, "5 × 3 sources fit in 16, a sixth element does not").toEqual([0, 1, 2, 3, 4]);
    expect(plan.sources).toHaveLength(15);
    expect(plan.densityLayers).toBe(4);
  });

  it("given_a_spread_when_its_kernel_is_widened_and_mass_scaled_then_a_half_plane_of_particles_reads_density_0_5_at_the_spread_distance", () => {
    const R = 7;
    for (const s of [6, 12, -3]) {
      const k = spreadKernel(R, s);
      expect(k.radius).toBe(R + Math.abs(s));
      // Particles on a 0.5 px lattice fill x < 0 (mass = h²): the density at x = s, y = 0.
      const h = 0.5;
      const Rp = k.radius;
      let rho = 0;
      for (let x = -h / 2; x > s - Rp - 1; x -= h) {
        for (let y = -Rp - 1; y <= Rp + 1; y += h) {
          const q = 1 - ((s - x) ** 2 + y * y) / (Rp * Rp);
          if (q > 0) rho += (h * h * k.weightScale * q * q) / ((Math.PI * Rp * Rp) / 3);
        }
      }
      expect(rho, `spread ${s}`).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 1);
    }
    expect(spreadKernel(R, 0)).toEqual({ radius: R, weightScale: 1 });
  });
});
```

**`packages/core/ts/__tests__/webgpu-shadows.test.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * @vitest-environment jsdom
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-6, D88-10, D88-11, D88-13; Review Focus 4): the WebGPU shadow passes against the
 * fake GPU (_fake-gpu.ts, which records pass labels, scissor rects and dynamic offsets and flags a
 * scissor outside its attachment or an offset off the 256-byte alignment). The canvas backing is
 * 800 × 600 at dpr 2 (400 × 300 CSS): half resolution 400 × 300, quarter 200 × 150.
 */
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { Dyn, DYNAMIC_FIELDS, ELEMENT_STRIDE, Interaction, St, STATE_STRIDE, Stat, STATIC_FIELDS } from "../src/fluid-layout";
import type { ElementPaint, RenderFrame } from "../src/renderers/frame";
import { WebGPURenderer } from "../src/renderers/webgpu/webgpu-renderer";
import { SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS, SP } from "../src/renderers/webgpu/shadow-passes";
import { parseBoxShadow, type ShadowLayer } from "../src/shadow";
import { installFakeGpu, type FakeGpu, type FakePass, type FakePipeline } from "./_fake-gpu";

let fake: FakeGpu | null = null;
afterEach(() => {
  fake?.restore();
  fake = null;
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

const PER = 6;
const DROP = parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px");
const paint = (id: number, shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[] = DROP): ElementPaint => ({
  id, background: [47, 111, 222, 1], text: [255, 255, 255, 1], radiusPx: 24, particleCount: PER, areaPerParticle: 4, spacingPx: 2, atlasRect: null, shadows,
});

/** Two slots, 6 particles each (slot 0 at x 110…130, slot 1 at x 210…230, y 120); restAlpha 0 unless `rest`. */
function frame(paints: ReadonlyArray<ElementPaint | undefined>, o: { rest?: boolean; dragged?: boolean; x0?: number; suppressShadows?: boolean } = {}): RenderFrame {
  const cap = 2 * PER;
  const dynamicView = new Float32Array(cap * DYNAMIC_FIELDS);
  const staticView = new Float32Array(cap * STATIC_FIELDS);
  for (let i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
    const id = i < PER ? 0 : 1;
    dynamicView[Dyn.X * cap + i] = (o.x0 ?? 110) + 100 * id + 4 * (i % PER);
    dynamicView[Dyn.Y * cap + i] = 120;
    staticView[Stat.HOME * cap + i] = id;
  }
  const elementView = new Float32Array(2 * ELEMENT_STRIDE);
  elementView.set([100, 100, 140, 48, 24, o.dragged ? Interaction.DRAGGED : Interaction.IDLE, 0, 0, NaN, NaN], 0);
  elementView.set([200, 100, 140, 48, 24, Interaction.IDLE, 0, 0, NaN, NaN], ELEMENT_STRIDE);
  const stateView = new Float32Array(2 * STATE_STRIDE);
  stateView[St.REST_ALPHA] = o.rest ? 1 : 0;
  stateView[STATE_STRIDE + St.REST_ALPHA] = o.rest ? 1 : 0;
  return {
    dynamicView, staticView, generation: 1, stateView, elementView, particleCapacity: cap, activeParticles: cap,
    paints, viewport: { widthCss: 400, heightCss: 300, dpr: 2 }, reducedMotion: false, textAtlas: null,
    ...(o.suppressShadows === undefined ? {} : { suppressShadows: o.suppressShadows }),
  };
}

async function ready(): Promise<{ r: WebGPURenderer; f: FakeGpu }> {
  const f = installFakeGpu();
  fake = f;
  const r = new WebGPURenderer();
  await r.init(document.createElement("canvas"));
  r.resize(800, 600, 2);
  return { r, f };
}

const label = (p: unknown): string => String((p as FakePipeline | null)?.descriptor.label ?? "");
const passLabels = (f: FakeGpu, from = 0): string[] => f.calls.passes.slice(from).map((p) => String(p.label ?? ""));
const screenOf = (f: FakeGpu): FakePass => f.calls.passes.at(-1)!;
const shadowTextures = (f: FakeGpu) => f.calls.textures.filter((t) => String(t.label).startsWith("liquiddom shadow"));
const OVER = {
  color: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one-minus-src-alpha", operation: "add" },
  alpha: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one-minus-src-alpha", operation: "add" },
};
const ADDITIVE = { color: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one", operation: "add" }, alpha: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one", operation: "add" } };

describe("W88: WebGPU shadow pipelines (D88-11, D88-13)", () => {
  it("given_init_when_built_then_six_shadow_pipelines_in_the_liquids_validation_scope_and_the_liquid_composite_blends_over", async () => {
    const { f } = await ready();
    const byLabel = new Map(f.calls.pipelines.map((p) => [String(p.descriptor.label), p.descriptor]));
    const targets = (name: string) => byLabel.get(name)?.fragment?.targets;
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow density")).toEqual([{ format: "rgba16float", blend: ADDITIVE }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow resolve")).toEqual([{ format: "rgba16float", blend: OVER }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow blur h")).toEqual([{ format: "rgba16float" }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow blur v")).toEqual([{ format: "rgba16float", blend: OVER }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow knockout")).toEqual([{ format: "r8unorm" }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom shadow composite")).toEqual([{ format: "bgra8unorm", blend: OVER }]);
    expect(targets("liquiddom composite"), "D88-13").toEqual([{ format: "bgra8unorm", blend: OVER }]);
    const log = f.calls.log;
    expect(log.lastIndexOf("createRenderPipeline")).toBeLessThan(log.lastIndexOf("popErrorScope"));
    expect(shadowTextures(f), "no shadow target before the first shadow").toEqual([]);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("W88: WebGPU shadow passes (D88-6, D88-11)", () => {
  it("given_one_moving_element_with_one_layer_when_rendering_then_density_resolve_blur_h_blur_v_and_knockout_run_between_the_splat_and_the_screen_pass_whose_first_draw_is_the_shadow_composite", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    expect(passLabels(f)).toEqual([
      "liquiddom splat",
      "liquiddom shadow density 0",
      "liquiddom shadow resolve",
      "liquiddom shadow blur h",
      "liquiddom shadow blur v",
      "liquiddom shadow knockout",
      "liquiddom screen",
    ]);
    const [, density, resolve, h, v, knock] = f.calls.passes;
    expect(density!.draws.map((d) => [label(d.pipeline), d.vertexCount, d.instanceCount, d.offsets])).toEqual([["liquiddom shadow density", 6, 2 * PER, [0]]]);
    expect(resolve!.draws.map((d) => [label(d.pipeline), d.vertexCount, d.instanceCount, d.firstInstance])).toEqual([["liquiddom shadow resolve", 6, 1, 0]]);
    expect(h!.draws.map((d) => [label(d.pipeline), d.offsets])).toEqual([["liquiddom shadow blur h", [256]]]);
    expect(v!.draws.map((d) => [label(d.pipeline), d.offsets])).toEqual([["liquiddom shadow blur v", [512]]]);
    expect(knock!.draws.map((d) => [label(d.pipeline), d.offsets])).toEqual([["liquiddom shadow knockout", [768]]]);
    expect(screenOf(f).draws.map((d) => label(d.pipeline)).slice(0, 2)).toEqual(["liquiddom shadow composite", "liquiddom composite"]);
    expect(screenOf(f).draws[0]!.offsets).toEqual([1024]);
    expect(density!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.format).toBe("rgba16float");
    expect([density!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.width, density!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.height], "half resolution").toEqual([400, 300]);
    expect(v!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.label).toBe("liquiddom shadow acc half");
    expect(knock!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.format).toBe("r8unorm");
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0)).toBe(true);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("given_the_blur_pass_records_when_written_then_sigma_is_blur_over_2_in_target_px_and_the_taps_reach_3_sigma", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    const params = f.calls.writes.filter((w) => w.buffer.label === "liquiddom shadow params")[0]!;
    const slot = (s: number, k: number): number => params.values[s * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS + k]!;
    // Slot 0 is the density source; 1 and 2 the blur: σ = 20 / 2 CSS px × dpr 2 × 0.5 = 10 px.
    expect([slot(1, SP.DIR_X), slot(1, SP.DIR_Y), slot(2, SP.DIR_X), slot(2, SP.DIR_Y)]).toEqual([1, 0, 0, 1]);
    expect(slot(1, SP.SIGMA)).toBeCloseTo(10, 6);
    expect(slot(1, SP.TAPS)).toBe(30);
    expect(slot(0, SP.ELEMENT)).toBe(0);
    expect([slot(0, SP.MASK), slot(0, SP.MASK + 1), slot(0, SP.MASK + 2), slot(0, SP.MASK + 3)]).toEqual([1, 0, 0, 0]);
  });

  it("given_two_elements_with_the_same_blur_when_rendering_then_one_resolve_pass_draws_both_quads", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0), paint(1)]));
    const resolves = f.calls.passes.filter((p) => p.label === "liquiddom shadow resolve");
    expect(resolves).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(resolves[0]!.draws.map((d) => d.instanceCount)).toEqual([2]);
    const density = f.calls.passes.find((p) => p.label === "liquiddom shadow density 0")!;
    expect(density.draws.map((d) => d.offsets[0]), "one draw per source, its own slot").toEqual([0, 256]);
    expect(density.draws.every((d) => d.scissor !== null)).toBe(true);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("given_a_blur_of_40_px_when_rendering_then_its_resolve_blur_and_accumulator_targets_are_quarter_resolution", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0, parseBoxShadow("rgb(0, 255, 255) 0px 0px 40px 0px")), undefined]));
    for (const name of ["liquiddom shadow resolve", "liquiddom shadow blur h", "liquiddom shadow blur v"]) {
      const t = f.calls.passes.find((p) => p.label === name)!.attachments[0]!.view.texture;
      expect([t.width, t.height], name).toEqual([200, 150]);
    }
    expect(f.calls.passes.find((p) => p.label === "liquiddom shadow blur v")!.attachments[0]!.view.texture.label).toBe("liquiddom shadow acc quarter");
    const composite = f.calls.writes.filter((w) => w.buffer.label === "liquiddom shadow params").at(-1)!;
    expect(composite.values[SP.FLAGS], "the composite reads the quarter accumulator only").toBe(2);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("given_a_spread_layer_when_rendering_then_its_own_density_source_and_a_knockout_mask_on_the_spread_0_channel_only", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0, parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3) 0px 4px 0px 6px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 8px 20px 0px")), undefined]));
    const density = f.calls.passes.find((p) => p.label === "liquiddom shadow density 0")!;
    expect(density.draws).toHaveLength(2);
    const params = f.calls.writes.filter((w) => w.buffer.label === "liquiddom shadow params")[0]!;
    // Slots: 2 sources, 2 blur (blur 20; the blur-0 spread group has none), knockout, composite.
    const knock = 4 * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS;
    expect(Array.from(params.values.slice(knock + SP.MASKS, knock + SP.MASKS + 4))).toEqual([1, 0, 0, 0]);
    expect(params.values[knock + SP.LAYERS]).toBe(1);
    expect(params.values[knock + SP.CLOSE], "1.5 × kernel radius 4.6 CSS px × dpr 2 × 0.5").toBe(7);
    const resolves = f.calls.passes.filter((p) => p.label === "liquiddom shadow resolve");
    expect(resolves, "one resolve per radius group (20 and 0)").toHaveLength(2);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("given_an_element_at_the_canvas_edge_with_a_far_offset_and_a_large_blur_when_rendering_then_every_scissor_is_clamped_inside_its_attachment_review_focus_4", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0, parseBoxShadow("rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) 120px 90px 64px 10px")), undefined], { x0: 380 }));
    const scissors = f.calls.passes.flatMap((p) => p.scissors.map((s) => ({ s, t: p.attachments[0]!.view.texture })));
    expect(scissors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(scissors.some(({ s, t }) => s[0] + s[2] === t.width), "a rect reaching past the right edge is cut at it").toBe(true);
    expect(f.calls.errors, "the fake flags every scissor outside its attachment").toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("W88: what costs nothing, shadowsDrawn and the targets' life (D88-4, D88-13)", () => {
  it("given_a_moving_element_without_shadows_or_a_resting_one_when_rendering_then_no_shadow_pass_texture_or_buffer_and_the_screen_pass_starts_with_the_liquid_composite", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0, []), undefined]));
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined], { rest: true }));
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined], { suppressShadows: true }));
    expect(passLabels(f)).toEqual(["liquiddom splat", "liquiddom screen", "liquiddom splat", "liquiddom screen", "liquiddom splat", "liquiddom screen"]);
    expect(shadowTextures(f)).toEqual([]);
    expect(f.calls.buffers.filter((b) => String(b.label).startsWith("liquiddom shadow"))).toEqual([]);
    expect(label(screenOf(f).draws[0]!.pipeline)).toBe("liquiddom composite");
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("given_a_dragged_element_at_restAlpha_1_when_rendering_then_its_shadow_passes_run_D88_1", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined], { rest: true, dragged: true }));
    expect(passLabels(f)).toContain("liquiddom shadow knockout");
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("given_shadows_drawn_when_resized_lost_or_destroyed_then_the_targets_are_rebuilt_at_the_new_size_shadowsDrawn_turns_false_and_nothing_is_left_or_flagged", async () => {
    const { r, f } = await ready();
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    const first = shadowTextures(f);
    expect(first.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    r.resize(1000, 600, 2);
    expect(first.every((t) => t.destroyed), "a resize drops the targets").toBe(true);
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    expect(shadowTextures(f).find((t) => t.label === "liquiddom shadow density" && !t.destroyed)!.width).toBe(500);
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0)).toBe(true);
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined], { rest: true }));
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0), "a frame without the element's shadow").toBe(false);
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    f.loseDevice("gpu reset");
    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));
    r.render(frame([paint(0), undefined]));
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0), "a lost device draws nothing").toBe(false);
    r.destroy();
    expect(shadowTextures(f).every((t) => t.destroyed)).toBe(true);
    expect(f.calls.buffers.filter((b) => String(b.label).startsWith("liquiddom shadow")).every((b) => b.destroyed)).toBe(true);
    expect(r.shadowsDrawn(0)).toBe(false);
    expect(f.calls.errors).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```diff
+describe("W88: the shadow toggle class (D88-1)", () => {
+  it("given_css_text_when_read_then_liquid_shadow_away_hides_the_box_shadow_in_the_layer_and_the_screen_block_only", () => {
+    expect(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS).toBe("liquid-shadow-away");
+    const screenOpen = "@media screen and (forced-colors: none) {";
+    const at = LIQUID_CSS.indexOf(screenOpen);
+    const rest = LIQUID_CSS.slice(at + screenOpen.length);
+    const end = rest.indexOf("\n  }");
+    const screen = rest.slice(0, end);
+    expect(screen).toContain(".liquid-shadow-away { box-shadow: none !important; }");
+    expect(LIQUID_CSS.slice(0, at) + rest.slice(end), "print and forced colours show the element's own shadow").not.toMatch(/liquid-shadow-away|box-shadow/);

+  it("given_two_holders_when_addShadowAwayClass_twice_and_removeShadowAwayClass_once_then_the_class_stays_until_the_last_release_and_an_authors_own_class_is_kept", () => {
+    const el = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("button"));
+    el.className = "btn";
+    addShadowAwayClass(el);
+    addShadowAwayClass(el); // a second instance
+    expect(el.className).toBe(`btn ${SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS}`);
+    removeShadowAwayClass(el);
+    expect(el.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS), "one hold left").toBe(true);
+    removeShadowAwayClass(el);
+    expect(el.className).toBe("btn");
+    removeShadowAwayClass(el);
+    expect(el.className, "an unbalanced release is a no-op").toBe("btn");
+    const authored = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("div"));
+    authored.className = SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS;
+    addShadowAwayClass(authored);
+    removeShadowAwayClass(authored);
+    expect(authored.className, "an author's own class is never removed").toBe(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS);
+  });
+
+  it("given_computed_transitions_when_asked_then_runsBoxShadowTransition_is_true_only_for_all_or_box_shadow_with_a_duration_resolution_12", () => {
+    const el = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("div"));
+    const runs = (property: string, duration: string): boolean => {
+      el.style.transitionProperty = property;
+      el.style.transitionDuration = duration;
+      return runsBoxShadowTransition(el);
+    };
+    expect(runs("color, box-shadow", "0.2s, 300ms")).toBe(true);
+    expect(runs("all", "0.1s")).toBe(true);
+    expect(runs("box-shadow, color", "0s, 1s"), "box-shadow's own duration is 0").toBe(false);
+    expect(runs("opacity", "1s")).toBe(false);
+    el.removeAttribute("style");
+    expect(runsBoxShadowTransition(el), "no transition").toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("given_an_element_with_a_box_shadow_transition_when_the_shadow_class_is_added_and_removed_then_each_write_holds_inline_transition_none_and_the_style_attribute_is_restored_exactly_resolution_12", () => {
+    const faded = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("button"));
+    faded.setAttribute("style", "transition-property: box-shadow; transition-duration: 0.3s;");
+    const plain = document.body.appendChild(document.createElement("button"));
+    const seen = new Map<HTMLElement, string[]>();
+    for (const el of [faded, plain]) {
+      const original = el.classList.toggle.bind(el.classList);
+      seen.set(el, []);
+      vi.spyOn(el.classList, "toggle").mockImplementation((token: string, force?: boolean) => {
+        seen.get(el)!.push(`${el.style.getPropertyValue("transition")}|${el.style.getPropertyPriority("transition")}`);
+        return original(token, force);
+      });
+    }
+    for (const el of [faded, plain]) {
+      addShadowAwayClass(el);
+      expect(el.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS)).toBe(true);
+      removeShadowAwayClass(el);
+      expect(el.classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS)).toBe(false);
+    }
+    expect(seen.get(faded), "the add and the removal start no box-shadow transition").toEqual(["none|important", "none|important"]);
+    expect(faded.getAttribute("style")).toBe("transition-property: box-shadow; transition-duration: 0.3s;");
+    expect(seen.get(plain), "no inline write without a box-shadow transition").toEqual(["|", "|"]);
+    expect(plain.hasAttribute("style")).toBe(false);
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+});
```

```html
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />

    <style>
      /* W88 (D88-8): shadows that follow the liquid. Every element keeps its own box-shadow: the
         browser draws it at rest, the renderer while the liquid is away. Fixed positions at
         1280×800 (it wraps on a phone), far enough apart that no two shadows meet. */
      :root {
        color-scheme: light;
      }
      html,
      body {
        margin: 0;
        min-height: 100%;
      }
      body {
        background: #f3efe6;
        color: #1d1d1f;
        font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
      }
      .stage {
        position: relative;
        width: 1200px;
        height: 520px;
        margin: 60px auto 0;
      }
      .el {
        position: absolute;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        width: 160px;
        height: 56px;
        padding: 0;
        border: 0;
        border-radius: 28px;
        color: #ffffff;
        font: 600 16px/56px system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
        text-align: center;
      }
      #drop { left: 60px; top: 60px; background: #2f6fde; box-shadow: 0 8px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4); }
      #glow { left: 340px; top: 60px; background: #0b6e78; box-shadow: 0 0 12px 2px rgb(0, 255, 255), 0 0 32px 8px rgba(0, 255, 255, 0.5); }
      #spread { left: 620px; top: 60px; background: #6b4bd6; box-shadow: 0 4px 0 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3); }
      #current { left: 900px; top: 60px; background: #ffe3ef; color: rgb(200, 40, 120); box-shadow: 0 0 16px currentColor; }
      #inset { left: 60px; top: 260px; width: 300px; height: 140px; border-radius: 16px; background: #ffffff; color: #1d1d1f; line-height: 140px; box-shadow: inset 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4); }
      #glass { left: 480px; top: 260px; background: rgba(47, 111, 222, 0.5); box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgb(0, 0, 0); }
      #plain { left: 760px; top: 260px; background: #1a8361; }
      /* W88 (Question 2): an author transition on box-shadow, the common hover-glow CSS. */
      #fade { left: 1000px; top: 260px; background: #b8432f; box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45); transition: box-shadow 0.3s ease; }
      .note { position: absolute; left: 60px; top: 460px; margin: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #4a4a4f; }
      @media (max-width: 1240px) {
        .stage { width: auto; height: auto; padding: 24px 16px 40px; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 48px; }
```

**`demo/scenes/shadows.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-8): the shadows scene for e2e/shadows.spec.ts, the webgpu-hw block of
 * e2e/webgpu-robust.spec.ts and the gold smoke test. One instance observes the seven [data-liquid]
 * elements (a drop shadow, a two-layer glow, a spread shadow, a currentColor glow, an inset card, a
 * translucent pill with a black glow, a pill without a shadow). RAF clock by default (live in a
 * browser); `?clock=manual` switches to the manual render-counting clock the e2e needs.
 * `?renderer=canvas2d|webgpu|auto` (default canvas2d); `?rm=1` forces reduced motion.
 * `window.__shadows` (demo/test-hooks.ts ShadowsHook).
 */
import { LiquidDOM, type LiquidDOMInstance } from "liquiddom";
import { createManualClock, rafClock, type FrameClock } from "../../packages/core/ts/src/clock";
import { runtimeOf } from "../../packages/core/ts/src/internal";
import type { FluidRuntime } from "../../packages/core/ts/src/runtime";
import { SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS } from "../../packages/core/ts/src/stylesheet";
import { createRenderCountingClock, type RenderCountingClock } from "../render-counting-clock";
import type { ShadowsHook, ShadowsId } from "../test-hooks";

/** The observed elements in DOM order (autoObserve: every [data-liquid]). */
const SHADOWS_IDS: readonly ShadowsId[] = ["drop", "glow", "spread", "current", "inset", "glass", "plain", "fade"];
const query = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
const rendererRaw = query.get("renderer") ?? "canvas2d";
if (rendererRaw !== "canvas2d" && rendererRaw !== "webgpu" && rendererRaw !== "auto") {
  throw new TypeError(`[shadows] ?renderer must be canvas2d, webgpu or auto, got "${rendererRaw}"`);
}
const renderer: "canvas2d" | "webgpu" | "auto" = rendererRaw;
const clockRaw = query.get("clock") ?? "raf";
if (clockRaw !== "raf" && clockRaw !== "manual") {
  throw new TypeError(`[shadows] ?clock must be "raf" or "manual", got "${clockRaw}"`);
}
const manual: RenderCountingClock | null = clockRaw === "manual" ? createRenderCountingClock(createManualClock(0)) : null;
const clock: FrameClock = manual ?? rafClock;
const forceReducedMotion = query.get("rm") === "1";

function byId(id: string): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) throw new Error(`[shadows] #${id} not found`);
  return el;
}

let instance: LiquidDOMInstance | null = null;

function runtime(): FluidRuntime {
  const rt = instance ? runtimeOf(instance) : undefined;
  if (!rt) throw new Error("[shadows] no instance yet");
  return rt;
}

async function start(): Promise<void> {
  instance = await LiquidDOM.create({ seed: 1, particles: 16000, maxElements: 8, clock, renderer, forceReducedMotion });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => queueMicrotask(resolve)); // the batched redistribute
}

const ready = start();
ready.catch((err: unknown) => {
  console.error("[shadows] start failed", err);
});

// A WebGPU canvas is readable only in the task that rendered it (W71): copy it after each advance.
let snapshot: { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement; ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D } | null = null;

function capture(): void {
  const src = runtime().canvas;
  if (!snapshot) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas"); // detached: never in the DOM
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    if (!ctx) throw new Error("[shadows] no 2d context for the pixel snapshot");
    snapshot = { canvas, ctx };
  }
  const { canvas, ctx } = snapshot;
  if (canvas.width !== src.width) canvas.width = src.width;
  if (canvas.height !== src.height) canvas.height = src.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
}

const hook: ShadowsHook = {
  ready,
  ids: SHADOWS_IDS,
  advance(frames) {
    if (!manual) throw new Error("[shadows] advance() needs ?clock=manual");
    if (manual.advance(frames) === 0) return; // no frame rendered: keep the previous snapshot
    capture();
  },
  pixels() {
    if (!snapshot) throw new Error("[shadows] no snapshot yet: advance() first");
    return snapshot.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, snapshot.canvas.width, snapshot.canvas.height);
  },
  restAlpha: () => SHADOWS_IDS.map((id) => runtime().elementState(byId(id))?.restAlpha ?? Number.NaN),
  held: () => SHADOWS_IDS.map((id) => byId(id).classList.contains(SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS)),
  splash(id: ShadowsId, strength = 1) {
    if (!instance) throw new Error("[shadows] no instance yet");
    instance.splash(byId(id), { strength });
  },
  layers: (id: ShadowsId) => runtime().shadowLayers(byId(id)).map((l) => ({ color: [...l.color], dx: l.dx, dy: l.dy, blur: l.blur, spread: l.spread, inset: l.inset })),
  rect(id: ShadowsId) {
    const r = byId(id).getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: r.x, y: r.y, width: r.width, height: r.height };
  },
  get activeRenderer() {
    return instance ? instance.activeRenderer : "none";
  },
};
(window as Window & { __shadows?: ShadowsHook }).__shadows = hook;
```
  The hook types and the window declaration:
```diff
--- a/demo/test-hooks.ts
+++ b/demo/test-hooks.ts
@@ -299,6 +299,42 @@
   hash(): string;
   /** Snapshot pixels with alpha > 128 outside every observed element's rect grown by `marginPx`. */
   outside(marginPx: number): number;
+  /** The instance's active renderer ("none" before create() resolved). */
+  readonly activeRenderer: string;
+}
+/** W88: the observed elements of demo/scenes/shadows.html, by id, in DOM order. */
+export type ShadowsId = "drop" | "glow" | "spread" | "current" | "inset" | "glass" | "plain" | "fade";
+/** W88: one box-shadow layer as the snapshot read it (CSS px; colour RGBA 0–255, alpha 0–1). */
+export interface ShadowsLayer {
+  color: number[];
+  dx: number;
+  dy: number;
+  blur: number;
+  spread: number;
+  inset: boolean;
+}
+/**
+ * W88 (D88-1 … D88-14): `window.__shadows` on demo/scenes/shadows.html. One instance observes the
+ * seven [data-liquid] elements (`ids`, DOM order). `?clock=manual` for advance();
+ * `?renderer=canvas2d|webgpu|auto` (default canvas2d); `?rm=1` forces reduced motion.
+ */
+export interface ShadowsHook {
+  readonly ready: Promise<void>;
+  readonly ids: readonly ShadowsId[];
+  /** Manual-clock frames of 1000/60 ms (throws without ?clock=manual); snapshots the canvas when a frame rendered (W71 pixels() rule). */
+  advance(frames: number): void;
+  /** RGBA8 copy of the liquid canvas from the last advance() that rendered. */
+  pixels(): ImageData;
+  /** restAlpha per element, `ids` order (NaN if unknown). */
+  restAlpha(): number[];
+  /** Whether each element carries `liquid-shadow-away`, `ids` order. */
+  held(): boolean[];
+  /** `instance.splash(#id, { strength })` (default 1). */
+  splash(id: ShadowsId, strength?: number): void;
+  /** The box-shadow layers the runtime's snapshot holds for #id (FluidRuntime.shadowLayers, @internal). */
+  layers(id: ShadowsId): ShadowsLayer[];
+  /** #id's client rect. */
+  rect(id: ShadowsId): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number };
   /** The instance's active renderer ("none" before create() resolved). */
   readonly activeRenderer: string;
 }
```

  The probes (`e2e/shadows.ts`) and the canvas2d spec. The default `testMatch` routes `shadows.spec.ts` to canvas2d only; `e2e-harness.test.ts` derives the canvas2d list from the files, so no harness pin changes.
**`e2e/shadows.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-1 … D88-14): probes for e2e/shadows.spec.ts (canvas2d) and the webgpu-hw block of
 * e2e/webgpu-robust.spec.ts, on demo/scenes/shadows.html (manual clock, seed 1, 1280×800).
 *
 * - `ringEnergy` reads a page screenshot (the browser's shadow and the canvas's, whichever shows):
 *   Σ |rgb − page background|₁ over the ring between `inner` and `outer` px outside the element's rect.
 * - `canvasBoxes` reads the liquid canvas (`__shadows.pixels()`, the frame of the last advance): the
 *   bounding boxes, in CSS px, of the element's liquid pixels and of its shadow pixels near it.
 */
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test";
import type { ShadowsId } from "../demo/test-hooks";
import { VIEWPORT } from "./projects";

/** demo/scenes/shadows.html: the page background. */
export const SHADOWS_PAGE_RGB: readonly [number, number, number] = [0xf3, 0xef, 0xe6];
/** 2 s of idle frames before a probe. */
export const SHADOWS_IDLE_FRAMES = 120;
/** NORTH-STAR's 3 s re-form budget, in frames, with the splash's own frames on top. */
export const SHADOWS_REST_BUDGET_FRAMES = 300;
/** The liquid colours of the scene (demo/scenes/shadows.html). */
export const LIQUID_RGB: Readonly<Record<"drop" | "glow" | "glass", readonly [number, number, number]>> = {
  drop: [0x2f, 0x6f, 0xde],
  glow: [0x0b, 0x6e, 0x78],
  glass: [0x2f, 0x6f, 0xde],
};

export interface Box {
  x0: number;
  y0: number;
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
}

export async function gotoShadows(page: Page, renderer: "canvas2d" | "webgpu", extra = ""): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto(`/scenes/shadows.html?renderer=${renderer}&clock=manual${extra}`);
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__shadows !== undefined, undefined, { timeout: 15_000 });
  await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.ready);
  await page.evaluate(async () => {
    await document.fonts.ready;
  });
  await page.evaluate((n) => window.__shadows!.advance(n), SHADOWS_IDLE_FRAMES);
  await page.mouse.move(VIEWPORT.width - 1, VIEWPORT.height - 1);
}

export async function advanceShadows(page: Page, frames: number): Promise<void> {
  await page.evaluate((n) => window.__shadows!.advance(n), frames);
}

/** `liquid-shadow-away` on #id now. */
export async function heldOf(page: Page, id: ShadowsId): Promise<boolean> {
  return page.evaluate((i) => window.__shadows!.held()[window.__shadows!.ids.indexOf(i)] ?? false, id);
}

export async function restAlphaOf(page: Page, id: ShadowsId): Promise<number> {
  return page.evaluate((i) => window.__shadows!.restAlpha()[window.__shadows!.ids.indexOf(i)] ?? Number.NaN, id);
}

/** Σ |rgb − background|₁ over the page pixels between `inner` and `outer` px outside #id's rect (a page screenshot). */
export async function ringEnergy(page: Page, id: ShadowsId, inner: number, outer: number): Promise<number> {
  const r = await page.evaluate((i) => window.__shadows!.rect(i), id);
  const x = Math.max(0, Math.floor(r.x - outer));
  const y = Math.max(0, Math.floor(r.y - outer));
  const clip = { x, y, width: Math.ceil(r.x + r.width + outer) - x, height: Math.ceil(r.y + r.height + outer) - y };
  const shot = await page.screenshot({ clip });
  return page.evaluate(
    async ([b64, rect, ox, oy, lo, hi, bg]) => {
      const blob = await (await fetch(`data:image/png;base64,${b64}`)).blob();
      const bmp = await createImageBitmap(blob);
      const off = new OffscreenCanvas(bmp.width, bmp.height);
      const ctx = off.getContext("2d");
      if (!ctx) throw new Error("W88: no 2d context for the ring probe");
      ctx.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0);
      const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, bmp.width, bmp.height).data;
      let energy = 0;
      for (let py = 0; py < bmp.height; py++) {
        for (let px = 0; px < bmp.width; px++) {
          const cx = ox + px + 0.5;
          const cy = oy + py + 0.5;
          const dx = Math.max(rect.x - cx, 0, cx - (rect.x + rect.width));
          const dy = Math.max(rect.y - cy, 0, cy - (rect.y + rect.height));
          const dist = Math.max(dx, dy);
          if (dist <= lo || dist > hi) continue;
          const o = (py * bmp.width + px) * 4;
          energy += Math.abs(d[o] - bg[0]) + Math.abs(d[o + 1] - bg[1]) + Math.abs(d[o + 2] - bg[2]);
        }
      }
      return energy;
    },
    [shot.toString("base64"), r, x, y, inner, outer, SHADOWS_PAGE_RGB] as const,
  );
}

/**
 * The bounding boxes (CSS px) of #id's liquid pixels (alpha ≥ 200, |rgb − liquid|₁ < 90) and of its
 * shadow pixels (`dark`: rgb ≤ 60 each; `cyan`: r < 120, g and b > 150; alpha ≥ 12 either way) within
 * `grow` px of its rect, from the last snapshot of the liquid canvas. Null when there are none.
 */
export async function canvasBoxes(page: Page, id: ShadowsId, grow: number, liquid: readonly [number, number, number], kind: "dark" | "cyan"): Promise<{ liquid: Box | null; shadow: Box | null }> {
  return page.evaluate(
    ([i, g, rgb, k]) => {
      const h = window.__shadows!;
      const img = h.pixels();
      const canvas = document.querySelector<HTMLCanvasElement>("canvas.liquid-canvas")!;
      const cr = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const sx = img.width / cr.width;
      const sy = img.height / cr.height;
      const r = h.rect(i);
      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((r.x - g - cr.left) * sx));
      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((r.y - g - cr.top) * sy));
      const x1 = Math.min(img.width, Math.ceil((r.x + r.width + g - cr.left) * sx));
      const y1 = Math.min(img.height, Math.ceil((r.y + r.height + g - cr.top) * sy));
      const grow = (b: Box | null, x: number, y: number): Box => (b === null ? { x0: x, y0: y, x1: x, y1: y } : { x0: Math.min(b.x0, x), y0: Math.min(b.y0, y), x1: Math.max(b.x1, x), y1: Math.max(b.y1, y) });
      type Box = { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number };
      let liq: Box | null = null;
      let sh: Box | null = null;
      for (let py = y0; py < y1; py++) {
        for (let px = x0; px < x1; px++) {
          const o = (py * img.width + px) * 4;
          const [cr0, cg, cb, ca] = [img.data[o], img.data[o + 1], img.data[o + 2], img.data[o + 3]];
          const cx = cr.left + (px + 0.5) / sx;
          const cy = cr.top + (py + 0.5) / sy;
          const isLiquid = ca >= 200 && Math.abs(cr0 - rgb[0]) + Math.abs(cg - rgb[1]) + Math.abs(cb - rgb[2]) < 90;
          if (isLiquid) {
            liq = grow(liq, cx, cy);
            continue;
          }
          const isShadow = ca >= 12 && (k === "dark" ? cr0 <= 60 && cg <= 60 && cb <= 60 : cr0 < 120 && cg > 150 && cb > 150);
          if (isShadow) sh = grow(sh, cx, cy);
        }
      }
      return { liquid: liq, shadow: sh };
    },
    [id, grow, liquid, kind] as const,
  );
}

/**
 * The largest alpha of a liquid-hued canvas pixel no lighter than the liquid (blue − red > 60, red
 * and green at most the liquid's + 20) within #id's rect grown by `grow` (the knockout probe,
 * D88-10). A black shadow under 50 % liquid would darken it and raise its alpha; WebGPU's white
 * liquid text (W74) lightens it and is left out.
 */
export async function maxLiquidAlpha(page: Page, id: ShadowsId, grow: number, liquid: readonly [number, number, number] = LIQUID_RGB.glass): Promise<number> {
  return page.evaluate(
    ([i, g, rgb]) => {
      const h = window.__shadows!;
      const img = h.pixels();
      const canvas = document.querySelector<HTMLCanvasElement>("canvas.liquid-canvas")!;
      const cr = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const sx = img.width / cr.width;
      const sy = img.height / cr.height;
      const r = h.rect(i);
      let max = 0;
      for (let py = Math.max(0, Math.floor((r.y - g - cr.top) * sy)); py < Math.min(img.height, Math.ceil((r.y + r.height + g - cr.top) * sy)); py++) {
        for (let px = Math.max(0, Math.floor((r.x - g - cr.left) * sx)); px < Math.min(img.width, Math.ceil((r.x + r.width + g - cr.left) * sx)); px++) {
          const o = (py * img.width + px) * 4;
          const hued = img.data[o + 2] - img.data[o] > 60 && img.data[o] <= rgb[0] + 20 && img.data[o + 1] <= rgb[1] + 20;
          if (hued && img.data[o + 3] > max) max = img.data[o + 3];
        }
      }
      return max;
    },
    [id, grow, liquid] as const,
  );
}

export interface HandoffRun {
  /** The frame (from the splash) at which restAlpha reached 1 and stayed. */
  rest: number;
  /** Per recorded frame: restAlpha, the class, the ring energy over the browser's at rest. */
  frames: Array<{ f: number; restAlpha: number; held: boolean; ratio: number }>;
  /** Class changes in the recorded window (restAlpha bounces before rest, spike W88.0). */
  toggles: number;
}

/**
 * D88-7 (Review Focus: the handoff with exact radii, the non-monotonic restAlpha window): splash #id
 * at `strength`, run frames until its restAlpha is 1 for 3 frames, and record from the first frame
 * with restAlpha ≥ `from` on the ring energy of every frame (page screenshots), relative to the
 * browser's at rest 3 frames after the end.
 */
export async function handoff(page: Page, id: ShadowsId, strength: number, inner: number, outer: number, from = 0.8): Promise<HandoffRun> {
  await page.evaluate(([i, s]) => window.__shadows!.splash(i, s), [id, strength] as const);
  const raw: Array<{ f: number; restAlpha: number; held: boolean; energy: number }> = [];
  let rest = -1;
  let calm = 0;
  let recording = false;
  for (let f = 1; f <= SHADOWS_REST_BUDGET_FRAMES; f++) {
    await advanceShadows(page, 1);
    const a = await restAlphaOf(page, id);
    if (!recording && a >= from && f > 4) recording = true;
    if (recording) raw.push({ f, restAlpha: a, held: await heldOf(page, id), energy: await ringEnergy(page, id, inner, outer) });
    calm = a === 1 ? calm + 1 : 0;
    if (calm === 1) rest = f;
    if (calm === 4) break;
  }
  const atRest = raw.at(-1)?.energy ?? 0;
  let toggles = 0;
  for (let k = 1; k < raw.length; k++) if (raw[k]!.held !== raw[k - 1]!.held) toggles += 1;
  return { rest, toggles, frames: raw.map((r) => ({ f: r.f, restAlpha: r.restAlpha, held: r.held, ratio: atRest > 0 ? r.energy / atRest : Number.NaN })) };
}
```
**`e2e/shadows.spec.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 "Shadows follow the liquid" (canvas2d, blocking; the default testMatch routes this file to
 * canvas2d only; the WebGPU twins of E2, E3 and E8 are the W88 block of webgpu-robust.spec.ts).
 * demo/scenes/shadows.html, manual clock, seed 1, 1280×800. D88-1: the browser draws the shadow at
 * rest, the renderer while the liquid is away (`liquid-shadow-away`); D88-7: the handoff is instant,
 * with no gap and no double frame (Review Focus: the non-monotonic restAlpha before rest); D88-2:
 * inset at rest only; D88-10: no shadow under the element's own liquid; reduced motion, print.
 */
import { test, expect } from "./fixtures";
import { VIEWPORT } from "./projects";
import { LIQUID_RGB, advanceShadows, canvasBoxes, gotoShadows, handoff, heldOf, maxLiquidAlpha, ringEnergy } from "./shadows";

test.use({ viewport: { ...VIEWPORT } });

/** A splash is in full motion after this many frames (restAlpha well below 1). */
const MID_SPLASH_FRAMES = 8;
/** D88-7: every frame's ring energy within this factor of the browser's at rest (no gap: never near 0; no double: never near 2). */
const HANDOFF_MIN = 0.6;
const HANDOFF_MAX = 1.4;
/** D88-10: the #glass liquid is 50 % opaque; a shadow under it would raise its alpha above this. */
const GLASS_ALPHA_MAX = 158;

test.describe("W88 – shadows follow the liquid (canvas2d)", () => {
  test("E1 D88-1 – given the scene at rest when screenshotted then each element's own box-shadow shows around it, no element holds liquid-shadow-away and the canvas draws no shadow", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.held()), "nothing is away at rest").toEqual([false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false]);
    const drop = await ringEnergy(page, "drop", 2, 40);
    const glow = await ringEnergy(page, "glow", 2, 40);
    const plain = await ringEnergy(page, "plain", 2, 40);
    console.log(`W88 E1 ring energy at rest: drop ${drop}, glow ${glow}, plain ${plain}`);
    expect(drop, "the drop shadow is drawn by the browser").toBeGreaterThan(10 * Math.max(plain, 1000));
    expect(glow).toBeGreaterThan(10 * Math.max(plain, 1000));
    const outside = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const h = window.__shadows!;
      const img = h.pixels();
      const rects = h.ids.map((i) => h.rect(i));
      let n = 0;
      for (let y = 0; y < img.height; y++) {
        for (let x = 0; x < img.width; x++) {
          if (img.data[(y * img.width + x) * 4 + 3]! <= 8) continue;
          if (!rects.some((r) => x >= r.x - 2 && x <= r.x + r.width + 2 && y >= r.y - 2 && y <= r.y + r.height + 2)) n++;
        }
      }
      return n;
    });
    expect(outside, "at rest the canvas holds only the liquid in the rects").toBe(0);
  });

  test("E2 D88-1, D88-5 – given a splash on Drop and Glow when mid-splash then both hold the class, the browser's shadow is off and the canvas's shadow follows the deformed liquid", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      window.__shadows!.splash("drop", 1);
      window.__shadows!.splash("glow", 1);
    });
    await advanceShadows(page, MID_SPLASH_FRAMES);
    expect([await heldOf(page, "drop"), await heldOf(page, "glow")]).toEqual([true, true]);
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById("drop")!).boxShadow)).toBe("none");
    const drop = await canvasBoxes(page, "drop", 80, LIQUID_RGB.drop, "dark");
    const glow = await canvasBoxes(page, "glow", 100, LIQUID_RGB.glow, "cyan");
    console.log(`W88 E2 drop ${JSON.stringify(drop)}; glow ${JSON.stringify(glow)}`);
    expect(drop.liquid && drop.shadow && glow.liquid && glow.shadow, "liquid and shadow pixels on the canvas").toBeTruthy();
    const dropRect = await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.rect("drop"));
    const deformed = Math.abs(drop.liquid!.x1 - drop.liquid!.x0 - dropRect.width) > 4 || Math.abs(drop.liquid!.y1 - drop.liquid!.y0 - dropRect.height) > 4;
    expect(deformed, "the splash deformed the liquid (else nothing follows)").toBe(true);
    expect(drop.shadow!.y1, "the drop shadow lies under the liquid's lowest point, 8 px down").toBeGreaterThan(drop.liquid!.y1 + 6);
    for (const side of ["x0", "y0"] as const) expect(glow.shadow![side], `the glow surrounds the liquid (${side})`).toBeLessThan(glow.liquid![side] - 6);
    for (const side of ["x1", "y1"] as const) expect(glow.shadow![side], `the glow surrounds the liquid (${side})`).toBeGreaterThan(glow.liquid![side] + 6);
  });

  test("E3 D88-7 – given a soft splash on Drop and on Glow when they come to rest then the class follows restAlpha frame by frame and the ring never shows a gap or a double shadow", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    for (const id of ["drop", "glow"] as const) {
      const run = await handoff(page, id, 0.5, 8, 40);
      const ratios = run.frames.map((f) => f.ratio);
      console.log(`W88 E3 ${id}: rest at f${run.rest}, ${run.frames.length} frames recorded, ${run.toggles} class changes, ratio ${Math.min(...ratios).toFixed(3)}…${Math.max(...ratios).toFixed(3)}`);
      expect(run.rest, `${id} rests within the budget`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const f of run.frames) {
        expect(f.held, `${id} f${f.f}: the class is on exactly while restAlpha < 1 (restAlpha ${f.restAlpha})`).toBe(f.restAlpha < 1);
        expect(f.ratio, `${id} f${f.f}: no gap`).toBeGreaterThan(HANDOFF_MIN);
        expect(f.ratio, `${id} f${f.f}: no double shadow`).toBeLessThan(HANDOFF_MAX);
      }
    }
  });

  test("E4 D88-2 – given a splash on the inset card when mid-splash then its inset shadow hides with the class and returns at rest", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    const inset = (): Promise<string> => page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById("inset")!).boxShadow);
    expect(await inset()).toContain("inset");
    await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.splash("inset", 1));
    await advanceShadows(page, MID_SPLASH_FRAMES);
    expect(await heldOf(page, "inset")).toBe(true);
    expect(await inset()).toBe("none");
    await advanceShadows(page, 300);
    expect(await heldOf(page, "inset")).toBe(false);
    expect(await inset()).toContain("inset");
  });

  test("E5 D88-3 – given a currentColor glow when the scene loads then the snapshot holds the element's text colour (Review Focus 5)", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    const layers = await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.layers("current"));
    expect(layers).toEqual([{ color: [200, 40, 120, 1], dx: 0, dy: 0, blur: 16, spread: 0, inset: false }]);
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.layers("plain"))).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("E6 reduced motion – given ?rm=1 when every element is splashed then nothing holds the class and the browser's drop shadow stays as it was", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d", "&rm=1");
    const before = await ringEnergy(page, "drop", 2, 40);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      for (const id of window.__shadows!.ids) window.__shadows!.splash(id, 1);
    });
    for (let f = 0; f < 30; f++) {
      await advanceShadows(page, 1);
      expect((await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.held())).some(Boolean), `f${f}`).toBe(false);
    }
    const after = await ringEnergy(page, "drop", 2, 40);
    expect(Math.abs(after - before) / before).toBeLessThan(0.02);
  });

  test("E7 print – given Drop mid-splash when print is emulated then its own box-shadow is back (the class rule is screen-only) and the canvas is hidden", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.splash("drop", 1));
    await advanceShadows(page, MID_SPLASH_FRAMES);
    expect(await heldOf(page, "drop")).toBe(true);
    await page.emulateMedia({ media: "print" });
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById("drop")!).boxShadow)).toContain("20px");
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.querySelector("canvas.liquid-canvas")!).display)).toBe("none");
    await page.emulateMedia({ media: "screen" });
  });

  test("E8 D88-10 – given the translucent Glass pill with a black glow mid-splash when its liquid is read then no shadow lies under its own liquid (the knockout)", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    await page.evaluate(() => window.__shadows!.splash("glass", 1));
    await advanceShadows(page, MID_SPLASH_FRAMES);
    expect(await heldOf(page, "glass")).toBe(true);
    const alpha = await maxLiquidAlpha(page, "glass", 60);
    console.log(`W88 E8 glass: max liquid alpha ${alpha}`);
    expect(alpha, "the liquid's own 50 % alpha, no shadow under it").toBeLessThanOrEqual(GLASS_ALPHA_MAX);
    expect(alpha, "the liquid is there").toBeGreaterThan(80);
  });

  test("E9 resolution 12 – given Fade with an author transition: box-shadow 0.3s when it splashes and comes to rest then its browser shadow goes and comes back at once (no fade: no double shadow, no gap) and no transitionend loop", async ({ page }) => {
    await gotoShadows(page, "canvas2d");
    const full = "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45) 0px 6px 18px 0px";
    const boxShadow = (): Promise<string> => page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById("fade")!).boxShadow);
    expect(await boxShadow()).toBe(full);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById("fade")!;
      (window as unknown as { __fadeEnds: number }).__fadeEnds = 0;
      el.addEventListener("transitionend", () => (window as unknown as { __fadeEnds: number }).__fadeEnds++);
      window.__shadows!.splash("fade", 0.5);
    });
    await advanceShadows(page, 1);
    expect(await heldOf(page, "fade")).toBe(true);
    expect(await boxShadow(), "the class takes the browser's shadow away at once").toBe("none");
    let rested = false;
    for (let f = 0; f < 400 && !rested; f++) {
      await advanceShadows(page, 1);
      if (!(await heldOf(page, "fade"))) {
        rested = true;
        expect(await boxShadow(), "the handoff gives the browser's shadow back at once").toBe(full);
      }
    }
    expect(rested).toBe(true);
    await page.waitForTimeout(400); // longer than the author's 0.3 s: any transition would have ended
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { __fadeEnds: number }).__fadeEnds), "no box-shadow transition ran").toBe(0);
  });
});
```

### Task W88.2: The snapshot, the toggle and the stylesheet (D88-1, D88-2, D88-3, D88-7)

**`packages/core/ts/src/shadow.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (Epic 16, D88-1 … D88-3): the author's `box-shadow`, read for the renderers. The browser
 * draws it at rest; while the element's liquid is away the runtime hides it (`liquid-shadow-away`,
 * shadow-toggle.ts) and the renderer draws the outer layers from the liquid's shape.
 *
 * `parseBoxShadow` reads a computed value: browsers serialise every layer as
 * `<color> <dx>px <dy>px <blur>px <spread>px [inset]` (`none` without a shadow; jsdom returns the
 * specified text, so the parser takes the parts in any order and a unitless 0). Colours go through
 * `parseCssColor` and the W86 colour probe, so oklch works; `currentColor` and CSS variables arrive
 * resolved as `rgb(...)` (Review Focus 5). A layer that does not parse, or whose colour is
 * transparent, is dropped. Pure apart from `snapshotShadow`, which reads the computed style.
 */
import { parseCssColor, withClassLifted, type RGBA } from "./color";
import { Interaction } from "./fluid-layout";
import { SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS } from "./stylesheet";

/** One `box-shadow` layer in CSS px (D88-3). */
export interface ShadowLayer {
  readonly color: RGBA;
  readonly dx: number;
  readonly dy: number;
  /** ≥ 0. */
  readonly blur: number;
  /** May be negative. */
  readonly spread: number;
  readonly inset: boolean;
}

/** D88-2: the renderer draws at most this many outer layers per element (the first ones in CSS order). */
export const MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS = 4;

const NONE: readonly ShadowLayer[] = Object.freeze([]);

/** Top-level commas only (a colour function holds commas of its own). */
function splitTopLevel(s: string, sep: (c: string) => boolean): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  let depth = 0;
  let start = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    const c = s[i] ?? "";
    if (c === "(") depth += 1;
    else if (c === ")") depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
    else if (depth === 0 && sep(c)) {
      out.push(s.slice(start, i));
      start = i + 1;
    }
  }
  out.push(s.slice(start));
  return out.map((t) => t.trim()).filter((t) => t.length > 0);
}

const LENGTH = /^(-?(?:\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)(?:e-?\d+)?)(px)?$/i;

function parseLayer(text: string): ShadowLayer | null {
  const tokens = splitTopLevel(text, (c) => c === " " || c === "\t" || c === "\n");
  const lengths: number[] = [];
  const colour: string[] = [];
  let inset = false;
  for (const t of tokens) {
    if (t.toLowerCase() === "inset") {
      if (inset) return null;
      inset = true;
      continue;
    }
    const m = LENGTH.exec(t);
    if (m) {
      const v = Number(m[1]);
      // Only px (or a bare 0) is a computed length; a bare non-zero number is invalid CSS.
      if (!Number.isFinite(v) || (m[2] === undefined && v !== 0)) return null;
      lengths.push(v);
      continue;
    }
    colour.push(t);
  }
  if (lengths.length < 2 || lengths.length > 4 || colour.length !== 1) return null;
  const [dx = 0, dy = 0, blur = 0, spread = 0] = lengths;
  if (blur < 0) return null;
  const color = parseCssColor(colour[0]);
  if (color === null) return null; // transparent or unreadable: nothing to draw
  return Object.freeze({ color, dx, dy, blur, spread, inset });
}

/** D88-3: every layer of a computed `box-shadow`, in CSS order (the first paints on top); `none`, empty or garbage → none. */
export function parseBoxShadow(computed: string | null | undefined): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  if (typeof computed !== "string") return NONE;
  const s = computed.trim();
  if (s === "" || s.toLowerCase() === "none") return NONE;
  const layers: ShadowLayer[] = [];
  for (const part of splitTopLevel(s, (c) => c === ",")) {
    const layer = parseLayer(part);
    if (layer) layers.push(layer);
  }
  return layers.length === 0 ? NONE : Object.freeze(layers);
}

const outerCache = new WeakMap<readonly ShadowLayer[], readonly ShadowLayer[]>();

/** D88-2: the layers the renderer draws: outer only, the first MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS in CSS order. Memoised per array. */
export function outerShadows(layers: readonly ShadowLayer[]): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  if (layers.length === 0) return NONE;
  const hit = outerCache.get(layers);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const outer = layers.filter((l) => !l.inset).slice(0, MAX_SHADOW_LAYERS);
  const frozen = outer.length === 0 ? NONE : Object.freeze(outer);
  outerCache.set(layers, frozen);
  return frozen;
}

/** True when any layer is an outer one (the renderer has something to draw). */
export function hasOuterLayer(layers: readonly ShadowLayer[]): boolean {
  for (const l of layers) if (!l.inset) return true;
  return false;
}

/**
 * D88-3: the element's computed `box-shadow`, parsed. While another instance (or this one) holds
 * `liquid-shadow-away` on it, the class is lifted for the read (inline `transition: none` meanwhile,
 * both attributes restored to their exact strings: W66's colour-snapshot lift), so the author's
 * shadow is read, never `none`.
 */
export function snapshotShadow(el: HTMLElement): readonly ShadowLayer[] {
  return withClassLifted(el, SHADOW_AWAY_CLASS, () => parseBoxShadow(getComputedStyle(el).boxShadow));
}

/**
 * D88-1: an element's liquid is away while its restAlpha < 1 or it is dragged, never under reduced
 * motion. NaN counts as rest (W74's rule). Shared by the toggle and both renderers, so the class and
 * the drawn shadow always agree.
 */
export function liquidAway(reducedMotion: boolean, restAlpha: number, interaction: number): boolean {
  if (reducedMotion) return false;
  return restAlpha < 1 || Math.abs(interaction - Interaction.DRAGGED) < 0.5;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `shadow-toggle.ts`** (replaces the stub):
**`packages/core/ts/src/shadow-toggle.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-1, D88-2, D88-7): the `liquid-shadow-away` toggle, W74's text-toggle.ts pattern. The runtime
 * calls `syncShadowClasses` once per frame after `render()` (and after syncText, syncAway and
 * syncHalo; the four never read each other's classes), so the class and the renderer's shadow
 * change in the same frame: the instant handoff of D88-7. An element holds the class while its
 * liquid is away (`liquidAway`: restAlpha < 1 or dragged, never under reduced motion) and a renderer
 * drew this frame — for an element with outer layers only when that renderer drew them
 * (`shadowsDrawn`), so the browser's shadow never hides without the renderer's; an inset-only
 * element hides its inset in motion (D88-2). An element without a box-shadow is never written.
 * Holds are refcounted across instances in stylesheet.ts; `held` is this instance's own set.
 * Every other path takes the class off: `clearShadowClasses` (a stop, reduced motion, a lost device,
 * a failed rebuild, destroy), `releaseShadowClass` (unobserve), the sweep (a missed release).
 */
import type { Renderer } from "./renderers/frame";
import { hasOuterLayer, liquidAway, type ShadowLayer } from "./shadow";
import { addShadowAwayClass, removeShadowAwayClass } from "./stylesheet";

export interface ShadowEntry {
  readonly id: number;
  readonly el: HTMLElement;
  /** Every layer the snapshot read, inset ones included (ElementRecord.shadows). */
  readonly shadows: readonly ShadowLayer[];
}

export interface ShadowSyncArgs {
  readonly entries: Iterable<ShadowEntry>;
  restAlpha(id: number): number;
  /** Slot 5 of the element buffer this frame (Interaction). */
  interaction(id: number): number;
  /** null while a device-lost rebuild is in flight. */
  readonly renderer: Renderer | null;
  readonly reducedMotion: boolean;
  readonly held: Set<HTMLElement>;
}

/**
 * D88-1, D88-2: `rendererOn` (a renderer drew this frame), `away` (liquidAway), the element's layers and
 * whether the renderer drew its outer layers this frame.
 */
export function wantsShadowAway(rendererOn: boolean, away: boolean, layers: readonly ShadowLayer[], drawn: boolean): boolean {
  if (!rendererOn || !away || layers.length === 0) return false;
  return hasOuterLayer(layers) ? drawn : true;
}

/** Elements visited by the current sync; reused (the sync is synchronous and never re-entered). */
const seen = new Set<HTMLElement>();

export function syncShadowClasses(a: ShadowSyncArgs): void {
  seen.clear();
  const r = a.renderer;
  for (const { id, el, shadows } of a.entries) {
    seen.add(el);
    const away = shadows.length > 0 && liquidAway(a.reducedMotion, a.restAlpha(id), a.interaction(id));
    const want = wantsShadowAway(r !== null, away, shadows, r?.shadowsDrawn?.(id) === true);
    const held = a.held.has(el);
    if (want && !held) {
      a.held.add(el);
      addShadowAwayClass(el);
    } else if (!want && held) {
      a.held.delete(el);
      removeShadowAwayClass(el);
    }
  }
  // An element this instance still holds but no longer observes (a missed release) lets go too (W74's rule).
  for (const el of a.held) {
    if (!seen.has(el)) {
      a.held.delete(el);
      removeShadowAwayClass(el);
    }
  }
  seen.clear();
}

/** Releases every hold of this instance. */
export function clearShadowClasses(held: Set<HTMLElement>): void {
  for (const el of held) removeShadowAwayClass(el);
  held.clear();
}

/** Releases this instance's hold on `el` (unobserve); no-op when it holds none. */
export function releaseShadowClass(held: Set<HTMLElement>, el: HTMLElement): void {
  if (held.delete(el)) removeShadowAwayClass(el);
}
```
