### Task W88.3: Canvas2D shadows (D88-4, D88-5, D88-10, D88-12, D88-14)

**Files:** Replace the stubs `renderers/shadow-plan.ts` and `renderers/shadow-canvas2d.ts`. Modify `renderers/fluid-canvas2d.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 1: `renderers/shadow-plan.ts`** (replaces the stub; the casters for both renderers and the WebGPU plan):
**`packages/core/ts/src/renderers/shadow-plan.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-6, D88-11): which elements cast a renderer shadow this frame, and how the WebGPU
 * renderer groups their layers into passes. Pure: no DOM, no GPU.
 *
 * `ShadowCasters.collect()` finds every painted, active element with outer layers whose liquid is
 * away (`liquidAway`, the rule the toggle uses), skips parked particles (W81) and inactive slots,
 * and buckets the casters' particles by slot (a counting sort) with their bounds. Both renderers use
 * it; an element without a shadow costs one array read per frame.
 *
 * `planShadows()` (WebGPU) assigns density sources and radius groups:
 * - one density source per element and distinct spread (spread 0 always, for the knockout), four per
 *   `rgba16float` layer, at most SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES; an element whose sources do not fit draws no
 *   renderer shadow this frame (its toggle then keeps the browser's shadow);
 * - one group per distinct exact blur radius (D88-6 amended), half resolution, quarter for blur ≥ 32 px;
 *   past SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS distinct radii a radius goes to its log-nearest bucket {4, 8, 16, 32, 64};
 * - one record per layer: its source, its offset (applied in the resolve, D88-11) and its colour.
 */
import { Dyn, El, ELEMENT_STRIDE, FLAG_PARKED, St, STATE_STRIDE, Stat } from "../fluid-layout";
import { liquidAway } from "../shadow";
import type { RenderFrame } from "./frame";
import { KERNEL_RADIUS_CAP_PX, kernelRadiusPx } from "./kernel-params";

/** D88-6: the shadow targets' scale against the canvas backing size. */
export const SHADOW_SCALE_HALF = 0.5;
export const SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER = 0.25;
/** D88-6 (spike W88.0 Q2): blur radii from this many CSS px on use the quarter-resolution targets. */
export const SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX = 32;
/** D88-6 (amended): distinct exact radii per frame; the rest go to a bucket. */
export const SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS = 6;
/** D88-6: the overflow buckets (CSS px). */
export const SHADOW_BUCKETS_PX: readonly number[] = Object.freeze([4, 8, 16, 32, 64]);
/** D88-11: density channels per `rgba16float` layer and layers per frame. */
export const SHADOW_CHANNELS = 4;
export const SHADOW_MAX_DENSITY_LAYERS = 4;
export const SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES = SHADOW_CHANNELS * SHADOW_MAX_DENSITY_LAYERS;
/** D88-10 (amended, resolution 3): the knockout closes holes narrower than about 2 × this × the kernel radius (both renderers). */
export const KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL = 1.5;

export interface Rect {
  x0: number;
  y0: number;
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
}

export class ShadowCasters {
  /** Slots that cast a renderer shadow this frame (painted, active, outer layers, liquid away, ≥ 1 particle), ascending. */
  readonly ids: number[] = [];
  /** Per slot: the particle-centre bounds `x0, y0, x1, y1` in CSS px (valid for `ids`). */
  bounds = new Float64Array(0);
  /** Per slot: the caster's particles are `order[start[id]] … order[start[id] + count[id] − 1]`. */
  start = new Int32Array(0);
  count = new Int32Array(0);
  order = new Int32Array(0);
  private want = new Uint8Array(0);
  private cursor = new Int32Array(0);

  private ensure(slots: number, cap: number): void {
    if (this.want.length < slots) {
      this.want = new Uint8Array(slots);
      this.bounds = new Float64Array(slots * 4);
      this.start = new Int32Array(slots);
      this.count = new Int32Array(slots);
      this.cursor = new Int32Array(slots);
    }
    if (this.order.length < cap) this.order = new Int32Array(cap);
  }

  /** The casters of `frame`; 0 (and no particle pass) when none. */
  collect(frame: RenderFrame): number {
    this.ids.length = 0;
    if (frame.suppressShadows === true || frame.reducedMotion) return 0;
    const ev = frame.elementView;
    const sv = frame.stateView;
    const slots = Math.min(frame.paints.length, Math.floor(ev.length / ELEMENT_STRIDE), Math.floor(sv.length / STATE_STRIDE));
    if (slots <= 0) return 0;
    this.ensure(slots, frame.particleCapacity);
    const want = this.want;
    want.fill(0, 0, slots);
    let any = false;
    for (let id = 0; id < slots; id++) {
      const layers = frame.paints[id]?.shadows;
      if (!layers || layers.length === 0) continue;
      const o = id * ELEMENT_STRIDE;
      if (!((ev[o + El.W] as number) > 0)) continue; // inactive (w == 0): no shadow (D88-4)
      if (!liquidAway(false, sv[id * STATE_STRIDE + St.REST_ALPHA] as number, ev[o + El.INTERACTION] as number)) continue;
      want[id] = 1;
      any = true;
    }
    if (!any) return 0;
    const b = this.bounds;
    const count = this.count;
    for (let id = 0; id < slots; id++) {
      count[id] = 0;
      b[4 * id] = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      b[4 * id + 1] = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      b[4 * id + 2] = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
      b[4 * id + 3] = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
    }
    const cap = frame.particleCapacity;
    const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(frame.activeParticles, cap));
    const dyn = frame.dynamicView;
    const st = frame.staticView;
    const casterOf = (i: number): number => {
      const h = st[Stat.HOME * cap + i] as number;
      if (!(h >= 0)) return -1;
      const id = h | 0;
      if (id >= slots || want[id] !== 1) return -1;
      if (((dyn[Dyn.FLAGS * cap + i] as number) & FLAG_PARKED) !== 0) return -1; // W81: parked
      const x = dyn[Dyn.X * cap + i] as number;
      const y = dyn[Dyn.Y * cap + i] as number;
      return Number.isFinite(x) && Number.isFinite(y) ? id : -1;
    };
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const id = casterOf(i);
      if (id < 0) continue;
      count[id] += 1;
      const x = dyn[Dyn.X * cap + i] as number;
      const y = dyn[Dyn.Y * cap + i] as number;
      if (x < b[4 * id]) b[4 * id] = x;
      if (y < b[4 * id + 1]) b[4 * id + 1] = y;
      if (x > b[4 * id + 2]) b[4 * id + 2] = x;
      if (y > b[4 * id + 3]) b[4 * id + 3] = y;
    }
    let at = 0;
    for (let id = 0; id < slots; id++) {
      this.start[id] = at;
      this.cursor[id] = at;
      at += count[id];
      if (want[id] === 1 && count[id] > 0) this.ids.push(id);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const id = casterOf(i);
      if (id < 0) continue;
      this.order[this.cursor[id]++] = i;
    }
    return this.ids.length;
  }
}

/** D88-6: the log-nearest bucket of `blur` (CSS px); 0 stays 0. */
export function nearestBucket(blur: number): number {
  if (!(blur > 0)) return 0;
  let best = SHADOW_BUCKETS_PX[0] ?? blur;
  for (const b of SHADOW_BUCKETS_PX) if (Math.abs(Math.log(b / blur)) < Math.abs(Math.log(best / blur))) best = b;
  return best;
}

/** The exact radius a group is keyed by: half-px steps, so float noise never splits a group. */
export function groupBlur(blur: number): number {
  return blur > 0 ? Math.round(blur * 2) / 2 : 0;
}

/** Share of the 2D kernel (1 − r²)² beyond a line at signed distance t (in kernel radii) from its centre. */
function kernelTail(t: number): number {
  if (t >= 1) return 0;
  if (t <= -1) return 1;
  // 1D marginal of the normalised kernel: (16 / 5π)(1 − u²)^{5/2}; Simpson over [t, 1].
  const steps = 128;
  const h = (1 - t) / steps;
  const p = (u: number): number => (16 / (5 * Math.PI)) * Math.pow(Math.max(0, 1 - u * u), 2.5);
  let s = p(t) + p(1);
  for (let k = 1; k < steps; k++) s += (k % 2 === 1 ? 4 : 2) * p(t + k * h);
  return (s * h) / 3;
}

const spreadCache = new Map<string, { readonly radius: number; readonly weightScale: number }>();

/**
 * D88-11: the widened, mass-scaled kernel of a spread layer (WebGPU). Radius R + |spread|; the
 * weight scale moves the 0.5 threshold `spread` px out of (or into) a straight edge of the particle
 * distribution: k · tail(spread / R') = 0.5. Spread 0 is the liquid's own kernel (scale 1).
 */
export function spreadKernel(kernelR: number, spread: number): { readonly radius: number; readonly weightScale: number } {
  if (spread === 0 || !Number.isFinite(spread)) return { radius: kernelR, weightScale: 1 };
  const key = `${kernelR}:${spread}`;
  const hit = spreadCache.get(key);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const radius = kernelR + Math.abs(spread);
  const tail = kernelTail(spread / radius);
  const out = { radius, weightScale: tail > 1e-6 ? 0.5 / tail : 0 };
  if (spreadCache.size > 256) spreadCache.clear();
  spreadCache.set(key, out);
  return out;
}

export interface ShadowSource {
  readonly id: number;
  readonly spread: number;
  /** Density layer and channel (4 channels per layer). */
  readonly layer: number;
  readonly channel: number;
  /** Kernel radius (CSS px) and the per-particle weight `mass · k / (π R² / 3)`. */
  readonly radius: number;
  readonly weight: number;
  /** The particle bounds grown by the kernel radius (CSS px): where the source's density can be > 0. */
  readonly rect: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowRecord {
  readonly source: number;
  readonly dx: number;
  readonly dy: number;
  /** Premultiplied colour, 0–1. */
  readonly color: readonly [number, number, number, number];
  /** `rect` of the source shifted by the offset (CSS px): where the resolved layer can be > 0. */
  readonly rect: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowGroup {
  /** CSS px; 0: no blur passes. */
  readonly blur: number;
  readonly scale: number;
  /** `records[first] … records[first + count − 1]`. */
  readonly first: number;
  readonly count: number;
  /** The records' rects grown by 3σ = 1.5 × blur (CSS px): where the group's result can be > 0. */
  readonly reach: Rect;
}

export interface ShadowPlan {
  readonly sources: readonly ShadowSource[];
  readonly records: readonly ShadowRecord[];
  readonly groups: readonly ShadowGroup[];
  /** Slots whose shadows the frame draws, ascending. */
  readonly drawn: readonly number[];
  readonly densityLayers: number;
}

export const EMPTY_PLAN: ShadowPlan = Object.freeze({ sources: [], records: [], groups: [], drawn: [], densityLayers: 0 });

const grow = (r: Rect, d: number): Rect => ({ x0: r.x0 - d, y0: r.y0 - d, x1: r.x1 + d, y1: r.y1 + d });

/**
 * D88-6, D88-11: sources, records and groups for the casters of `frame`. `texelCss` is one shadow
 * texel in CSS px (the splat's floor, as SPLAT_WGSL's T0 texel).
 */
export function planShadows(casters: ShadowCasters, frame: RenderFrame, texelCss: number): ShadowPlan {
  if (casters.ids.length === 0) return EMPTY_PLAN;
  const sources: ShadowSource[] = [];
  const drawn: number[] = [];
  const exact: number[] = [];
  const byGroup = new Map<number, Array<Omit<ShadowRecord, "rect"> & { rect: Rect }>>();
  const groupOrder: number[] = [];
  for (const id of casters.ids) {
    const paint = frame.paints[id];
    const layers = paint?.shadows;
    if (!paint || !layers || layers.length === 0) continue;
    const spreads = [0];
    for (const l of layers) if (l.spread !== 0 && !spreads.includes(l.spread)) spreads.push(l.spread);
    if (sources.length + spreads.length > SHADOW_MAX_SOURCES) continue; // D88-11: no room this frame
    const base = Math.min(Math.max(kernelRadiusPx(paint.spacingPx), texelCss), KERNEL_RADIUS_CAP_PX);
    const mass = Number.isFinite(paint.areaPerParticle) && paint.areaPerParticle > 0 ? paint.areaPerParticle : 0;
    const b = casters.bounds;
    const bounds: Rect = { x0: b[4 * id] ?? 0, y0: b[4 * id + 1] ?? 0, x1: b[4 * id + 2] ?? 0, y1: b[4 * id + 3] ?? 0 };
    const sourceOf = new Map<number, number>();
    for (const spread of spreads) {
      const k = spreadKernel(base, spread);
      const s = sources.length;
      sourceOf.set(spread, s);
      sources.push({
        id,
        spread,
        layer: Math.floor(s / SHADOW_CHANNELS),
        channel: s % SHADOW_CHANNELS,
        radius: k.radius,
        weight: (mass * k.weightScale) / ((Math.PI * k.radius * k.radius) / 3),
        rect: grow(bounds, k.radius),
      });
    }
    drawn.push(id);
    // CSS paints the first layer on top: the last layer is recorded first, and OVER keeps that order within a
    // group. Across groups (and between the half- and quarter-resolution accumulators) the order is the
    // groups' creation order, so CSS layer order is kept only approximately when the layers' radii differ.
    for (let li = layers.length - 1; li >= 0; li--) {
      const l = layers[li];
      if (!l) continue;
      let key = groupBlur(l.blur);
      if (key > 0 && !exact.includes(key)) {
        if (exact.length < SHADOW_MAX_EXACT_GROUPS) exact.push(key);
        else key = nearestBucket(key);
      }
      let list = byGroup.get(key);
      if (!list) {
        list = [];
        byGroup.set(key, list);
        groupOrder.push(key);
      }
      const src = sourceOf.get(l.spread) ?? 0;
      const a = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, l.color[3]));
      const srcRect = sources[src]?.rect ?? bounds;
      list.push({
        source: src,
        dx: l.dx,
        dy: l.dy,
        color: [((l.color[0] / 255) * a), ((l.color[1] / 255) * a), ((l.color[2] / 255) * a), a],
        rect: { x0: srcRect.x0 + l.dx, y0: srcRect.y0 + l.dy, x1: srcRect.x1 + l.dx, y1: srcRect.y1 + l.dy },
      });
    }
  }
  const records: ShadowRecord[] = [];
  const groups: ShadowGroup[] = [];
  for (const key of groupOrder) {
    const list = byGroup.get(key) ?? [];
    if (list.length === 0) continue;
    const first = records.length;
    let reach: Rect | null = null;
    for (const r of list) {
      records.push(r);
      const g = grow(r.rect, 1.5 * key);
      reach = reach === null ? g : { x0: Math.min(reach.x0, g.x0), y0: Math.min(reach.y0, g.y0), x1: Math.max(reach.x1, g.x1), y1: Math.max(reach.y1, g.y1) };
    }
    groups.push({
      blur: key,
      scale: key >= SHADOW_QUARTER_FROM_PX ? SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER : SHADOW_SCALE_HALF,
      first,
      count: list.length,
      reach: reach ?? { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 0, y1: 0 },
    });
  }
  return { sources, records, groups, drawn, densityLayers: Math.ceil(sources.length / SHADOW_CHANNELS) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `renderers/shadow-canvas2d.ts`** (replaces the stub):
**`packages/core/ts/src/renderers/shadow-canvas2d.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-4, D88-5, D88-10, D88-12, D88-14): Canvas2D shadows, drawn before all liquid.
 *
 * Per caster (shadow-plan.ts `ShadowCasters`): its particles splat into an alpha mask at the density
 * grid's cell (the same kernel and 0.5 smoothstep as DensityGrid), put into a reusable canvas. Each
 * outer layer (last first: CSS paints the first on top) then draws the mask once with
 * `shadowColor`/`shadowBlur`/`shadowOffset`, the mask itself pushed off the canvas and the offset
 * compensating, so only the shadow lands (D88-5). Canvas shadows ignore the transform, so the blur
 * and both offsets are multiplied by the DPR (D88-14). Spread dilates the mask by 8 shifted draws
 * (erodes with `destination-in` when negative); a spread layer with blur 0 gets a blur floor of one
 * cell (D88-12). Last, the knockout (D88-10): every caster's closed coverage (its own liquid, plus
 * holes narrower than about two closing distances) goes into one grid-sized image that is drawn once
 * with `destination-out`, so no shadow lies under the casters' own liquid or shows through its holes.
 * Plan resolution A6: canvases from `document.createElement`, pixels from `createImageData`.
 */
import { Dyn } from "../fluid-layout";
import type { ShadowLayer } from "../shadow";
import type { RenderFrame } from "./frame";
import { DENSITY_THRESHOLD, EDGE_SOFTNESS, KERNEL_RADIUS_CAP_PX, kernelRadiusPx } from "./kernel-params";
import { KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL, ShadowCasters } from "./shadow-plan";

/** A mask never exceeds this many cells per side (a particle flung far would size it). */
export const MASK_MAX_CELLS = 2048;
const S = Math.SQRT1_2;
/** D88-5: the 8 dilation directions. */
export const DILATE_DIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = Object.freeze([
  [1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1], [S, S], [S, -S], [-S, S], [-S, -S],
]);
const CLOSE_AXES: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [[1, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1], [1, -1]];
const TRANSPARENT = "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)";

interface Surface {
  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
}

function surface(): Surface | null {
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  return ctx ? { canvas, ctx } : null;
}

const colourCache = new WeakMap<ShadowLayer, string>();
function cssColour(l: ShadowLayer): string {
  let s = colourCache.get(l);
  if (s === undefined) {
    const [r, g, b, a] = l.color;
    s = `rgba(${Math.round(r)}, ${Math.round(g)}, ${Math.round(b)}, ${a})`;
    colourCache.set(l, s);
  }
  return s;
}

export class ShadowCanvas2D {
  private readonly casters = new ShadowCasters();
  private drawnIds = new Uint8Array(0);
  private mask: Surface | null = null;
  private spreadSurface: Surface | null = null;
  private knock: Surface | null = null;
  private maskImg: ImageData | null = null;
  private knockImg: ImageData | null = null;
  private rho = new Float32Array(0);
  private cov = new Float32Array(0);
  private closed = new Float32Array(0);
  private broken = false;

  /** True when the last draw() drew slot `id`'s shadow (the toggle reads it). */
  drawn(id: number): boolean {
    return Number.isInteger(id) && id >= 0 && id < this.drawnIds.length && this.drawnIds[id] === 1;
  }

  /**
   * Draws every caster's shadow into `ctx` (DPR transform already set) and knocks out the casters'
   * own coverage. `cellPx` is the density grid's cell, `gridW × gridH` its size in cells.
   */
  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, frame: RenderFrame, cellPx: number, gridW: number, gridH: number): void {
    this.drawnIds.fill(0);
    if (this.broken) return;
    const n = this.casters.collect(frame);
    if (n === 0) return;
    if (!this.ensureSurfaces(gridW, gridH)) return;
    if (this.drawnIds.length < frame.paints.length) this.drawnIds = new Uint8Array(frame.paints.length);
    const dpr = Number.isFinite(frame.viewport.dpr) && frame.viewport.dpr > 0 ? frame.viewport.dpr : 1;
    let knocked = false;
    for (const id of this.casters.ids) {
      if (this.drawCaster(ctx, frame, id, cellPx, dpr, gridW, gridH)) {
        this.drawnIds[id] = 1;
        knocked = true;
      }
    }
    ctx.shadowColor = TRANSPARENT;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.shadowOffsetX = 0;
    ctx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
    if (!knocked) return;
    const knock = this.knock;
    const img = this.knockImg;
    if (!knock || !img) return;
    knock.ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
    ctx.drawImage(knock.canvas, 0, 0, gridW * cellPx, gridH * cellPx);
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
    img.data.fill(0); // ready for the next frame
  }

  destroy(): void {
    this.mask = null;
    this.spreadSurface = null;
    this.knock = null;
    this.maskImg = null;
    this.knockImg = null;
    this.drawnIds = new Uint8Array(0);
  }

  private ensureSurfaces(gridW: number, gridH: number): boolean {
    this.mask ??= surface();
    this.spreadSurface ??= surface();
    this.knock ??= surface();
    if (!this.mask || !this.spreadSurface || !this.knock) {
      this.broken = true; // no 2d context for a scratch canvas: no renderer shadows (the toggle keeps the browser's)
      return false;
    }
    if (!this.knockImg || this.knockImg.width !== gridW || this.knockImg.height !== gridH) {
      this.knock.canvas.width = gridW;
      this.knock.canvas.height = gridH;
      this.knockImg = this.knock.ctx.createImageData(gridW, gridH);
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** One caster: its mask, its layers, its knockout cells. False when it has nothing to draw. */
  private drawCaster(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, frame: RenderFrame, id: number, s: number, dpr: number, gridW: number, gridH: number): boolean {
    const paint = frame.paints[id];
    const layers = paint?.shadows;
    const mask = this.mask;
    if (!paint || !layers || layers.length === 0 || !mask) return false;
    const R = Math.min(Math.max(kernelRadiusPx(paint.spacingPx), s), KERNEL_RADIUS_CAP_PX);
    const b = this.casters.bounds;
    const x0 = (b[4 * id] ?? 0) - R;
    const y0 = (b[4 * id + 1] ?? 0) - R;
    const x1 = (b[4 * id + 2] ?? 0) + R;
    const y1 = (b[4 * id + 3] ?? 0) + R;
    const ox = Math.floor(x0 / s);
    const oy = Math.floor(y0 / s);
    const mw = Math.min(MASK_MAX_CELLS, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(x1 / s) - ox + 1));
    const mh = Math.min(MASK_MAX_CELLS, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(y1 / s) - oy + 1));
    this.splatMask(frame, id, R, s, ox, oy, mw, mh);
    this.putMask(mw, mh);
    for (let li = layers.length - 1; li >= 0; li--) {
      const l = layers[li];
      if (l) this.drawLayer(ctx, l, s, dpr, ox, oy, mw, mh);
    }
    this.knockOut(R, s, ox, oy, mw, mh, gridW, gridH);
    return true;
  }

  private splatMask(frame: RenderFrame, id: number, R: number, s: number, ox: number, oy: number, mw: number, mh: number): void {
    const size = mw * mh;
    if (this.rho.length < size) {
      this.rho = new Float32Array(size);
      this.cov = new Float32Array(size);
      this.closed = new Float32Array(size);
    }
    const rho = this.rho;
    rho.fill(0, 0, size);
    const paint = frame.paints[id];
    const mass = paint && Number.isFinite(paint.areaPerParticle) && paint.areaPerParticle > 0 ? paint.areaPerParticle : 0;
    const R2 = R * R;
    const k = mass / ((Math.PI * R2) / 3);
    const cap = frame.particleCapacity;
    const dyn = frame.dynamicView;
    const { order, start, count } = this.casters;
    const end = (start[id] ?? 0) + (count[id] ?? 0);
    for (let j = start[id] ?? 0; j < end; j++) {
      const i = order[j] ?? 0;
      const px = (dyn[Dyn.X * cap + i] as number) - ox * s;
      const py = (dyn[Dyn.Y * cap + i] as number) - oy * s;
      const ix0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((px - R) / s - 0.5));
      const ix1 = Math.min(mw - 1, Math.ceil((px + R) / s - 0.5));
      const iy0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((py - R) / s - 0.5));
      const iy1 = Math.min(mh - 1, Math.ceil((py + R) / s - 0.5));
      for (let iy = iy0; iy <= iy1; iy++) {
        const dy = (iy + 0.5) * s - py;
        const row = iy * mw;
        for (let ix = ix0; ix <= ix1; ix++) {
          const dx = (ix + 0.5) * s - px;
          const q = 1 - (dx * dx + dy * dy) / R2;
          if (q > 0) rho[row + ix] += k * q * q;
        }
      }
    }
    const lo = DENSITY_THRESHOLD - EDGE_SOFTNESS;
    const span = 2 * EDGE_SOFTNESS;
    const cov = this.cov;
    for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) {
      const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, ((rho[i] ?? 0) - lo) / span));
      cov[i] = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
    }
  }

  /** The coverage as white with alpha into the mask canvas (top-left mw × mh). */
  private putMask(mw: number, mh: number): void {
    const mask = this.mask;
    if (!mask) return;
    if (!this.maskImg || this.maskImg.width < mw || this.maskImg.height < mh) {
      const w = Math.max(mw, this.maskImg?.width ?? 0);
      const h = Math.max(mh, this.maskImg?.height ?? 0);
      mask.canvas.width = w;
      mask.canvas.height = h;
      this.maskImg = mask.ctx.createImageData(w, h);
    }
    const img = this.maskImg;
    const W = img.width;
    const d = img.data;
    for (let iy = 0; iy < mh; iy++) {
      for (let ix = 0; ix < mw; ix++) {
        const p = (iy * W + ix) * 4;
        d[p] = 255;
        d[p + 1] = 255;
        d[p + 2] = 255;
        d[p + 3] = (this.cov[iy * mw + ix] ?? 0) * 255;
      }
    }
    mask.ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0, 0, 0, mw, mh);
  }

  private drawLayer(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, l: ShadowLayer, s: number, dpr: number, ox: number, oy: number, mw: number, mh: number): void {
    const mask = this.mask;
    if (!mask) return;
    let src: HTMLCanvasElement = mask.canvas;
    let sw = mw;
    let sh = mh;
    let pad = 0;
    if (l.spread !== 0 && this.spreadSurface) {
      pad = Math.ceil(Math.abs(l.spread) / s) + 1;
      sw = mw + 2 * pad;
      sh = mh + 2 * pad;
      const sp = this.spreadSurface;
      if (sp.canvas.width < sw || sp.canvas.height < sh) {
        sp.canvas.width = Math.max(sw, sp.canvas.width);
        sp.canvas.height = Math.max(sh, sp.canvas.height);
      }
      const r = Math.abs(l.spread) / s;
      sp.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, sp.canvas.width, sp.canvas.height);
      sp.ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
      sp.ctx.drawImage(mask.canvas, 0, 0, mw, mh, pad, pad, mw, mh);
      // D88-5: dilate (union of 8 shifted copies) or, for a negative spread, erode (their intersection).
      sp.ctx.globalCompositeOperation = l.spread > 0 ? "source-over" : "destination-in";
      for (const [ux, uy] of DILATE_DIRS) sp.ctx.drawImage(mask.canvas, 0, 0, mw, mh, pad + ux * r, pad + uy * r, mw, mh);
      sp.ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
      src = sp.canvas;
    }
    const dx = (ox - pad) * s;
    const dy = (oy - pad) * s;
    const dw = sw * s;
    const dh = sh * s;
    // D88-5: the source lands off the canvas (its right edge at x = −1); the offset brings only the shadow back.
    const far = dx + dw + 1;
    const blur = l.blur > 0 ? l.blur : l.spread !== 0 ? s : 0; // D88-12: one cell for a hard spread edge
    ctx.shadowColor = cssColour(l);
    ctx.shadowBlur = blur * dpr; // D88-14: canvas shadows ignore setTransform
    ctx.shadowOffsetX = (far + l.dx) * dpr;
    ctx.shadowOffsetY = l.dy * dpr;
    ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0, sw, sh, dx - far, dy, dw, dh);
  }

  /** D88-10: max(coverage, closed coverage) into the knockout image at the caster's cells. */
  private knockOut(R: number, s: number, ox: number, oy: number, mw: number, mh: number, gridW: number, gridH: number): void {
    const img = this.knockImg;
    if (!img) return;
    const cov = this.cov;
    const closed = this.closed;
    const full = Math.max(1, Math.round((KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * R) / s));
    const half = Math.max(1, Math.round(full / 2));
    const at = (ix: number, iy: number): number => (ix < 0 || iy < 0 || ix >= mw || iy >= mh ? 0 : (cov[iy * mw + ix] ?? 0));
    for (let iy = 0; iy < mh; iy++) {
      for (let ix = 0; ix < mw; ix++) {
        let k = cov[iy * mw + ix] ?? 0;
        // A cell between two covered cells (± the closing distance or half of it, on four axes) is covered.
        for (let pass = 0; k < 1 && pass < 2; pass++) {
          const d = pass === 0 ? full : half;
          for (const [ax, ay] of CLOSE_AXES) {
            const bridge = Math.min(at(ix + ax * d, iy + ay * d), at(ix - ax * d, iy - ay * d));
            if (bridge > k) k = bridge;
          }
        }
        closed[iy * mw + ix] = k;
      }
    }
    const d = img.data;
    for (let iy = 0; iy < mh; iy++) {
      const gy = oy + iy;
      if (gy < 0 || gy >= gridH) continue;
      for (let ix = 0; ix < mw; ix++) {
        const gx = ox + ix;
        if (gx < 0 || gx >= gridW) continue;
        const a = (closed[iy * mw + ix] ?? 0) * 255;
        const p = (gy * gridW + gx) * 4 + 3;
        if (a > (d[p] ?? 0)) d[p] = a;
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### Task W88.4: WebGPU shadows (D88-6, D88-10, D88-11, D88-13)

**Files:** Modify `renderers/webgpu/shaders.ts` and `renderers/webgpu/webgpu-renderer.ts`. Replace the stub `renderers/webgpu/shadow-passes.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 1: `shaders.ts`**: the five shadow shaders. `SHADOW_PASS_WGSL` mirrors `SP` in `shadow-passes.ts` (128 bytes).
```diff
--- a/packages/core/ts/src/renderers/webgpu/shaders.ts
+++ b/packages/core/ts/src/renderers/webgpu/shaders.ts
@@ -4,7 +4,8 @@

  * mirror gpu-buffers.ts (`Element`, 64 bytes, ELEMENT_GPU_FLOATS) and webgpu-renderer.ts
  * (`View`, 32 bytes, VIEW_UNIFORM_FLOATS). W74 adds the text pass (TEXT_WGSL into T1/T1w) and the
- * text composite (TEXT_COMPOSITE_WGSL); the splat reads the 32-byte particle record.
+ * text composite (TEXT_COMPOSITE_WGSL); the splat reads the 32-byte particle record. W88 adds the
+ * shadow shaders (SHADOW_DENSITY/RESOLVE/BLUR/KNOCKOUT/COMPOSITE_WGSL, shadow-passes.ts).

   let coverage = select(smoothstep(DENSITY_THRESHOLD - soft, DENSITY_THRESHOLD + soft, sum_w), 0.0, mixed);

+`;
+
+// ---- W88 (D88-6, D88-10, D88-11, D88-13): shadows -----------------------------------------------
+
+/** W88: the blur loop's cap, in taps per side. */
+export const SHADOW_MAX_TAPS = 64;
+
+/**
+ * W88 (D88-11): one 128-byte record per shadow pass at a 256-byte dynamic offset
+ * (shadow-passes.ts SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS, SP): blur taps, the density source, the knockout's closing
+ * distance and channel masks, the composite's accumulator flags.
+ */
+const SHADOW_PASS_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+struct ShadowPass {
+  target_px: vec2f,
+  dir: vec2f,
+  sigma: f32,
+  taps: f32,
+  element: f32,
+  kernel_r: f32,
+  weight: f32,
+  close: f32,
+  layers: f32,
+  flags: f32,
+  mask: vec4f,
+  masks: array<vec4f, 4>,
+}
+`;
+
+const FULL_TRIANGLE_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+@vertex
+fn vs(@builtin(vertex_index) vi: u32) -> @builtin(position) vec4f {
+  var p = array<vec2f, 3>(vec2f(-1.0, -3.0), vec2f(3.0, 1.0), vec2f(-1.0, 1.0));
+  return vec4f(p[vi % 3u], 0.0, 1.0);
+}
+`;
+
+/**
+ * Shadow pass 1 (W88, D88-11): one density splat per source (element and distinct spread), without
+ * an offset, into its channel of a shadow density layer (rgba16float, half resolution, additive). The
+ * kernel is the liquid's (spread 0) or the widened, mass-scaled one of shadow-plan.ts spreadKernel;
+ * a particle of another element is a degenerate quad. Parked particles were packed off-screen.
+ */
+export const SHADOW_DENSITY_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+${VIEW_WGSL}
+${QUAD_WGSL}
+${PARTICLE_WGSL}
+${SHADOW_PASS_WGSL}
+
+@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> view: View;
+@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> sp: ShadowPass;
+@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage, read> particles: array<Particle>;
+@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage, read> homes: array<i32>;
+
+struct DensityVarying {
+  @builtin(position) pos: vec4f,
+  @location(0) local: vec2f,
+}
+
+@vertex
+fn vs(@builtin(vertex_index) vi: u32, @builtin(instance_index) ii: u32) -> DensityVarying {
+  var out: DensityVarying;
+  out.pos = DEGENERATE;
+  out.local = vec2f(0.0, 0.0);
+  if (homes[ii] != i32(sp.element)) {
+    return out;
+  }
+  let corner = quad_corner(vi) * sp.kernel_r;
+  out.pos = css_to_clip(particles[ii].a.xy + corner, view.size_css);
+  out.local = corner;
+  return out;
+}
+
+@fragment
+fn fs(in: DensityVarying) -> @location(0) vec4f {
+  let q = 1.0 - dot(in.local, in.local) / max(sp.kernel_r * sp.kernel_r, 1e-6);
+  if (q <= 0.0) {
+    discard;
+  }
+  return sp.mask * (sp.weight * q * q);
+}
+`;
+
+/**
+ * Shadow pass 2 (W88, D88-11): one scissored pass per radius group. One quad per layer record; the
+ * layer's offset is applied here, by sampling its source's density at (position − offset), and the
+ * 0.5 threshold with the liquid's edge gives the coverage; premultiplied colour, blended over.
+ */
+export const SHADOW_RESOLVE_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+${VIEW_WGSL}
+${QUAD_WGSL}
+const DENSITY_THRESHOLD: f32 = ${f32(DENSITY_THRESHOLD)};
+const EDGE_SOFTNESS: f32 = ${f32(EDGE_SOFTNESS)};
+
+struct ShadowRecord {
+  rect: vec4f,
+  offset: vec2f,
+  src: vec2f,
+  color: vec4f,
+}
+
+@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> view: View;
+@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> records: array<ShadowRecord>;
+@group(0) @binding(2) var density: texture_2d_array<f32>;
+@group(0) @binding(3) var density_sampler: sampler;
+
+struct ResolveVarying {
+  @builtin(position) pos: vec4f,
+  @location(0) css: vec2f,
+  @location(1) @interpolate(flat) index: u32,
+}
+
+@vertex
+fn vs(@builtin(vertex_index) vi: u32, @builtin(instance_index) ii: u32) -> ResolveVarying {
+  var out: ResolveVarying;
+  let r = records[ii];
+  let t = quad_corner(vi) * 0.5 + vec2f(0.5, 0.5);
+  let p = mix(r.rect.xy, r.rect.zw, t);
+  out.pos = css_to_clip(p, view.size_css);
+  out.css = p;
+  out.index = ii;
+  return out;
 }
+
+@fragment
+fn fs(in: ResolveVarying) -> @location(0) vec4f {
+  let r = records[in.index];
+  let uv = (in.css - r.offset) / max(view.size_css, vec2f(1.0, 1.0));
+  let d4 = textureSampleLevel(density, density_sampler, uv, i32(r.src.x), 0.0);
+  let d = d4[min(u32(r.src.y), 3u)];
+  let coverage = smoothstep(DENSITY_THRESHOLD - EDGE_SOFTNESS, DENSITY_THRESHOLD + EDGE_SOFTNESS, d);
+  return r.color * coverage;
+}
 `;
+
+/**
+ * Shadow pass 3 (W88, D88-6): a separable gaussian, σ = blur / 2 CSS px (the CSS definition) in the
+ * group target's px, one direction per pass (dir), at most SHADOW_MAX_TAPS taps per side; the
+ * horizontal pass writes the temporary target, the vertical one blends over into the accumulator.
+ */
+export const SHADOW_BLUR_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+${SHADOW_PASS_WGSL}
+const MAX_TAPS: i32 = ${SHADOW_MAX_TAPS};
+
+@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> sp: ShadowPass;
+@group(0) @binding(1) var source: texture_2d<f32>;
+${FULL_TRIANGLE_WGSL}
+@fragment
+fn fs(@builtin(position) frag: vec4f) -> @location(0) vec4f {
+  let size = vec2i(textureDimensions(source));
+  let p = vec2i(frag.xy);
+  let n = min(i32(sp.taps), MAX_TAPS);
+  let s2 = max(sp.sigma * sp.sigma, 1e-6);
+  let axis = vec2i(sp.dir);
+  var acc = vec4f(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+  var wsum = 0.0;
+  for (var i = -n; i <= n; i++) {
+    let f = f32(i);
+    let w = exp(-0.5 * f * f / s2);
+    let q = clamp(p + axis * i, vec2i(0, 0), size - vec2i(1, 1));
+    acc += textureLoad(source, q, 0) * w;
+    wsum += w;
+  }
+  return acc / max(wsum, 1e-6);
+}
+`;
+
+/**
+ * Shadow pass 4 (W88, D88-10): the knockout. Per texel the closed coverage of the spread-0 density
+ * channels (each element's own liquid): its coverage, or the smaller of two coverages at ± the
+ * closing distance (and half of it) along four axes, so a hole narrower than about twice that
+ * distance counts as covered while a straight outer edge stays where it is. r8unorm, half resolution.
+ */
+export const SHADOW_KNOCKOUT_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+${SHADOW_PASS_WGSL}
+const DENSITY_THRESHOLD: f32 = ${f32(DENSITY_THRESHOLD)};
+const EDGE_SOFTNESS: f32 = ${f32(EDGE_SOFTNESS)};
+
+@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> sp: ShadowPass;
+@group(0) @binding(1) var density: texture_2d_array<f32>;
+${FULL_TRIANGLE_WGSL}
+fn coverage(p: vec2i, size: vec2i, layer: i32) -> vec4f {
+  if (any(p < vec2i(0, 0)) || any(p >= size)) {
+    return vec4f(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+  }
+  let lo = vec4f(DENSITY_THRESHOLD - EDGE_SOFTNESS);
+  let hi = vec4f(DENSITY_THRESHOLD + EDGE_SOFTNESS);
+  return smoothstep(lo, hi, textureLoad(density, p, layer, 0));
+}
+
+@fragment
+fn fs(@builtin(position) frag: vec4f) -> @location(0) vec4f {
+  let size = vec2i(textureDimensions(density).xy);
+  let p = vec2i(frag.xy);
+  let d_full = max(i32(sp.close), 1);
+  let d_half = max(d_full / 2, 1);
+  var axes = array<vec2i, 4>(vec2i(1, 0), vec2i(0, 1), vec2i(1, 1), vec2i(1, -1));
+  var k = 0.0;
+  let n = min(i32(sp.layers), 4);
+  for (var l = 0; l < n; l++) {
+    var best = coverage(p, size, l);
+    for (var a = 0; a < 4; a++) {
+      let ax = axes[a];
+      best = max(best, min(coverage(p + ax * d_full, size, l), coverage(p - ax * d_full, size, l)));
+      best = max(best, min(coverage(p + ax * d_half, size, l), coverage(p - ax * d_half, size, l)));
+    }
+    let m = best * sp.masks[l];
+    k = max(k, max(max(m.x, m.y), max(m.z, m.w)));
+  }
+  return vec4f(k, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
+}
+`;
+
+/**
+ * Shadow pass 5 (W88, D88-4, D88-10): the first draw of the screen pass, under all liquid: the half-
+ * and quarter-resolution accumulators (flags bit 0 and bit 1: written this frame), over each other,
+ * times 1 − knockout; blended over. The liquid composite after it blends over too (D88-13).
+ */
+export const SHADOW_COMPOSITE_WGSL = /* wgsl */ `
+${VIEW_WGSL}
+${SHADOW_PASS_WGSL}
+
+@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> view: View;
+@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> sp: ShadowPass;
+@group(0) @binding(2) var half_acc: texture_2d<f32>;
+@group(0) @binding(3) var quarter_acc: texture_2d<f32>;
+@group(0) @binding(4) var knockout: texture_2d<f32>;
+@group(0) @binding(5) var shadow_sampler: sampler;
+${FULL_TRIANGLE_WGSL}
+@fragment
+fn fs(@builtin(position) frag: vec4f) -> @location(0) vec4f {
+  let uv = frag.xy / max(view.size_px, vec2f(1.0, 1.0));
+  let flags = u32(sp.flags);
+  let h = textureSample(half_acc, shadow_sampler, uv) * f32(flags & 1u);
+  let q = textureSample(quarter_acc, shadow_sampler, uv) * f32((flags >> 1u) & 1u);
+  let shadow = h + q * (1.0 - h.a);
+  let k = textureSample(knockout, shadow_sampler, uv).r;
+  return shadow * (1.0 - k);
+}
+`;
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `shadow-passes.ts`** (replaces the stub):
**`packages/core/ts/src/renderers/webgpu/shadow-passes.ts`**
```ts
/**
 * W88 (D88-6, D88-10, D88-11): the WebGPU shadow passes, encoded between the liquid splat and the
 * screen pass. Per frame with casters (shadow-plan.ts):
 * 1. one density pass per used layer of the density array (rgba16float, half resolution, 4 sources
 *    per layer), one scissored draw per source;
 * 2. per radius group: a resolve pass (one quad per layer record, the offset applied in the
 *    resolve), then — blur > 0 — a horizontal and a vertical gaussian pass; the vertical one (or the
 *    resolve, for blur 0) blends over into the group's accumulator (half or quarter resolution);
 * 3. one knockout pass (r8unorm, half resolution): the casters' closed own coverage;
 * 4. in the screen pass, before all liquid: the accumulators × (1 − knockout).
 * Every pass is scissored to its rect, clamped to its target (Review Focus 4). Targets are created on
 * the first frame with shadows and kept (like T1); a resize drops them. An element at rest, without
 * outer layers, or past the source cap costs nothing here. No DOM.
 */
import type { RenderFrame } from "../frame";
import {
  EMPTY_PLAN,
  planShadows,
  type Rect,
  SHADOW_CHANNELS,
  SHADOW_SCALE_HALF,
  SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER,
  ShadowCasters,
  type ShadowPlan,
} from "../shadow-plan";
import { KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL } from "../shadow-plan";
import { SHADOW_MAX_TAPS } from "./shaders";

/** Floats per ShadowPass record (WGSL `struct ShadowPass`, 128 bytes) and per 256-byte slot. */
export const SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS = 32;
export const SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS = 64;
export const SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES = SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS * 4;
/** Slots per frame: ≤ 16 sources + 2 per group (≤ 11 groups) + knockout + composite. */
export const SHADOW_MAX_SLOTS = 64;
/** Offsets in a ShadowPass record. */
export const SP = {
  TARGET_W: 0, TARGET_H: 1, DIR_X: 2, DIR_Y: 3, SIGMA: 4, TAPS: 5, ELEMENT: 6, KERNEL_R: 7, WEIGHT: 8, CLOSE: 9, LAYERS: 10, FLAGS: 11, MASK: 12, MASKS: 16,
} as const;
/** Floats per ShadowRecord (WGSL: rect, offset, src, colour = 48 bytes). */
export const SHADOW_RECORD_FLOATS = 12;
/** Records per frame: 16 sources × 4 layers. */
export const SHADOW_MAX_RECORDS = 64;
export const SHADOW_DENSITY_FORMAT: GPUTextureFormat = "rgba16float";
export const SHADOW_ACC_FORMAT: GPUTextureFormat = "rgba16float";
export const SHADOW_KNOCKOUT_FORMAT: GPUTextureFormat = "r8unorm";

/** The shadow pipelines and layouts (built at init with the liquid's, in its validation scope). */
export interface ShadowPipelines {
  readonly shadowDensityLayout: GPUBindGroupLayout;
  readonly shadowResolveLayout: GPUBindGroupLayout;
  readonly shadowBlurLayout: GPUBindGroupLayout;
  readonly shadowKnockoutLayout: GPUBindGroupLayout;
  readonly shadowCompositeLayout: GPUBindGroupLayout;
  readonly shadowDensity: GPURenderPipeline;
  readonly shadowResolve: GPURenderPipeline;
  readonly shadowBlurH: GPURenderPipeline;
  readonly shadowBlurV: GPURenderPipeline;
  readonly shadowKnockout: GPURenderPipeline;
  readonly shadowComposite: GPURenderPipeline;
}

const CLEAR: GPUColorDict = { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0 };

interface ScaleTargets {
  readonly scale: number;
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly resolve: GPUTexture;
  readonly temp: GPUTexture;
  readonly acc: GPUTexture;
  readonly resolveView: GPUTextureView;
  readonly tempView: GPUTextureView;
  readonly accView: GPUTextureView;
  /** Bind groups for the blur passes: H reads `resolve`, V reads `temp`. */
  readonly blurFromResolve: GPUBindGroup;
  readonly blurFromTemp: GPUBindGroup;
}

interface Targets {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly layers: number;
  readonly density: GPUTexture;
  readonly densityLayerViews: readonly GPUTextureView[];
  readonly densityArrayView: GPUTextureView;
  readonly knockout: GPUTexture;
  readonly knockoutView: GPUTextureView;
  readonly half: ScaleTargets;
  readonly quarter: ScaleTargets;
}

const clampInt = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

/** `r` (CSS px) in a target of `w × h` px at `pxPerCss`: integers inside the target; null when empty. */
export function scissorOf(r: Rect, pxPerCss: number, w: number, h: number): [number, number, number, number] | null {
  if (![r.x0, r.y0, r.x1, r.y1].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;
  const x0 = clampInt(Math.floor(r.x0 * pxPerCss), 0, w);
  const y0 = clampInt(Math.floor(r.y0 * pxPerCss), 0, h);
  const x1 = clampInt(Math.ceil(r.x1 * pxPerCss), 0, w);
  const y1 = clampInt(Math.ceil(r.y1 * pxPerCss), 0, h);
  return x1 > x0 && y1 > y0 ? [x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0] : null;
}

export class ShadowPasses {
  private readonly casters = new ShadowCasters();
  private plan: ShadowPlan = EMPTY_PLAN;
  private drawnIds = new Uint8Array(0);
  private targets: Targets | null = null;
  private paramsBuffer: GPUBuffer | null = null;
  private recordsBuffer: GPUBuffer | null = null;
  private readonly params = new Float32Array(SHADOW_MAX_SLOTS * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
  private readonly records = new Float32Array(SHADOW_MAX_RECORDS * SHADOW_RECORD_FLOATS);
  private densityGroup: GPUBindGroup | null = null;
  private densityGroupFor: { particles: GPUBuffer; homes: GPUBuffer } | null = null;
  private resolveGroup: GPUBindGroup | null = null;
  private knockoutGroup: GPUBindGroup | null = null;
  private compositeGroup: GPUBindGroup | null = null;
  private dpr = 1;
  private compositeSlot = 0;
  private knockoutSlot = 0;
  private knockoutRect: Rect | null = null;
  private closePx = 1;
  private readonly sourceSlots: number[] = [];
  private readonly groupSlots: number[] = [];

  constructor(
    private readonly device: GPUDevice,
    private readonly pipelines: ShadowPipelines,
    private readonly sampler: GPUSampler,
    private readonly viewBuffer: GPUBuffer,
  ) {}

  /** True when the last prepared frame drew slot `id`'s shadow (the runtime's toggle reads it). */
  drawn(id: number): boolean {
    return Number.isInteger(id) && id >= 0 && id < this.drawnIds.length && this.drawnIds[id] === 1;
  }

  /** Called first in every render(): nothing is drawn until prepare() says so. */
  resetDrawn(): void {
    this.drawnIds.fill(0);
  }

  /**
   * Plans the frame's shadows, sizes the targets and writes the pass records and layer records.
   * False (nothing to encode) without casters. `widthPx × heightPx` is the canvas backing size.
   */
  prepare(frame: RenderFrame, widthPx: number, heightPx: number, particles: GPUBuffer, homes: GPUBuffer, maxDim: number): boolean {
    this.plan = EMPTY_PLAN;
    if (this.casters.collect(frame) === 0) return false;
    const dpr = Number.isFinite(frame.viewport.dpr) && frame.viewport.dpr > 0 ? frame.viewport.dpr : 1;
    this.dpr = dpr;
    const plan = planShadows(this.casters, frame, 1 / (dpr * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF));
    if (plan.drawn.length === 0 || plan.records.length === 0) return false;
    this.ensureTargets(widthPx, heightPx, plan.densityLayers, maxDim);
    this.ensureBuffers();
    const t = this.targets;
    if (!t || !this.paramsBuffer || !this.recordsBuffer) return false;
    this.ensureGroups(particles, homes);
    this.plan = plan;
    this.writeParams(plan, t);
    this.writeRecords(plan);
    if (this.drawnIds.length < frame.paints.length) this.drawnIds = new Uint8Array(frame.paints.length);
    for (const id of plan.drawn) this.drawnIds[id] = 1;
    return true;
  }

  /** Passes 1–3 (density, per group resolve/blur, knockout). After prepare() returned true. */
  encode(encoder: GPUCommandEncoder, count: number): void {
    const t = this.targets;
    const plan = this.plan;
    const p = this.pipelines;
    if (!t || plan.records.length === 0 || !this.densityGroup || !this.resolveGroup || !this.knockoutGroup) return;
    // 1. Density: one pass per layer, one scissored draw per source.
    for (let layer = 0; layer < plan.densityLayers; layer++) {
      const view = t.densityLayerViews[layer];
      if (!view) continue;
      const pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({
        label: `liquiddom shadow density ${layer}`,
        colorAttachments: [{ view, clearValue: CLEAR, loadOp: "clear", storeOp: "store" }],
      });
      pass.setPipeline(p.shadowDensity);
      plan.sources.forEach((src, s) => {
        if (src.layer !== layer) return;
        const sc = scissorOf(src.rect, this.dpr * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, t.width, t.height);
        if (!sc) return;
        pass.setScissorRect(...sc);
        pass.setBindGroup(0, this.densityGroup!, [(this.sourceSlots[s] ?? 0) * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES]);
        pass.draw(6, count);
      });
      pass.end();
    }
    // 2. Groups: resolve (+ blur H, blur V) into the accumulators, over.
    let halfWritten = false;
    let quarterWritten = false;
    plan.groups.forEach((g, gi) => {
      const st = g.scale === SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER ? t.quarter : t.half;
      const sc = scissorOf(g.reach, this.dpr * st.scale, st.width, st.height);
      if (!sc) return;
      const first = st === t.quarter ? !quarterWritten : !halfWritten;
      if (st === t.quarter) quarterWritten = true;
      else halfWritten = true;
      const accLoad: GPULoadOp = first ? "clear" : "load";
      const blurred = g.blur > 0;
      const resolve = encoder.beginRenderPass({
        label: "liquiddom shadow resolve",
        colorAttachments: [{ view: blurred ? st.resolveView : st.accView, clearValue: CLEAR, loadOp: blurred ? "clear" : accLoad, storeOp: "store" }],
      });
      resolve.setPipeline(p.shadowResolve);
      resolve.setScissorRect(...sc);
      resolve.setBindGroup(0, this.resolveGroup!);
      resolve.draw(6, g.count, 0, g.first);
      resolve.end();
      if (!blurred) return;
      const slot = this.groupSlots[gi] ?? 0;
      const h = encoder.beginRenderPass({
        label: "liquiddom shadow blur h",
        colorAttachments: [{ view: st.tempView, clearValue: CLEAR, loadOp: "clear", storeOp: "store" }],
      });
      h.setPipeline(p.shadowBlurH);
      h.setScissorRect(...sc);
      h.setBindGroup(0, st.blurFromResolve, [slot * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES]);
      h.draw(3);
      h.end();
      const v = encoder.beginRenderPass({
        label: "liquiddom shadow blur v",
        colorAttachments: [{ view: st.accView, clearValue: CLEAR, loadOp: accLoad, storeOp: "store" }],
      });
      v.setPipeline(p.shadowBlurV);
      v.setScissorRect(...sc);
      v.setBindGroup(0, st.blurFromTemp, [(slot + 1) * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES]);
      v.draw(3);
      v.end();
    });
    // The composite reads only the accumulators written this frame (flags).
    this.params[this.compositeSlot * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS + SP.FLAGS] = (halfWritten ? 1 : 0) + (quarterWritten ? 2 : 0);
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(this.paramsBuffer!, this.compositeSlot * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES, this.params, this.compositeSlot * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS, SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS);
    // 3. Knockout: the casters' closed own coverage.
    const knock = encoder.beginRenderPass({
      label: "liquiddom shadow knockout",
      colorAttachments: [{ view: t.knockoutView, clearValue: CLEAR, loadOp: "clear", storeOp: "store" }],
    });
    const ksc = this.knockoutRect ? scissorOf(this.knockoutRect, this.dpr * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, t.width, t.height) : null;
    if (ksc) {
      knock.setPipeline(p.shadowKnockout);
      knock.setScissorRect(...ksc);
      knock.setBindGroup(0, this.knockoutGroup, [this.knockoutSlot * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES]);
      knock.draw(3);
    }
    knock.end();
  }

  /** Pass 4, the first draw of the screen pass (D88-4): under all liquid. */
  drawComposite(screen: GPURenderPassEncoder): void {
    if (!this.compositeGroup || this.plan.records.length === 0) return;
    screen.setPipeline(this.pipelines.shadowComposite);
    screen.setBindGroup(0, this.compositeGroup, [this.compositeSlot * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES]);
    screen.draw(3);
  }

  /** A new backing size: the targets are rebuilt on the next frame with shadows. */
  resize(): void {
    this.releaseTargets();
  }

  destroy(): void {
    this.releaseTargets();
    this.paramsBuffer?.destroy();
    this.recordsBuffer?.destroy();
    this.paramsBuffer = null;
    this.recordsBuffer = null;
    this.densityGroup = null;
    this.densityGroupFor = null;
    this.resolveGroup = null;
    this.knockoutGroup = null;
    this.compositeGroup = null;
    this.plan = EMPTY_PLAN;
    this.drawnIds = new Uint8Array(0);
  }

  private releaseTargets(): void {
    const t = this.targets;
    if (!t) return;
    for (const tex of [t.density, t.knockout, t.half.resolve, t.half.temp, t.half.acc, t.quarter.resolve, t.quarter.temp, t.quarter.acc]) tex.destroy();
    this.targets = null;
    this.resolveGroup = null;
    this.knockoutGroup = null;
    this.compositeGroup = null;
  }

  private ensureBuffers(): void {
    const usage = GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST;
    this.paramsBuffer ??= this.device.createBuffer({ label: "liquiddom shadow params", size: SHADOW_MAX_SLOTS * SHADOW_SLOT_BYTES, usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | usage });
    this.recordsBuffer ??= this.device.createBuffer({ label: "liquiddom shadow records", size: SHADOW_MAX_RECORDS * SHADOW_RECORD_FLOATS * 4, usage: GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | usage });
  }

  private scaleTargets(scale: number, widthPx: number, heightPx: number, maxDim: number): ScaleTargets {
    const width = Math.min(maxDim, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(widthPx * scale)));
    const height = Math.min(maxDim, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(heightPx * scale)));
    const usage = GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT | GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING;
    const name = scale === SHADOW_SCALE_HALF ? "half" : "quarter";
    const make = (role: string): GPUTexture =>
      this.device.createTexture({ label: `liquiddom shadow ${role} ${name}`, size: [width, height], format: SHADOW_ACC_FORMAT, usage });
    const resolve = make("resolve");
    const temp = make("temp");
    const acc = make("acc");
    const resolveView = resolve.createView();
    const tempView = temp.createView();
    const blur = (view: GPUTextureView): GPUBindGroup =>
      this.device.createBindGroup({
        label: "liquiddom shadow blur",
        layout: this.pipelines.shadowBlurLayout,
        entries: [
          { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: this.paramsBuffer!, size: SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS * 4 } },
          { binding: 1, resource: view },
        ],
      });
    return {
      scale, width, height, resolve, temp, acc, resolveView, tempView, accView: acc.createView(),
      blurFromResolve: blur(resolveView),
      blurFromTemp: blur(tempView),
    };
  }

  private ensureTargets(widthPx: number, heightPx: number, layers: number, maxDim: number): void {
    const width = Math.min(maxDim, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(widthPx * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF)));
    const height = Math.min(maxDim, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(heightPx * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF)));
    const t = this.targets;
    if (t && t.width === width && t.height === height && t.layers >= layers) return;
    const keepLayers = Math.max(layers, t && t.width === width && t.height === height ? t.layers : 0);
    this.releaseTargets();
    this.ensureBuffers();
    const usage = GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT | GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING;
    const density = this.device.createTexture({ label: "liquiddom shadow density", size: [width, height, keepLayers], format: SHADOW_DENSITY_FORMAT, usage });
    const densityLayerViews = Array.from({ length: keepLayers }, (_, l) => density.createView({ dimension: "2d", baseArrayLayer: l, arrayLayerCount: 1 }));
    const densityArrayView = density.createView({ dimension: "2d-array", baseArrayLayer: 0, arrayLayerCount: keepLayers });
    const knockout = this.device.createTexture({ label: "liquiddom shadow knockout", size: [width, height], format: SHADOW_KNOCKOUT_FORMAT, usage });
    const knockoutView = knockout.createView();
    this.targets = {
      width, height, layers: keepLayers, density, densityLayerViews, densityArrayView, knockout, knockoutView,
      half: this.scaleTargets(SHADOW_SCALE_HALF, widthPx, heightPx, maxDim),
      quarter: this.scaleTargets(SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER, widthPx, heightPx, maxDim),
    };
  }

  private ensureGroups(particles: GPUBuffer, homes: GPUBuffer): void {
    const t = this.targets;
    const params = this.paramsBuffer;
    const records = this.recordsBuffer;
    if (!t || !params || !records) return;
    const slot = { buffer: params, size: SHADOW_PASS_FLOATS * 4 };
    if (!this.densityGroup || this.densityGroupFor?.particles !== particles || this.densityGroupFor.homes !== homes) {
      this.densityGroup = this.device.createBindGroup({
        label: "liquiddom shadow density",
        layout: this.pipelines.shadowDensityLayout,
        entries: [
          { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: this.viewBuffer } },
          { binding: 1, resource: slot },
          { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: particles } },
          { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: homes } },
        ],
      });
      this.densityGroupFor = { particles, homes };
    }
    this.resolveGroup ??= this.device.createBindGroup({
      label: "liquiddom shadow resolve",
      layout: this.pipelines.shadowResolveLayout,
      entries: [
        { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: this.viewBuffer } },
        { binding: 1, resource: { buffer: records } },
        { binding: 2, resource: t.densityArrayView },
        { binding: 3, resource: this.sampler },
      ],
    });
    this.knockoutGroup ??= this.device.createBindGroup({
      label: "liquiddom shadow knockout",
      layout: this.pipelines.shadowKnockoutLayout,
      entries: [
        { binding: 0, resource: slot },
        { binding: 1, resource: t.densityArrayView },
      ],
    });
    this.compositeGroup ??= this.device.createBindGroup({
      label: "liquiddom shadow composite",
      layout: this.pipelines.shadowCompositeLayout,
      entries: [
        { binding: 0, resource: { buffer: this.viewBuffer } },
        { binding: 1, resource: slot },
        { binding: 2, resource: t.half.accView },
        { binding: 3, resource: t.quarter.accView },
        { binding: 4, resource: t.knockoutView },
        { binding: 5, resource: this.sampler },
      ],
    });
  }

  /** One slot per source, two per blurred group, then the knockout and the composite. */
  private writeParams(plan: ShadowPlan, t: Targets): void {
    const P = this.params;
    let slot = 0;
    const at = (s: number): number => s * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS;
    this.sourceSlots.length = 0;
    this.groupSlots.length = 0;
    let close = 0;
    const masks = [0, 0, 0, 0].map(() => [0, 0, 0, 0]);
    let knock: Rect | null = null;
    for (const src of plan.sources) {
      const o = at(slot);
      P.fill(0, o, o + SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
      P[o + SP.TARGET_W] = t.width;
      P[o + SP.TARGET_H] = t.height;
      P[o + SP.ELEMENT] = src.id;
      P[o + SP.KERNEL_R] = src.radius;
      P[o + SP.WEIGHT] = src.weight;
      P[o + SP.MASK + src.channel] = 1;
      this.sourceSlots.push(slot++);
      if (src.spread === 0) {
        const row = masks[src.layer];
        if (row) row[src.channel] = 1;
        close = Math.max(close, src.radius);
        const g = { x0: src.rect.x0 - KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * src.radius, y0: src.rect.y0 - KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * src.radius, x1: src.rect.x1 + KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * src.radius, y1: src.rect.y1 + KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * src.radius };
        knock = knock === null ? g : { x0: Math.min(knock.x0, g.x0), y0: Math.min(knock.y0, g.y0), x1: Math.max(knock.x1, g.x1), y1: Math.max(knock.y1, g.y1) };
      }
    }
    for (const g of plan.groups) {
      this.groupSlots.push(slot);
      if (!(g.blur > 0)) continue;
      const st = g.scale === SHADOW_SCALE_QUARTER ? t.quarter : t.half;
      const sigma = (g.blur / 2) * this.dpr * st.scale;
      const taps = Math.min(SHADOW_MAX_TAPS, Math.ceil(3 * sigma));
      for (const [dx, dy] of [[1, 0], [0, 1]] as const) {
        const o = at(slot++);
        P.fill(0, o, o + SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
        P[o + SP.TARGET_W] = st.width;
        P[o + SP.TARGET_H] = st.height;
        P[o + SP.DIR_X] = dx;
        P[o + SP.DIR_Y] = dy;
        P[o + SP.SIGMA] = sigma;
        P[o + SP.TAPS] = taps;
      }
    }
    this.knockoutSlot = slot++;
    const ko = at(this.knockoutSlot);
    P.fill(0, ko, ko + SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
    P[ko + SP.TARGET_W] = t.width;
    P[ko + SP.TARGET_H] = t.height;
    this.closePx = Math.max(1, Math.round(KNOCKOUT_CLOSE_PER_KERNEL * close * this.dpr * SHADOW_SCALE_HALF));
    P[ko + SP.CLOSE] = this.closePx;
    P[ko + SP.LAYERS] = plan.densityLayers;
    for (let l = 0; l < masks.length; l++) for (let c = 0; c < SHADOW_CHANNELS; c++) P[ko + SP.MASKS + 4 * l + c] = masks[l]?.[c] ?? 0;
    this.knockoutRect = knock;
    this.compositeSlot = slot++;
    const co = at(this.compositeSlot);
    P.fill(0, co, co + SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(this.paramsBuffer!, 0, this.params, 0, slot * SHADOW_SLOT_FLOATS);
  }

  private writeRecords(plan: ShadowPlan): void {
    const R = this.records;
    const n = Math.min(plan.records.length, SHADOW_MAX_RECORDS);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const r = plan.records[i];
      const src = r ? plan.sources[r.source] : undefined;
      if (!r || !src) continue;
      const o = i * SHADOW_RECORD_FLOATS;
      R[o] = r.rect.x0;
      R[o + 1] = r.rect.y0;
      R[o + 2] = r.rect.x1;
      R[o + 3] = r.rect.y1;
      R[o + 4] = r.dx;
      R[o + 5] = r.dy;
      R[o + 6] = src.layer;
      R[o + 7] = src.channel;
      R[o + 8] = r.color[0];
      R[o + 9] = r.color[1];
      R[o + 10] = r.color[2];
      R[o + 11] = r.color[3];
    }
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(this.recordsBuffer!, 0, R, 0, Math.max(1, n) * SHADOW_RECORD_FLOATS);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `webgpu-renderer.ts`**. It adds:
  - the shadow pipelines and layouts, built at init in the liquid's scope (resolution 7);
  - `OVER_BLEND` on the composite (D88-13);
  - `ShadowPasses` created at init (no GPU object until the first shadow);
  - `prepare` after the uploads, `encode` after the splat, `drawComposite` first in the screen pass;
  - `shadowsDrawn`, the reset at the start of every `render()`, `resize()`, and the release.

+function buildShadowPipelines(device: GPUDevice, canvasFormat: GPUTextureFormat): { modules: GPUShaderModule[]; pipelines: ShadowPipelines } {
+  const V = GPUShaderStage.VERTEX;

+      { binding: 1, visibility: V | F, buffer: dynamicUniform },
+      { binding: 2, visibility: V, buffer: { type: "read-only-storage" } },
+      { binding: 3, visibility: V, buffer: { type: "read-only-storage" } },

+  const shadowResolveLayout = device.createBindGroupLayout({

+      { binding: 1, visibility: V | F, buffer: { type: "read-only-storage" } },
+      { binding: 2, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float", viewDimension: "2d-array" } },
+      { binding: 3, visibility: F, sampler: { type: "filtering" } },

+  const shadowBlurLayout = device.createBindGroupLayout({
+    label: "liquiddom shadow blur",
+    entries: [
+      { binding: 0, visibility: F, buffer: dynamicUniform },
+      { binding: 1, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float" } },
+    ],
+  });
+  const shadowKnockoutLayout = device.createBindGroupLayout({
+    label: "liquiddom shadow knockout",
+    entries: [
+      { binding: 0, visibility: F, buffer: dynamicUniform },
+      { binding: 1, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float", viewDimension: "2d-array" } },
+    ],
+  });
+  const shadowCompositeLayout = device.createBindGroupLayout({
+    label: "liquiddom shadow composite",
+    entries: [
+      { binding: 0, visibility: F, buffer: { type: "uniform" } },
+      { binding: 1, visibility: F, buffer: dynamicUniform },
+      { binding: 2, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float" } },
+      { binding: 3, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float" } },
+      { binding: 4, visibility: F, texture: { sampleType: "float" } },
+      { binding: 5, visibility: F, sampler: { type: "filtering" } },

+  const pipeline = (
+    label: string,
+    layout: GPUBindGroupLayout,
+    module: GPUShaderModule,
+    target: GPUColorTargetState,

+    device.createRenderPipeline({

+      vertex: { module, entryPoint: "vs" },
+      fragment: { module, entryPoint: "fs", targets: [target] },
+      primitive: { topology: "triangle-list" },

+      shadowBlurV: pipeline("liquiddom shadow blur v", shadowBlurLayout, blur, { format: SHADOW_ACC_FORMAT, blend: OVER_BLEND }),
+      shadowKnockout: pipeline("liquiddom shadow knockout", shadowKnockoutLayout, knockout, { format: SHADOW_KNOCKOUT_FORMAT }),
+      shadowComposite: pipeline("liquiddom shadow composite", shadowCompositeLayout, composite, { format: canvasFormat, blend: OVER_BLEND }),
+    },
   };
 }

- [ ] **Step 3: Docs** (D88-8):
  - `getting-started.md:44` becomes: "liquiddom hides the element's own background and border while it is observed and draws the liquid in their place. Its `box-shadow` stays: the browser draws it at rest, and while the liquid moves liquiddom draws its outer layers (up to four) from the liquid's shape; inset shadows show at rest only. The element's text, focus ring and events are untouched."
  - `limitations.md:14` becomes: "**Shapes.** Only a uniform, circular `border-radius` is honoured. Borders of observed elements are not drawn. Box-shadows are: up to four outer layers follow the liquid while it moves, inset layers show at rest only, and `filter: drop-shadow()` is not drawn. A box-shadow that changes on `:hover` without a transition needs `refresh(el)`, as colours do. While the liquid moves, a resting element's own shadow paints over liquid passing it, and an element with an author `transition: box-shadow` has any other transition running at that moment jump to its end when the liquid leaves or comes back to rest."
  - `packages/core/README.md:24` becomes: "Every `[data-liquid]` element is observed. Its computed `background-color` becomes the liquid colour, its own background and border are hidden, and its box-shadow follows the liquid while it moves."
  - `packages/core/README.md:120` becomes: "Only uniform circular `border-radius` is honoured. Borders of observed elements are not drawn; box-shadows are (up to four outer layers; inset at rest only; a `:hover` shadow change without a transition needs `refresh(el)`)."
  - `packages/core/README.md:119` becomes: "Colours and box-shadows are snapshotted at `observe()` and re-read automatically when the element's own `class`/`style` changes or a `color`, `background-color` or `box-shadow` transition on it ends. Call `refresh(el)` after a change elsewhere (a new stylesheet rule, an ancestor class, a `:hover` rule without a transition)."

    - a "Known" line:
      - holes wider than ≈ 3 kernel radii show the shadow on their rim (D88-10 amended);
      - WebGPU draws no renderer shadow for an element past 16 density sources per frame, and that element keeps its browser shadow static at its box (resolution 6);
      - a resting element's browser shadow paints over a neighbour's moving liquid (resolution 13);
      - an element with an author `transition: box-shadow` has its other running transitions jump to their end at a toggle (resolution 12);
      - a `:hover` shadow needs `refresh(el)`;
      - inset hides in motion;
      - more than 4 outer layers draw the first 4;
      - CSS layer order is kept only approximately across radii (resolution 15);
      - `unobserve` mid-motion, or reduced motion on a paused instance, can show both shadows until the next frame.
  - Test conventions: one sentence on `shadow*.test.ts`, `webgpu-shadows.test.ts`, the fakes' new fields and `e2e/shadows.ts`.
  - Run `npx vitest run packages/core/__tests__/wdd-docs.test.ts`. It guards the Architecture section's Fluid-engine note: keep its strings.

- [ ] **Step 5: CONTEXT.md first trims, then adds.**
  - `.wdd/CONTEXT.md` is 8153 B of the 8192 B `wdd validate` allows. First merge the two "Liquid text" known-limitation lines (W74 v1 and costs) into one pointer to CLAUDE.md.
  - Then add a W88 row to the decisions table ("Shadows follow the liquid: browser at rest, `liquid-shadow-away` + renderer while away (D88-1 … D88-14)") and one Known line (the knockout's hole size, the WebGPU source cap, a resting shadow over moving liquid).
  - Replace "What Comes Next" with Epic 16.
  - `wdd validate` passes.

- [ ] **Step 6: Changeset** `.changeset/w88-shadows.md`:
  ```markdown
  ---
  "liquiddom": minor
  ---

  Shadows follow the liquid. An observed element keeps its own CSS `box-shadow`: the browser draws it at rest, and while the element's liquid is away (a splash, a shake, a drag) liquiddom hides it with the new class `liquid-shadow-away` and draws up to four outer layers from the liquid's shape in both renderers, under all liquid, so a drop shadow sags with a splash and a glow wraps a merge. Inset shadows show at rest only. The shadow is re-read with the colours (the element's own class or style, `refresh(el)`) and when a `box-shadow` transition on it ends; an author `transition: box-shadow` does not fade the shadow when the liquid leaves or returns to rest. Previously liquiddom hid every observed element's box-shadow.
  ```

  - Run the Docker `--project=canvas2d` without `--update-snapshots`: no baseline moves.

  - Canvas2D: the p95 of `render()` CPU time.
  - WebGPU: the amortised GPU time of the shadow passes (encode them ×10 in the copy only and divide, as the spike did; `timestamp-query` where available).
  - Record the tables in `ward-088.md` next to the spike's.
  - **If Canvas2D exceeds +2 ms over the baseline at 6 elements, STOP and present before gold.** The spike measured +0.75 … +0.99 ms without the knockout.
  - **If WebGPU's amortised GPU shadow time at 6 elements × 3 layers, DPR 2, exceeds 3.76 ms, STOP and present before gold.** That is 2 × the spike's 1.88 ms (buckets, half resolution, scissored), and the real passes add the knockout and exact radii.

### Questions for Dennis

They are asked at the pre-red gate (W88.1 Step 0), in full in the chat, before any red test is written: Question 1 (the D88-10 amendment), Question 2 (the author transition, fixed by resolution 12), the WebGPU source cap, the resting-shadow limitation, the other extensions and the six approved-test changes.

## Plan validation

W88.1 was applied, as written above, to a plain copy of `6c8d0f5` in `$S/w88plan/repo` (`git archive`, `npm install`, `npm run build:wasm`, `npm run build`), with the e2e port moved to 4191 in the copy only. The copy is deleted.

- **Baseline in the copy:** `870` tests, 869 passed. The one failure is `e2e-harness … gitignore`, which needs a `.git`, and the copy has none.
- **Red:** `930` tests, 865 passed, 65 failed. The two copy artefacts are that `.git` test and the moved port's `e2e-harness … strictPort`. So in the repo: **867 passed, 63 failed**, matching every row's Red column (no import error; 7 TypeErrors and 2 spy errors as marked).
- `npm run build -w liquiddom` (tsc strict) passed with the stubs. `npm run e2e:typecheck` was clean. `--list`: canvas2d 109, webgpu-hw 102, the others unchanged.
- **e2e at red:** `shadows.spec.ts` 8 failed on assertions (E3 caught f120 at restAlpha 0.833 with the class off). The W88 block failed 3 of 3 on Metal.
- **The widened gate** passed with W88 set to `red` in the copy (40/40), so the ward's decision lines and NORTH-STAR rows already satisfy it.
- **With W88.2–W88.4 applied** (the code above):
  - tsc strict was clean, and vitest passed 930/930 (the two copy artefacts aside).
  - `shadows.spec.ts` passed 8/8: E1 ring energy at rest drop 360903 / glow 649294 / plain 0; E3 ratios 1.000–1.046 (Canvas2D); E8 alpha 135.
  - The W88 block passed 3/3 on Metal: E3w 0.981–1.028; E8w 148 after excluding WebGPU's white liquid text, which the first probe counted (alpha 193).
  - A mutation run with the knockout disabled made E8 fail (alpha 255), so E8 pins D88-10.
  - The full canvas2d project ran 94 passed / 15 skipped (the Linux-only baselines), 0 failed.
  - The full webgpu-hw project on Metal ran 58 passed / 44 skipped, 0 failed: the OVER composite regresses nothing.
  - The intermediate states measured: after W88.2, 890/930; after W88.3, 916/930.
- **Vision on the copy** (stills deleted):
  - Both renderers matched the browser's shadows at rest.
  - Mid-splash the drop shadow sagged under the deformed liquid, the cyan glow wrapped it, the spread ring followed, the inset hid, and the glass pill had no dark core.
  - Small holes were knocked out, and wide holes showed the shadow on their rim (Question 1).
- **Folded back from the validation:**
  - three more approved pins (resolution 9 (iv)–(vi));
  - the WebGPU knockout probe excludes liquid text;
  - `captureRenders(…, () => el)` in row 30;
  - the half-plane density check's lattice bound in row 51;
  - jsdom has no `matchMedia` (row 29 defines it);
  - jsdom returns box-shadow as specified (the parser is order-agnostic and reads a unitless 0);
  - the Canvas2D closing loop allocates nothing per cell.

**The first validation measured 930 tests; these figures are superseded by fix round 1.**

### Fix round 1 (re-validated)

The plan as committed in `880d2d7` was re-applied from its own text: every file block and `git apply` of every diff, on a fresh copy of `880d2d7` in `$S/w88plan2`. It reproduced 930/930. The fixes were then made in that copy, and the plan's code blocks were regenerated from it. The copy is deleted.
- **The author transition (I3)**, measured before the fix (Fade, `transition: box-shadow 0.3s`, `splash(0.5)`, frames ≈ 50 ms of wall time):
  - the browser's shadow faded out over ≈ 3 frames after the class went on, with the ring at 1.55 → 2.15 × rest (a double shadow);
  - after the handoff it faded in (0.00 → 0.16 → 0.64 → 0.98: a gap);
  - **no loop**: 2 `transitionend` events, one lift (2 style writes) and 6 class writes over the whole splash.

  With resolution 12, E9 passes: `none` in the first moving frame, the full author value at the handoff, and 0 `transitionend`. With the plain class write E9 fails (`Received: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) 0px 5.32416px …"`).
- **Red:** `933` tests, 865 passed, 68 failed in the copy (the same two copy artefacts), so **867 passed, 66 failed** in the repo. Rows 13a, 13b and 27a fail on assertions. Row 29 fails on an assertion, and so does row 25 (S, as before).
- **e2e at red:** `shadows.spec.ts` 9 failed; `--list` canvas2d 110, webgpu-hw 102.
- **After W88.2:** 893 / 933 (repo terms). **After W88.3:** 919 / 933. **After W88.4:** 933 / 933.
- `shadows.spec.ts` passed 9/9 on the final copy (E3 ratios 1.000–1.046, E8 alpha 135), and the W88 block passed 3/3 on Metal (E3w 0.982–1.027, E8w 148).
- `git apply --check` of the W88.1 diffs against HEAD passed, and `git apply -R --check` of the W88.2–W88.4 diffs against the final copy passed.
